# 1491 Krakow Octoechos of Schweipolt Fiol (Осмогласник / Октоїх Швайпольта Фіоля, Краків, 1491) <a id="1491-krakow-octoechos-of-schweipolt-fiol"></a>

[p1LA] [Binding: Contemporary bevelled wooden boards covered in dark-brown blind-tooled calfskin leather. The central panel is adorned with blind-stamped geometric and foliate borders. The binding is fitted with five brass fittings: four ornate chased brass corner-plates bearing raised hemispherical dome bosses, a central lozenge-shaped brass centrepiece boss, and remnants of two brass clasp catches on the fore-edge.][^1]

[p2LA] 1.

[Calligraphic monogram and paraph in ink at the head of the leaf:]

& A

[Sixteenth-century Latin presentation inscription in humanist cursive:]

Iohanne Hessu munere hoc

donavit, C. Adelshatzius[^2]

[Archival shelfmark in pencil:]

Ink. 80[^3]

[Oval library stamp in blue-green ink:]

STADT-BIBLIOTHEK BRESLAU[^4]

[Faint reverse offset strike-through impression of the full-page Crucifixion woodcut from the facing leaf (p3).]

[p3LA] [Full-page woodcut frontispiece within a double-line border: The Crucifixion of our Lord Jesus Christ. Christ is crucified upon the Cross at Golgotha, His head crowned with thorns inclined to the right, with blood streaming from His pierced side, hands, and feet upon the footstool. Above the Cross is a titulus tablet. To the left of the Cross stands the Most Holy Theotokos, swooning in grief and supported by the holy Myrrh-bearing women; to the right stand the holy Apostle and Evangelist John the Theologian with hand placed upon his breast, and the holy Centurion Longinus with halo. In the background rise the battlemented walls and towers of Jerusalem. The woodcut is illuminated with contemporary hand-coloring in green, cinnabar red, ochre, and brown pigments.][^5]

# TONE 1

## SATURDAY EVENING: VESPERS (TONE 1)

[p4LA] *Services I sing for the Octoechos, the work of our venerable father John of Damascus. On Saturday evening, at "Lord, I have cried", we bless... [Kathisma] and we sing Stichera: the Resurrection Stichera, three, repeating each one to make six, and the Anatolian Stichera; in the Menaion, Stichera are sung to make ten: Resurrection Stichera to six, and of the Menaion to four. But if there be a great saint, we sing Resurrection Stichera to four, and to the saint to six Stichera. Tone 1:–*

*Sticheron 1:* Accept our evening prayers, O holy Lord, and grant us remission of sins, for Thou alone art He Who revealed the Resurrection unto the world.

*Sticheron 2:* Encompass Zion, O ye people, and surround her, and give glory therein to Him Who is risen from the dead; for He is our God, Who delivered us from our iniquities.

*Sticheron 3:* Come, O ye people, let us hymn and bow down before Christ, glorifying His Resurrection from the dead; [p5LA] for He is our God, Who delivered the world from the deception of the enemy.

*Other Eastern Stichera:*

*Sticheron 4:* Rejoice, O ye heavens, and sound the trumpets, ye foundations of the earth; break forth into singing, ye mountains, in gladness; for behold, Emmanuel nailed our sins unto the Cross, and granted life, and put death to death, and raised Adam up, for He is the Lover of mankind.

*Sticheron 5:* Him Who was crucified in the flesh of His own will for our sake, Who suffered and was buried, and rose from the dead, let us hymn, saying: Establish the right belief[^6] of Thy Church, O Christ, and bring peace unto our life, for Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Sticheron 6:* Standing before Thy life-giving tomb, unworthy as we are, we offer a hymn of praise unto Thine ineffable compassion, O Christ our God; for Thou, O sinless One, didst accept crucifixion and death, that Thou mightest grant the Resurrection unto the world, for Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Sticheron 7:* The Word, Who was begotten of the Father without beginning and is co-eternal,[^7] Who came forth ineffably from the virginal womb, and willingly accepted crucifixion and death for our sake, and is risen in glory: let us hymn Him, saying: O Lord, the Giver of life, glory to Thee, the Savior of our souls!

*Repeat one [Sticheron].*

*Tone 1. Theotokion (Dogmatikon):* Let us hymn the Virgin Mary, the glory of the whole world, who sprang forth from mankind and gave birth unto the Master, the heavenly gate, the song of the bodiless hosts, and the adornment of the faithful; for she was shown forth as heaven and the temple of the Godhead; she, having destroyed the barrier of enmity, brought in peace and opened the Kingdom. Having her, therefore, as the anchor of faith, we have as champion the Lord Who was born of her. Be [p6LA] of good courage, O people of God, for He will conquer the enemies, for He is the Lover of mankind.

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Tone [Tone 1]:*

*Sticheron 1:* By Thy Passion, O Christ, we were set free from passions, and by Thy Resurrection we were delivered from corruption; O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Verse: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty.*

*Sticheron 2:* Thou didst willingly accept crucifixion, O Savior, and having endured burial, Thou didst rise from the dead as our God; as One abundant in mercy, Thou didst shatter the arrogant assaults of the adversary by Thy power, granting salvation unto those who glorify Thy third-day Resurrection.

*Verse: For He hath established the world, so that it cannot be moved.*

*Sticheron 3:* Being King, out of love for mankind Thou wast willingly crucified and buried. When Hades met Thee below, it was embittered; and when the souls of the righteous received Thee, they rejoiced. And Adam, beholding Thee, the Creator, in the nethermost regions, was set free from corruption. Oh, what a marvel! How did the Life of all taste of death? How was He enclosed in a tomb, save that He willed to illumine the world, as we cry aloud and say: O Thou Who art risen from the dead, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Glory... Both now...*

*Theotokion:* Behold, the prophecy of Isaiah is fulfilled; for a Virgin hast thou given birth, and after childbirth thou remainest as before childbirth; for God was born unto us, therefore nature was not corrupted. Therefore, O Mother of God, despise not the prayers of thy servants offered in thy church; but as thou bearest Thine own compassionate Son in thine arms, have compassion upon us, thy servants, and show mercy unto our souls!

*Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 1:*

While the stone was sealed by the Jews, and the soldiers guarded Thine all-pure Body, Thou didst rise on the third day, O Savior, granting life unto all the world. Therefore the heavenly powers cried aloud unto Thee, O Giver of life: Glory to Thy Resurrection, O Christ! Glory to Thy Kingdom! Glory to Thy dispensation, O only [p7LA] Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion: Glory... Both now...*

When Gabriel announced unto the Virgin: Rejoice!, with his voice the Master of all became incarnate in thee, the holy Ark, as the righteous David said; thou wast shown forth more spacious than the heavens, having borne thy Creator. Glory to Him Who dwelt within thee! Glory to Him Who came forth from thee! Glory to Him Who freed our race through thy childbearing!

## SUNDAY MIDNIGHT OFFICE (TONE 1)

*At Matins, Canon to the Holy and Life-Creating Trinity, Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos: Thy victorious [right hand]...*

*Troparion 1:* The Seraphim glorify unceasingly the unoriginate and co-eternal Principle in Three Persons,[^8] the unsearchable Godhead, which every tongue faithfully glorifies with hymns.

*Troparion 2:* Manifesting Thy singular and thrice-radiant Godhead, O God, Thou didst create man according to Thine image, and didst grant him understanding, giving him mind, word, and spirit, for Thou art the Lover of mankind.

*Troparion 3:* From on high Thou didst show forth the singular and divine dominion in three Hypostases, O Father, having made It equal in action unto Thy Son and Spirit; therefore in three Hypostases we hymn Thee.

*Theotokion:* The unoriginate Father, the co-equal Son, and the co-enthroned Holy Spirit, let us hymn in the manner of the Seraphim; let us glorify the One God in Trinity: Holy, Holy, Holy! Worshipped and hymned in unity, and worshipped in three Persons as One.

*Ode 3. Heirmos: Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human...*

*Troparion 1:* Of old Thou didst manifestly appear unto Abraham as three Hypostases in one [p8LA] hypostatic essence of the Godhead, prefiguring the truest theology, and the only-begotten Son is faithfully hymned in the Trinity of Father, Son, and Holy Spirit.

*Troparion 2:* With holy lips, the Seraphim glorify the unoriginate Father, the Son, and the Holy Spirit, right-believingly and perfectly; and for our sake as men, the Light of Light was united into one, the Savior without change following human nature.

*Troparion 3:* O Unity in Trinity transcendent in essence, beyond mind and understanding! Ineffably the spiritual and rational essences glorify Thee, with thrice-holy voices unceasingly offering praise, where the tri-hypostatic Hymn is fittingly sung.

*Theotokion:* He Who is beyond time came forth in time without change, and the timeless One was well-pleased in one essence and lordship; O beloved Savior, and the Spirit came forth together!

*Sessional Hymn of the Trinity, Tone 1:* O Trinity, Unity, God, beloved of all the world! In the appearance of a dove Thou didst well-pleasingly reveal the Father and Thyself and the undivided Spirit, in three Hypostases and in union, worshipped in right belief for the salvation of mankind.

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Behold my salvation, O Virgin Theotokos! In the day of His second coming, may He not reprove in anger my secret transgressions, nor set me with the portion of the goats; but may He have compassion on me through thy prayers, O all-immaculate Virgin!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Habakkuk discerning the mountain overshadowed by grace...*

*Troparion 1:* Shine upon me, O divine thrice-radiant Light, Thy gracious beam, and with the beauty of the eyes of the heart [p9LA] purify the mind, and fill me with that divine brightness which is beyond understanding, by Thy light-bestowing and radiant grace!

*Troparion 2:* Before establishing the heavens, O almighty Lord, Thy creative Word and the consubstantial Spirit were, by Whom Thou rulest through all ages, O singular Divine Dominion!

*Troparion 3:* The Children in the furnace prefigured the immortal Father, Son, and Holy Spirit, singing with a trumpet; and now, O Lord, confirm us to hymn Thee with heart and lips, together with the holy youths, worshipping the Three Hypostases.

*Theotokion:* Thou didst bear One of the Trinity, O all-pure One, God made man beyond understanding, Who illumined the earthly essence, the thrice-radiant Godhead, shining with unwaning light!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Illumine with the radiant dawn...*

*Troparion 1:* Before all, Thou didst establish the angels in the midst of creation; by the inaccessible rays of Thy beauty and by Thy many-lighted beams, illumine those who right-believingly hymn Thee, O Trinity of single dominion!

*Troparion 2:* The Father, Son, and Holy Spirit are one Divine Essence; O ye faithful, glorify the One God, divided and undivided, Creator of visible and invisible creation!

*Troparion 3:* The prophet Elijah prefigured the Trinity by the Spirit of old on the field; Thou, O Lord, by the will of the Father didst raise the first-formed man by Thy third-day Resurrection; and now, O Lord, strengthen us by the power of the Paraclete to hymn the consubstantial Trinity in one union!

*Theotokion:* The sayings of the holy prophets prefigured concerning thee, O all-pure One, thy divine childbearing, ineffable and unspeakable, whereby we [p10LA] have mystically learned to know the one all-creative Godhead in three Persons.

*Ode 6. Heirmos: The lowest abyss hath compassed us about, and...*

*Troparion 1:* Possessing equal dignity and power, O superessential Trinity, in the essence of Thy will, Thou art a simple and undivided Unity; do Thou, therefore, preserve us by Thy power!

*Troparion 2:* O unoriginate and co-enthroned Holy Trinity: Father, Son, and Spirit, from Whom all who dwell in the world have life, for all salvation is by Thy single power! Thee the Cherubim and Seraphim glorify, and with them we, unworthy as we are, offer praise unto Thee.

*Troparion 3:* A thrice-radiant Sun hath shone upon us, united in essence: the Father, Son, and Most Holy Spirit; distinct in Persons, yet undivided in essence: Father, Son, and Holy Spirit.

*Theotokion:* Thou wast the temple of the unwaning Sun, O all-pure One, Who created the great luminaries; having taken abode in thee, O all-pure Virgin and Bride of God, deliver us now from eternal torment!

*Sessional Hymn of the Trinity, Tone 1:* Falling down in faith, crying out from the heart: Have mercy upon us, Thy unworthy servants, O most good Trinity: Father, Son, and Holy Spirit! In Thy mercy have compassion upon Thine own flock which Thou hast acquired, that we may unceasingly glorify Thee and Thy goodness!

*Theotokion:* O Mother of God, who didst bear One of the Trinity, most fair paradise and salvation of the earth, protect with thy shelter those who blessedly hymn thee; for thou didst bring forth, as the prophets foretold, and thou didst carry Him Who upholds all things.

*Ode 7. Heirmos: Thee, O Mother of God, we faithful perceive...*

*Troparion 1:* The illumination of the Word and consubstantial brightness of God the Almighty [p11LA] Who didst promise graciously to make Thine abode, mercifully together with Thy Father and with the Holy Spirit, and hast shown [us] fearful unto demons.[^9]

*Troparion 2:* That Thou mightest show Thy tender mercy, O Master, Thou didst send Thy Savior into the abyss unto our lowliness, and didst re-fashion [us] unto the primal brightness; but now grant me understanding, O Divine Spirit!

*Troparion 3:* O most radiant Properties, O Father ineffably mighty in days, fill [us] with ineffable things; and, O Holy Spirit, make the man spiritual and perfect in the vision of nature, crying aloud unto the Light and Essence: O Father, Son, and God!

*Theotokion:* He Who is seated co-enthroned upon the Cherubim, the Upholder of all things, made His abode in thy womb, O all-pure One, appearing as God and the Lover of mankind; and by thy prayers preserve us who hymn thee!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: A marvelous wonder...*[^10]

*Troparion 1:* O Lord abundant in grace, all-tri-hypostatic Almighty Ruler, Who hast stretched out the heavens like a skin, and didst fashion the earth and the deep by Thy potent majesty: likewise strengthen us also in love and faith, O Lover of mankind, that we may hymn Thee unto the ages!

*Troparion 2:* Accept praise from corruptible beings, O most perfect Trinity, Who dost illumine and enlighten all spiritual essences by Thy goodness and power; for the unceasing powers glorify Thee, and we, earthly beings, cry aloud with untainted voices: May all creation bless Thee!

*Troparion 3:* Unto Abraham in his sojourning, He appeared in angels three-personed yet of single form, to be known as the one God in Trinity: Father, Son, and Holy Spirit; and He blessed his seed to endure unto the ages, and that man might return unto light and primal blessedness, [p12LA] let every creature bless and exalt Him above all!

*Theotokion:* He Who hath exalted us unto the heavens, Thine immutable Son, O Virgin Theotokos, assumed human nature through the multitude of His mercy, and delivered us from the ancient curse; unto Whom, giving thanks, let us cry aloud: Let all creation bless the Lord!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: The type of thy pure childbearing...*

*Troparion 1:* Save Thy creation, both visible and spiritual servants of Thine, from demonic assault and malice; teach [us] to honor the consubstantial Trinity, and preserve Thine own flock ever unharmed!

*Troparion 2:* Thou hast revealed the depth of Thine ineffable essence, and hast distributed grace unto us, O thrice-radiant Light and sole Sovereign, God Almighty, our Savior, wisely accomplishing our salvation!

*Troparion 3:* Look down upon our prayer, O Master, Who in tri-hypostatic subsistence art the one truly glorified God; and grant comfort unto Thy servants, through the prayers of the all-pure and all-praised Mother of God!

*Theotokion:* The envious enemy could not bear to look upon earthly nature, and the first-formed fell into corruption through transgression; but bowing the heavens, He came down without departing from the Godhead, and was incarnate of the Virgin, the Father's [Word], that He might deify earthly beings!

*Then: It is truly meet, and More honorable. Then the Trisagion. And after Our Father, these troparia, Tone 6:*

*Troparion:* Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us; for, lacking all defense, we sinners offer unto Thee as Master this supplication: Have mercy on us, O Lord, have mercy on us!

*Glory... Troparion:* O Lord, have mercy on us, for in Thee have we put our trust; be not exceedingly wroth with us, neither remember our iniquities; but [p13LA] look upon us even now as Thou art compassionate, and deliver us from our enemies; for Thou art our God, and we are Thy people, all the work of Thy hands, and we call upon Thy name.

*Both now... Theotokion:* Open unto us the door of loving-kindness, O blessed Theotokos and Virgin, that hoping in thee we may not perish, but may be delivered by thee from tribulations; for thou art the salvation of the Christian race!

*Then the priest:* Have mercy on us, O God, according to Thy great mercy, we pray Thee, O Lord, hear and have mercy.

*Priest:* Again we pray that He may preserve this holy monastery, and every city and countryside, from famine, pestilence, earthquake, flood, fire, sword, the invasion of aliens, and civil war; and from every mortal wound; and that our good and man-befriending God may be merciful, benign, and easily propitiated toward our sins, and may turn away and deliver us from all His righteous wrath that impends against us, and send down upon the earth beneficial rain by His grace, and have mercy upon us, let us all say:

> **Choir:** "Lord, have mercy *(40 times)*."

*Then the priest's exclamation:*

> **Priest:** "Hear us, O God our Savior, the hope of all the ends of the earth, and of those who are far off upon the sea; and be merciful, be merciful, O Master, toward our sins, and have mercy on us; for a merciful and man-befriending God art Thou, and unto Thee do we send up glory: to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen."

*Then: Glory to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, both now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen. Lord, have mercy; Lord, have mercy; bless.*

*Then the dismissal; and commemoration for the Orthodox sovereigns and people. And thereafter the reader begins Matins, and the priest says:*[^11]

[p14LA] **Priest:** Blessed is our God, always, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen.

*Then three prostrations. Then Psalm [19]: "The Lord hear thee in the day of affliction..." Another Psalm [20]: "O Lord, in Thy strength the king shall rejoice..."*

*Then the Trisagion; and after Our Father, the troparia:*

*Troparion, Tone 1:* O Lord, save Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance, granting victories to our Sovereign over adversaries, and by Thy Cross preserving Thy community.

*Glory... Troparion:* Thou Who of Thine own will wast lifted up upon the Cross, O Christ our God, bestow Thy compassions upon the new community that is called after Thy name; gladden our Sovereign by Thy power, granting him victories over enemies; may he have Thine aid in battle: a weapon of peace, an invincible trophy!

*Both now... Theotokion:* O champion and dread protection, who art not put to shame, despise not our supplications, O good and all-laudable Theotokos; confirm the estate of the Orthodox; save our Sovereign whom Thou hast appointed to rule, and grant him victory from heaven, for thou didst bring forth God, O only blessed one!

*Then the litany:*

> **Deacon:** "Have mercy on us, O God, according to Thy great mercy, we pray Thee, O Lord, hear and have mercy."

> **Deacon:** "Again we pray for our Grand Prince, [Name]; we pray Thee, O Lord, hear and have mercy."[^12]

> **Deacon:** "Again we pray for the Archbishop [Name], and Bishop [Name], for their health and salvation."

> **Deacon:** "Again we pray for those who serve and have served in this holy monastery, and for all our brethren in Christ."

> **Deacon:** "Again we pray for all Christian people."

> **Priest:** "For a merciful and man-befriending God art Thou, and unto Thee do we send up glory: to the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen."

> **Priest:** "Glory to the holy, consubstantial, life-creating, and undivided Trinity, always, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen."

*Then: Glory to God in the highest, and on earth peace, good will among men (thrice).*

*O Lord, Thou shalt open my lips, and my mouth shall declare Thy praise (twice).*

*Then the Six Psalms. Then the litany of peace. And "God is the Lord" in the Tone of the Octoechos.*

*Resurrection Troparion, Tone 1:* While the stone was sealed by the Jews, and the soldiers were guarding Thine immaculate body, Thou didst rise on the third day, O Savior, granting life unto the world. Therefore, the powers of heaven cried aloud unto Thee, O Giver of life: Glory to Thy Resurrection, O Christ! Glory to Thy kingdom! Glory to Thy dispensation, O only Lover of mankind! *(Twice)*

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* As Gabriel announced to thee, O Virgin, Hail! with the voice was the Master of all incarnate in thee, the holy ark, as the righteous David said; thou hast been shown more spacious than the heavens, having borne Thy Creator. Glory to Him Who took abode in thee! Glory to Him Who came forth from thee! Glory to Him Who hath delivered us by thy childbearing!

*After the 1st Stichologia, Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:* While the soldiers were keeping watch at Thy tomb, O Savior, they became as dead through the lightning-appearance of the angel who shone forth, proclaiming the Resurrection unto the women. We glorify Thee, the Destroyer of corruption; and we fall down before Thee, Who didst rise from the tomb, our only God! *(Twice)*

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Isaiah foretold the birth from the Virgin, and Daniel called thee a mountain from which the stone was cut without hands; and David [called] thee the holy ark, and Ezekiel the sealed door; therefore we also cry aloud unto thee with the angel: Rejoice, O Theotokos, more spacious than the heavens!

[p15LA] Rejoice, O temple! Rejoice, thou who didst bring forth the Light! Rejoice, O undefiled Virgin!

*After the 2nd Stichologia, Sessional Hymn, Tone 1:* The women came early unto Thy tomb, and seeing the appearance of the angel, they trembled; the tomb shone forth with life, and the miracle astonished them. Therefore, departing, they proclaimed the Resurrection unto the disciples: Christ hath despoiled hades, as He alone is mighty and strong, and hath raised up those fallen into corruption, dissolving the condemnation of fear by the Cross!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Knowing thee to be the Mother of God and a Virgin in truth, we, who with love flee unto thy goodness, cry aloud unto thy compassions; for we sinners possess thee as our protectress, and in temptations have acquired thee, the only undefiled one, as our salvation!

*Then: "Blessed are the undefiled in the way" [Psalm 118 / Kathisma 17]. And after the undefiled, the Resurrectional Troparia: "The assembly of angels was amazed..."*

*Then the Ypakoë, Tone 1:* The repentance of the thief plundered paradise, and the lamentation of the myrrh-bearing women transformed into joy; for they were commanded to announce the joy unto the Apostles: that Christ God is risen, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Anabathmoi, Tone 1. Antiphon 1:*

*Troparion 1:* When I am afflicted, hear my pangs; O Lord, unto Thee do I cry!

*Troparion 2:* Unto those who dwell in the wilderness, there is unceasing divine yearning, as they live outside of the worldly vanity.

*Glory... Both now... To the Holy Spirit:* Unto the Holy Spirit belong honor and glory, as is meet unto the Father and likewise unto the Son; therefore let us sing unto the consubstantial Trinity!

*Antiphon 2:*

*Troparion 1:* Having elevated me unto Thy mountains, O God, illumine me with the laws of virtues, that I may hymn Thee!

*Troparion 2:* Having taken hold of me by Thy right hand, O Word, preserve [p16LA] and protect me, lest the fire of sin consume me!

*Glory... Both now... To the Holy Spirit:* By the Holy Spirit every creature is renewed, returning unto its primal state, for He is equal in power unto the Father and the Word.

*Antiphon 3:*

*Troparion 1:* Unto those who said unto me: "Let us go into the house of the Lord," my spirit rejoiced, and my heart was glad!

*Troparion 2:* In the house of David great fear is there, for thrones are set there to judge all the tribes of the earth and the nations.

*Glory... Both now... To the Holy Spirit:* Honor, worship, glory, and dominion are meet to be offered unto the Father and likewise unto the Son, so also unto the Holy Spirit; for the Trinity is a Unity, one in essence, though distinct in Persons!

*They sing the Resurrectional Prokeimenon: Now will I arise, says the Lord... (or: The words of the Lord are pure words...). Let every breath praise the Lord. The Resurrectional Gospel of the cycle. And then: Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ... and Psalm 50: Have mercy on me, O God... Then: Glory... Through the prayers of the Apostles... Both now... Through the prayers of the Theotokos... Then: Have mercy on me, O God... and the sticheron incipit: Jesus, having risen from the tomb, as He foretold, hath given us life eternal and great mercy. Then the prayer:*

> **Priest:** "O God, save Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance; visit Thy world with mercy and compassions; exalt the horn of Orthodox Christians, and send down upon us Thy rich mercies; and strengthen the realm of our Sovereign, [Name]; through the prayers of our most pure Lady, the Theotokos and Ever-Virgin Mary; by the power of the precious and life-creating Cross; through the protection of the honorable bodiless powers of heaven; of the honorable, glorious prophet, Forerunner and Baptist John; [p17LA] of the holy, glorious, and all-laudable Apostles; of Saint [Name], whose temple it is; of our venerable and God-bearing fathers; of the holy and righteous Ancestors of God, Joachim and Anna; and of all Thy saints: we beseech Thee, O greatly-merciful Lord, hear us sinners praying unto Thee, and have mercy on us; and let us all say:"

> **Choir:** "Lord, have mercy *(12 times)*."

*Then the priest's exclamation:*

> **Priest:** "By the mercy and compassions and love for mankind of Thine only-begotten Son, with Whom Thou art blessed, together with Thine all-holy, good, and life-creating Spirit, now and ever, and unto ages of ages. Amen."

*The Resurrectional Canon, Tone 1. Ode 1. Heirmos: Thy triumphant right hand hath been glorified in power in a manner befitting God; for it, being immortal and almighty, hath shattered adversaries, renewing for the Israelites a path through the deep; for He is glorified!*[^13][^14]

*Troparion 1:* Having from the beginning fashioned me from clay in a manner befitting God with Thine immaculate hands, Thou didst stretch out Thy hands upon the Cross, recalling from the earth my corruptible body which Thou hadst taken from the Virgin; for He is glorified!

*Troparion 2:* He accepted slaying on my behalf, and surrendered His soul unto death, Who in the beginning breathed a divine soul into me; and He loosed eternal bonds, and co-raised [me], and glorified me in incorruption!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, O fountain of grace! Rejoice, O ladder and heavenly gate! Rejoice, O lampstand and golden urn, and mountain uncut, who didst bear Christ, the Giver of life, unto the world!

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos: Christ is born, give ye glory...*

*Troparion 1:* Christ is made visible, [p18LA] being incarnate; Christ is exalted, humbling Himself; Christ makes me passionless, suffering in the flesh as the Giver of life according to nature; therefore let us sing a hymn of praise, for He is glorified!

*Troparion 2:* Christ is lifted up, being crucified; Christ hath resurrected the dead [humanity]; Christ bestows life upon us; therefore, clapping our hands with great joy, let us sing a song of victory, glorifying God!

*Theotokion:* In thy virginity thou didst conceive; in virginity thou didst bear Christ; incarnate from thee, O all-pure One, one in Hypostasis, yet known in two natures as our Savior; for He is glorified!

*Another Canon, to the Theotokos, Tone 1. Heirmos: Having escaped from bondage...*

*Troparion 1:* Unto the young Child, our weakness brings a fitting hymn: Hail, O thou full of grace, as Gabriel mystically taught us: Rejoice, O Theotokos and Virgin, unwedded Mother!

*Troparion 2:* O blossomed rod of the highest powers, O all-pure One, we the faithful spiritually cry out from our hearts: Rejoice, O Theotokos and Virgin, unwedded Mother!

*Troparion 3:* Inexperienced in wedlock is the depth of thine incomprehensible childbearing, O all-honorable One; we the faithful fall down before thee with an unhesitating mind, purely crying aloud: Rejoice, O Theotokos and Virgin, unwedded Mother!

*Katavasia: I shall open my mouth...*

*Ode 3. Heirmos: O Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and didst mercifully take form therein: gird me with power from on high, that I may cry aloud unto Thee, O animate and holy Church: Holy art Thou, the animate temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind!*

*Troparion 1:* Being good unto us, Thou didst have compassion on the fallen, [p19LA] and wast well-pleased to come down unto me; and by Thy Crucifixion Thou didst raise me up, to cry aloud unto Thee: Holy art Thou, the God of glory, ineffable in goodness!

*Troparion 2:* Being the hypostatic Life, O Christ, and having clothed Thyself with me who had corrupted, as the merciful God, Thou didst descend into the dust of death, O Master, and didst destroy the dominion of death; and having risen as dead on the third day, Thou didst clothe me in incorruption!

*Theotokion:* Having conceived God in thy womb, O Virgin, by the all-holy Spirit, thou didst remain unconsumed; for as the bush unburnt yet burning was revealed of old unto Moses the lawgiver, it clearly prefigured thee who received the unendurable Fire!

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos: To Him Who before the ages was born of the Father...*

*Troparion 1:* Unto Him Who took upon His shoulders the slain sheep, and destroyed its transgression by the Tree, unto Christ God let us cry aloud: Thou Who hast exalted our horn, holy art Thou, O Lord!

*Troparion 2:* Unto Him Who declared Christ the great Shepherd from hades, and by the true preaching of the Apostles pastured the nations: in truth, let us the faithful serve with the Spirit!

*Theotokion:* Unto Christ God, Who of His own will was incarnate of the Virgin without seed, and Who preserved her after childbearing by divine power, let us worthily cry aloud unto Christ God: Thou Who hast exalted our horn, holy art Thou, O Lord!

*Another Canon, to the Theotokos. Heirmos: The same.*

*Troparion 1:* A light cloud do we truly name thee, O Virgin, instructed of old by prophetic words; for the Lord came upon thee to destroy the man-made illusions of Egypt, and to enlighten those who served them not!

*Troparion 2:* Truly the choir of prophets named thee the sealed fountain [p20LA] and the closed door, depicting thy virginity from afar in types, O all-praised one, which was preserved after childbearing!

*Troparion 3:* After childbearing, O undefiled one, thou didst remain as before childbearing, for thou didst bear God; by the Tree He saved man, O all-pure Lady, protectress of our race!

*Katavasia: O Mother of God, living and bountiful fountain, establish those who hymn thee...*

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Perceiving thee with prophetic eyes as a mountain overshadowed by divine grace, Habakkuk foretold that the Holy One of Israel should come forth from thee, unto our salvation and renewal!*

*Troparion 1:* Who is this stranger coming from Edom, wearing a crown of thorns, clad in garments dyed red, hanging upon the Tree? He is the Holy One of Israel, unto our salvation and renewal![^15]

*Troparion 2:* Behold, ye disobedient people, and be ashamed! He Whom ye lifted up upon the Cross as an evildoer, having asked Him of Pilate by a wicked verdict, hath destroyed the power of death, and hath risen from the tomb in a manner befitting God!

*Theotokion:* We know thee, O Virgin, as the Tree of Life; for not as an inedible fruit bringing death unto mankind, but from thee sprouted forth Life and enjoyment of eternal sweetness, unto our salvation and renewal!

*Another Canon, of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos: The same.*

*Troparion 1:* Who is this beautiful One coming from Edom, His garments reddened from the vineyard of Bozrah? Beautiful as God, yet as an earthly man wearing a bodily vesture stained with blood; unto Whom we sing: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion 2:* Christ being truly good, by His manifestation hath destroyed the sins that had power over us; He hath renewed [p21LA] into a marvelous path by His own Blood, appearing in perfection where the shadow had departed, having become a forerunner for us into the holy places.[^16]

*Theotokion:* The ancient unpaid debt, O all-hymned One, for the sake of the wounded, the New Adam, coming from thee before conception, an animate temple of flesh; and from thee came forth Christ, being One, twofold in nature, and restoring shattered men.

*Another canon, to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same [Thou art the mountain overshadowed]:*

*Troparion:* Hear marvelously, and give ear, O earth: for the daughter of fallen, transgressing Adam becomes again the Mother of her own Creator, for our salvation and recall!

*Troparion:* We hymn the great and fearful mystery of thine: the transcendent Originator of all things secretly descended upon thee like rain upon the fleece, O all-praised One, for our salvation and renewal!

*Troparion:* Tear away the savage soul-corrupting [enemy] who seeks to destroy me by deceit; for thou art, O all-pure One, the helper of Christians and the joy of the sorrowing, for our salvation and renewal!

*Katavasia: He Who sits in glory...*[^17]

*Ode 5. Heirmos: By the illumination of Thy coming, O Christ, and the enlightenment of Thy Cross which hath shone upon the ends [of the earth], enlighten the hearts of those who praise Thee in true faith with the light of Thy divine knowledge!*

*Troparion:* The Jews put Thee to death in the flesh upon the Tree, as the Great Shepherd of the sheep; but Thou, like sheep, didst deliver from the dominion of death the dead who had been laid in Hades.

*Troparion:* Bearing peace by Thy Cross, O Announcer of good tidings, Thou didst preach unto the captives [p22LA] saving remission; and Thou didst put to shame the one who held them fast, O Christ, showing him naked and destitute by Thy divine Resurrection.

*Theotokion:* Despise not the petitions of those who ask in faith, O all-praised One, but accept them, and offer these unto Thy God, O all-pure One, unto God the only Benefactor; for we have acquired thee as our mediatress!

*Another canon, of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos: Being God of peace, Father of mercies...*[^18]

*Troparion:* O the depth of the riches and of the wisdom of God! The Lord, the wise Creator, hath delivered us from the craft of the enemy: having suffered willingly in the infirmity of the flesh, by His own might, as the Giver of life, He raised up the dead!

*Troparion:* Being God, He united Himself with flesh for our sake, was crucified and put to death, and was buried; and forthwith Christ arises and gloriously ascends with His flesh unto the Father, with which [flesh] He shall come again, and shall save those who serve Him devoutly!

*Theotokion:* As the Holy of holies, O pure Virgin, thou didst holily give birth unto the Holy One Who sanctifies all things, Christ the Redeemer; therefore we devoutly proclaim thee as Queen and Mistress of all, as Mother of the Creator of creation!

*Another canon, to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same:*

*Troparion:* The heavenly powers made merry beholding thee, and with them the races of mankind rejoice in the resurrection; for they were united together through thy childbearing, O Virgin Theotokos, whom we worthily glorify!

*Troparion:* Every hymn glorifies thee, and the praises of the wise are offered unto the Virgin Mother of God; for she hath become the temple of glory!

*Troparion:* Let every human tongue be moved unto true adornment, glorifying holily the Virgin who is manifested, chanting her wonders with faith!

[p23LA] *Ode 6. Heirmos: The lowest abyss hath compassed us, and there is none to deliver; we are accounted as sheep for the slaughter. Save Thy people, O our God, for Thou art the strength and the restoration of the infirm!*[^19]

*Troparion:* Through transgression the first-created man was grievously wounded, but by Thy wound are we healed, wherewith Thou wast wounded for our sake, O Christ; for Thou art the strength and the restoration of the infirm!

*Troparion:* Thou hast brought [us] up out of Hades, O Lord, and hast made empty the all-devouring monster, O Almighty One, by Thy sovereign might, having shattered his power; for Thou art our Life and Resurrection!

*Theotokion:* The forefathers of our race rejoice in thee, O pure Virgin, having regained through thee Eden, which they had lost through transgression; for thou, O pure One, art [Virgin] before childbearing and after childbearing!

*Another canon, of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos: The womb of Jonah...*[^20]

*Troparion:* Being an immaterial and impassible Mind, Christ united Himself to the human mind, mediating unto life the nature of the flesh, and was immutably united unto all things, that being crucified He might grant salvation unto all our world!

*Troparion:* Deceived Adam fell and was crushed, having imagined in ancient times the hope of deification; but he is raised up by the union of the Word, and becomes divine: through His Passion having received impassibility, and being glorified upon the throne as son, seated together with the Father and the Spirit!

*Theotokion:* Without leaving the bosom of His unoriginate Parent, He makes His abode in the bosom of the pure Virgin; and He becomes without mother [in Heaven] and is incarnate without father [on earth], Christ God reigning in righteousness: for incomprehensible is this, and fearful His ineffable birth!

[p24LA] *Another canon, to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same:*

*Troparion:* The heavenly ranks of Thrones in slavish trembling hymn thy childbearing, marvelling worthily at thy seedless childbearing, O Ever-Virgin; for thou, O pure One, art [Virgin] before childbearing and after childbearing!

*Troparion:* He Who was before heavenly and bodiless, the Word, became incarnate of thee, O all-pure One, He Who created all things by His will, and Who brought the bodiless hosts out of non-existence, as He is almighty!

*Troparion:* Guide us into the path of repentance by thy prayers, O all-pure Lady, conquering the enemies by thy supplications!

*Katavasia: Celebrating this divine and all-honorable feast... And the Kontakion of the day, or to the Theotokos: O Protection of Christians that cannot be put to shame...*[^21]

*Ode 7. Heirmos: We, the faithful, perceive thee to be a spiritual furnace, O Theotokos; for as the Supremely Exalted One saved the three youths, so hath He wholly renewed the world in thy womb, the praised God of our fathers, and blessed art Thou!*

*Troparion:* The earth was dismayed and the sun hid itself; the light was restrained, the divine veil of the temple was rent, and the rocks were split; for the Righteous One hung upon the Cross, the praised God of our fathers!

*Troparion:* Thou becamest as one helpless, and wounded among the dead, willingly for our sake, O Supremely Exalted One; yet, setting all free by Thy sovereign hand, Thou didst raise them up with Thyself, the praised God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, fount of grace of living water! Rejoice, paradise of delight! Rejoice, wall of the faithful! Rejoice, unwedded one! Rejoice, glory of the universe, from whom shone forth the praised God of our fathers!

*Another canon, of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos: The youths reared in piety...*

[p25LA] *Troparion:* Of old the earth was cursed, crimsoned by the blood of Abel from a brother-slaying hand; but now, sprinkled with the God-flowing Blood of God, it leaps and chants: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:* Let the Jewish people weep, having wrought the God-opposing deed of the slaying of Christ; but let the nations rejoice, clapping their hands and crying aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:* Manifesting His two-fold birth clearly, for supernatural is Christ's, Who wrought divine things with mighty operation, it belongs unto the one Christ the Giver of life unto all ages!

*Another canon, to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same:*

*Troparion:* Rejoice, O pure One! For clothed in Adam's skin, out of thee in truth came forth the Shepherd, the Supremely Exalted One in humanity, by reason of His incomprehensible mercy: praised is the God of our fathers, and blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:* Rejoice, fount of ever-living water! Rejoice, paradise of delight! Rejoice, wall of the faithful! Rejoice, unwedded one! Rejoice, joy of the universe, for whose sake hath shone forth upon us the praised God of our fathers!

*Troparion:* [We hymn thee as] golden jar, all-pure table of the divine Bread of life, candlestick of light, O pure one, place of sanctification, high throne whereupon hath rested the supremely praised God of our fathers!

*Katavasia: The divinely-wise youths worshipped not creation...*[^22]

*Ode 8. Heirmos: In the furnace the children of Israel, as in a crucible, shone with the beauty of piety purer than gold, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord; hymn and exalt Him above all forever!*

*Troparion:* Creating and transforming all things by Thy will, turning the shadow of death [p26LA] into eternal life by Thy Passion in fitting majesty: bless Thee unceasingly all works as Lord, hymn and exalt Him above all forever!

*Troparion:* Thou didst shatter destruction, O Christ, and the misery in the gates and strongholds of Hades, having risen from the tomb on the third day; Thee unceasingly all works hymn as Lord and exalt Him above all forever!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, throne of God and wall of the faithful! Rejoice, through whom Christ, the Light in darkness, hath shone forth! Therefore, blessing thee, we cry aloud: Bless, all ye works of the Lord, the Lord!

*Another canon, of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos: A wonder most great...*[^23]

*Troparion:* Standing around the acceptable and adored place whereon the all-pure feet of Christ stood, and the Tree whereon the divine hands of the Giver of life were stretched out for the salvation of earthly beings, and the life-bearing tomb, we chant: Let all creation bless the Lord!

*Troparion:* He refuted the transgressing slander of the deicide Jews, for they had called Him a deceiver; He arose as mighty, unharmed though sealed by earthly men; therefore, rejoicing we sing: Let all creation bless the Lord, hymn and exalt Him above all forever!

*Theotokion:* The rich Word, glorified by angelic doxology, having become poor for our sake, chose thee for maternity, the beauty of Jacob, O blessed and pure one; therefore, chanting we cry aloud: Let all creation bless the Lord and exalt Him above all forever!

*Another canon, to the Theotokos. Heirmos the same:*

*Troparion:* As light-radiant bridal chamber, from which into light Christ the Master came forth as a bridegroom, we hymn thee, crying aloud unceasingly: Bless the Virgin, all ye works of the Lord, and exalt her above all forever!

*Troparion:* Rejoice, glorious throne of God! Rejoice, wall of the faithful, for whose sake Christ, the Light of all churches, hath shone forth in darkness; and those who bless thee cry aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works, [p27LA] and having given birth without seed to the flesh of human nature from the divine flash of lightning, to the most precious Pearl, Christ: sing aloud, saying: Bless the Lord, all creation, hymn and exalt Him above all forever!

*Katavasia: The pious youths in the furnace...*[^24]

*Ode 9. Heirmos: The burning bush showed forth unconsumed the image of thy pure childbearing; and now we pray thee to quench the furnace of temptations raging fiercely against us, that we may unceasingly magnify thee, O Theotokos!*

*Troparion:* O how the lawless and disobedient people, taking evil counsel, acquitted the unrighteous and impious one, but nailed to the Cross the Righteous One, the Lord of glory, Whom worthily we magnify!

*Troparion:* O Savior, spotless Lamb, Who takest away the sins of the world, we glorify Thee Who didst rise on the third day, together with the Father and the Divine Spirit, being the Lord of glory, Whom worthily we magnify!

*Theotokion:* Thou hast budded forth, O Virgin, from the root of David, the prophet and forefather of God; and truly hast thou glorified David, since thou hast given birth unto the foretold Lord of glory, Whom worthily we magnify!

*Another canon, of the Cross and Resurrection. Heirmos: A strange and most glorious mystery...*[^25]

*Troparion:* Thy Cross is glorified by Thine ineffable power, O Lord; for that which appeared weak in Thee proved mightier than all things: whereby the mighty were cast down, and the lowly upon earth are exalted unto Heaven!

*Troparion:* The death of Hades is slain, and Thou hast manifested resurrection from the dead; unto the ages, O Christ, Thou hast bestowed life: therefore, chanting in the three Hypostases, we magnify Thee, our Life and Resurrection!

*Triadikon:* The unoriginate and undivided Essence, glorified in three Hypostases, [p28LA] with one divine principle, one Godhead: Father, Son, and Holy Spirit; placing divine hope in Whom, [the faithful] are saved!

*Another canon, to the Theotokos. Heirmos: Ineffable mystery...*[^26]

*Troparion:* Every rule of praise, O all-pure One, is surpassed by the majesty of thy glory; yet, O Sovereign Lady, accept with goodness this zeal offered unto thee by thine unworthy servants who pray!

*Troparion:* Save Thy people, O Lord, whom Thou didst acquire by Thy precious Blood; grant strength unto Thy Church against enemies, and unto Thy Churches, O Lover of mankind, bestowing peace through the prayers of the Theotokos!

*Troparion:* Beyond comprehension are thy wonders, for thou alone under the sun, O Virgin, hast given all to perceive that which is transcendent, O all-pure One, in thy mysterious childbearing; therefore, we all magnify thee!

*Katavasia: Let every earthborn mortal leap for joy...*

*After this: the litany. And again: Holy is the Lord our God (thrice). After this, the Exapostilarion.*

*At the Praises: Sunday stichera, we chant on 8, Tone 1:*[^27]

*Sticheron 1, Tone 1:* We hymn Thy saving Passion, O Christ, and we glorify Thy Resurrection!

*Sticheron 2:* Having endured crucifixion and abolished death, and risen from the dead, grant peace unto our life, O Lord, as Thou alone art almighty!

*Sticheron 3:* Thou Who didst overthrow Hades and raise up man by Thy Resurrection, O Christ, vouchsafe us with pure heart to hymn and glorify Thee!

*Sticheron 4:* Glorifying Thy divinely-fitting condescension, we cry aloud unto Thee, O Christ: born of the Virgin and inseparable from the Father, Thou didst suffer as man and willingly endure crucifixion; Thou didst arise from the tomb, coming forth as from a bridal chamber, that Thou mightest save the world. O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Eastern Stichera:* When Thou wast nailed upon the tree of the Cross, then was the power of the enemy slain:

[p29LA] creation was shaken by the fear of Thee, and Hades was overthrown by Thy sovereign might; the dead Thou didst raise from the graves, and unto the thief Thou didst open paradise, O Christ our God, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron 6:* Weeping with haste, the honorable women came unto Thy tomb, and finding the tomb opened, and having learned from the angel, they proclaimed the most glorious wonder unto the apostles: that the Lord is risen, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Verse: Arise, O Lord, help us, and deliver us for Thy name's sake.*

*Sticheron 7:* O strife-loving Jewish race, give ear! Where are they that went unto Pilate? Let the guarding soldiers speak: Where are the seals of the tomb? Whither was the Buried One transferred? Whither was the Invaluable One sold? How was the treasure stolen? Why do ye slander the Resurrection of the Savior, O lawless Jews? He that is Free among the dead is risen, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*

*Sticheron 8:* We venerate Thy Passion-bearing wounds, O Christ God, and the sovereign ministry in Zion that was manifested by God at the end of the ages; for Thou, the Sun of righteousness, hast enlightened those sitting in darkness, guiding us unto the unwaning Light. O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Then: Glory... the appointed Evangelical Sticheron. Both now...*

*Theotokion: Most blessed art thou, O Virgin Theotokos... And the Great Doxology. Dismissal.*

*On Sunday, at the Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 1:*[^28]

*Troparion 1:* Through food the enemy led Adam out of Paradise, but by the Cross Christ brought the thief into it, [who cried]: Remember me when Thou comest into Thy kingdom! Crucified with sinners, Thou wast laid in the tomb of Thine own will; but having risen as God, Thou didst raise up the first-fashioned man with Thyself, [p30LA] [who cried]: Remember me when Thou comest into Thy kingdom!

*Troparion 2:* I venerate Thy Passion, O Christ, and we praise Thy Resurrection; with Adam and with the thief, with a radiant voice we cry aloud, calling: Remember me, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy kingdom!

*Troparion 3:* Thy apostles, O Lord, came with haste unto the mountain where Thou hadst commanded them, and seeing Thee they worshipped; whom Thou didst send forth unto the nations to preach Thy power.

*Troparion 4:* Thy bodily temple, by Thy three-day burial and Resurrection, together with Adam and those from Hades, Thou didst raise up, O Christ God, who cry: Remember me when Thou comest into Thy kingdom!

*Troparion 5:* The myrrh-bearing women came weeping unto Thy tomb, O Christ God, very early in the morning; and seeing an angel in white garments sitting, he said unto them: Why seek ye the Living One? Christ is risen, weep not! and so forth.

*Glory... Triadikon:* Let us worship the Father, and let us praise the Son, and let us, the faithful, hymn the All-Holy Spirit, crying: Remember me, O consubstantial Trinity, Unity, our God!

*Both now... Theotokion:* Thy people bring Thy Mother in prayer, O Christ; through her intercession grant us Thy compassions, that we may glorify Him Who hath shone forth unto us from the tomb!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 1: Let Thy mercy, O Lord, be upon us, according as we have hoped in Thee.* [Psalm 32:22 LXX]

*Verse: All the ends of the earth have seen the salvation of our God.* [Psalm 97:3 LXX]* [^29]

*Epistle to the Hebrews:*[^30]

*Reader:* Brethren: All the saints through faith subdued kingdoms, wrought righteousness, obtained promises, stopped the mouths of lions, quenched the violence of fire, escaped the edge of the sword, out of weakness were made strong, waxed valiant in fight, turned to flight the armies of the aliens... [Hebrews 11:33-34] [p31LA] Women received their dead raised to life again; and others were tortured, not accepting deliverance, that they might obtain a better resurrection. And others had trial of mockings and scourgings, yea, moreover of bonds and imprisonment. They were stoned, they were sawn asunder, were tempted, were slain with the sword; they wandered about in sheepskins and goatskins, being destitute, afflicted, tormented—of whom the world was not worthy—wandering in deserts and in mountains, and in dens and in caves of the earth. And all these, having obtained a good testimony through faith, received not the promise, God having provided some better thing for us, that they without us should not be made perfect. Therefore, seeing we also are compassed about with so great a cloud of witnesses, laying aside every weight and the sin which doth so easily beset us, let us run with patience the race that is set before us, looking unto Jesus the Author and Finisher of faith... [Hebrews 11:35–12:2]

*Gospel according to Matthew:*[^31]

The Lord said unto His disciples: Every one who shall confess Me before men, him will I also confess before My Father Who is in the heavens. But whosoever shall deny Me before men, him will I also deny before My Father Who is in the heavens. He that loves father or mother more than Me is not worthy of Me; and he that loves son or daughter more than Me is not worthy of Me. And he that does not take his cross and follow after Me is not worthy of Me. Then answered Peter and said unto Him: Behold, we have forsaken all and followed Thee; what then shall we have? [p32LA] And Jesus said unto them: Truly, truly, I say unto you, that ye who have followed Me, in the regeneration when the Son of Man shall sit upon the throne of His glory, ye also shall sit upon twelve thrones, judging the twelve tribes of Israel. And every one that hath forsaken father, or mother, or brethren, or sisters, or wife, or children, or lands, or houses, for My name's sake, shall receive an hundredfold, and shall inherit everlasting life. But many that are first shall be last, and the last first.[^32] [Matthew 19:28–30]

## SUNDAY EVENING VESPERS (TONE 1)

*On Sunday evening, Penitential Stichera at "Lord, I have cried". Work of Lord Joseph, Tone 1.*

*Podoben: "O all-lauded martyrs" (прехвалнїи ц̃ї):* [^33]

*Sticheron 1:* O all-good Father, Thou didst send Thine own Son, the all-good God, to take upon His shoulder the fallen son, to restore the strayed sheep, and to lead him back whence he fell into Thine angelic fold, O unerring Son!

*Sticheron 2:* O all-good Father, Who by Thy great and long-suffering goodness didst bring into being as God that which was not; and at last, having fashioned man with Thine own hand according to Thine image, when he disobeyed the commandments given unto him, Thou didst recreate him, as the Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 3:* As the Lover of mankind, O Lord, being the true Son of Heaven and of the divine Parent, Thou wast manifested, having become Son of Man, that, taking upon Thine own shoulder man who was deceived by the guile of the serpent, like a sheep, Thou mightest lead him into the heavenly fold!

*Other stichera, to the Bodiless Powers:*[^34]

*Sticheron 1:* Incorporeal Angels, standing before the divine throne [p33LA] and radiant with the brightness thence, possessing eternal effusion of light, and becoming second lights: pray ye unto Christ to grant unto our souls peace and great mercy!

*Sticheron 2:* Incorporeal Angels, who have received in truth immortal life from the First Life, O all-wealthy ones, beholders of the supersubstantial and honorable glory: ye are ever shown full of light and turned toward the lamp by union, and ye dance; pray ye unto Christ to grant unto our souls peace and great mercy!

*Sticheron 3:* Archangels, Angels, Principalities, Thrones, Dominions, and six-winged Seraphim, and many-eyed Cherubim, divine vessels of wisdom, most divine Powers and Authorities: pray ye unto Christ to grant unto our souls peace and great mercy!

*Theotokion:* Having become the pure dwelling-place of God, O pure one, pray thou with the Incorporeal Hosts that I may purely finish the path of life; lest the evening of death overtake me unprepared, and cast me into the flame of Gehenna to be tormented; but do thou snatch me away therefrom!

*At the Aposticha, Tone 1:*[^35]

*Sticheron 1:* As a great abyss are my sins, O Christ Savior, and grievously am I submerged by mine iniquities; give me a helping hand as unto Peter, O God, and have mercy on me!

*Sticheron 2:* Because by evil thoughts and deeds I have condemned myself, O Savior: grant me the thought of return [p34LA] O God, that having turned [from evil], O good Benefactor, have mercy on me!

*Martyrikon:* By the prayers of all the holy martyrs, O Lord, grant us Thy peace, and have mercy on us, as the only compassionate One!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Thou art the joy of angelic ranks, and upon earth the mighty helper of men, O all-pure Virgin; save those who flee unto thee, for in thee have we placed our hope, O Theotokos!

---

# TONE 2

## SATURDAY EVENING: VESPERS (TONE 2)

*On Saturday evening, at "Lord, I have cried", we set 8 verses, and sing resurrectional stichera, Tone 2:*[^36]

*Sticheron 1:* Come, let us worship God the Word, begotten of the Father before the ages, and incarnate of the Virgin Mary; for having endured the Cross, He surrendered Himself unto burial as He Himself willed; and having risen from the dead, He hath saved me, a wandering man!

*Sticheron 2:* Christ our Savior, having nailed unto the Cross the handwriting that was against us, tore it asunder and abolished the dominion of death. Therefore we worship His third-day Resurrection!

*Sticheron 3:* With the archangels let us hymn the Resurrection of Christ; for He is the Redeemer and the Savior of our souls, and in fearful glory and mighty power He comes again to judge the world which He hath fashioned!

*Anatolian stichera:*

*Sticheron 4:* Thee, Who wast crucified and buried, an angel proclaimed as Master, and [p35LA] said unto the women: Come and see where the Lord lay; for He is risen as He said, as almighty! Therefore we worship Thee, the only Immortal One: O Life-giver Christ, have mercy on us!

*Sticheron 5:* By Thy Cross, O Christ, Thou hast abolished the curse of the tree; and by Thy burial Thou hast put to death the dominion of death; and by Thy Resurrection Thou hast enlightened the race of mankind. Therefore we cry aloud unto Thee: O Benefactor, Christ our God, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron 6:* Unto Thee, O Lord, the gates of death opened in fear, and the gatekeepers of Hades, seeing Thee, were terrified; for Thou didst shatter the brazen gates and crush the iron bars, and didst lead us out from darkness and the shadow of death, and tore asunder our chains!

*Sticheron 7:* Chanting a saving hymn, we send it forth from our mouths: Come, all ye, into the house of the Lord, let us cry aloud, saying: O Thou Who wast crucified upon the tree and art risen from the dead, Who art in the bosom of God, cleanse our sins!

*Dogmatikon Theotokion:*[^37]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* The shadow of the law hath passed away when grace arrived; for as the bush burned with fire yet was not consumed, so as a virgin thou didst give birth and didst remain a virgin. In place of a pillar of fire, the Sun of righteousness hath shone forth; in place of Moses, Christ, the Salvation of our souls!

*At the Aposticha, Resurrectional Stichera, Tone 2:*

*Sticheron 1:* Thy Resurrection, O Christ Savior, hath enlightened all the universe and recalled Thine own creation; O almighty Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron 2:* When Thou, O Christ, wast nailed unto the Cross [p36LA] the form of creation was changed; and the soldiers, displaying inhumanity, pierced Thy side; and the Jews asked that the tomb be sealed, not knowing Thy power. But for the tender mercy of Thy compassions, having accepted a three-day burial and Resurrection, O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron 3:* An angel brought "Rejoice" unto her that is full of grace before Thy conception, O Lord; and an angel also rolled away the stone of Thy glorious tomb at Thy Resurrection. The one indeed proclaimed signs of joy in place of sorrow; while the other preached unto us the Master and Life-giver in place of death. Therefore we cry aloud unto Thee: O Benefactor of all, Lord, glory to Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O new wonder, greater than all ancient wonders! For who hath known a mother giving birth without a man, and carrying upon her arm Him Who upholds all creation? It is the Word of God that was born, Whom as an infant, O all-pure one, thou didst bear in thine arms; and having maternal boldness toward Him, cease not to pray for those who honor thee, to have compassion and save our souls!

*Resurrectional Troparion, Tone 2:*[^38]

When Thou didst descend unto death, O Life Immortal, then didst Thou slay Hades with the brilliance of the Godhead; and when Thou didst raise up the dead from the nethermost depths, all the heavenly powers cried aloud: O Life-giver, Christ God, glory to Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* All beyond thought, all most glorious are thy mysteries, O Theotokos! Sealed in purity [p37LA] and preserved in virginity, thou art recognized to be the true Mother, having given birth unto the true God; entreat Him to save our souls!

## SUNDAY MIDNIGHT OFFICE (TONE 2)

*Canon to the Holy and Life-Originating Trinity, Tone 2. Ode 1. Heirmos: In the deep He submerged of old Pharaoh...*[^39]

*Troparion 1:* One essence in two essences we confess Christ: begotten of the Father before the ages and not made, in the last times incarnate without seed of the pure Virgin, and having renewed the human race by His rising!

*Troparion 2:* Strange and marvelous was the birth before the ages from the unoriginate Father, and again from the Virgin without father, which the heavenly hosts glorify with fear; and we the earthly hymn Thy Nativity, and worship Thy rising!

*Troparion 3:* Thou art the source from the root, being the cause from the Father unto the Son... unto the consubstantial and three-personed Godhead: enlighten my heart with the brilliance of communion, by Thy beneficent radiance!

*Theotokion:* In thee have I placed my hope and my wretched soul; give me a helping hand, O Sovereign Lady: deliver me from corruption and save me by thy tender mercy!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: The desert hath blossomed as a lily...*[^40]

*Troparion 1:* We glorify the one essence in the thrice-holy God; we worship the undivided Trinity: the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit, that It may uphold and preserve the Christian race without stumbling!

*Troparion 2:* From the unoriginate Father the Word descended into her, taking upon Himself a twofold nature, becoming at once God and man [p38LA] that He might renew man by His Resurrection!

*Troparion 3:* Establish upon the rock of faith, and broaden by Thy love the teaching of my heart, [and] of Thy servants, O three-personed Unity; for Thou art our God, and hoping in Thee we shall not be put to shame!

*Theotokion:* I have despaired of my life, O all-pure Sovereign Lady; vilely have I lived upon earth, both in words and in deeds, and I bow down before thee: have mercy on me, I cry unto thee; cast me not away in the time of sorrow!

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 2.*

*Podoben: "Seeking the highest" (Въ вышнихъ ища :~):* [^41]

O Most Holy Trinity, have mercy on those whom Thou hast fashioned; deliver them from fire and all condemnation; for Thou canst do all things, being compassionate, and there is none so merciful. Therefore we fall down before Thee, crying: Forgive those who have sinned, O Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion:* O all-pure Virgin, have mercy on thy servants; deliver from fire and every torment those who cry day and night unto thy Son, O all-hymned one; despise not Thy flock, O Christ, through the prayers of her that bare Thee!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: I hear Thee by report, O Lord...*[^42]

*Troparion 1:* Perfect from Perfect, God begotten of God and not made, not in time, and born before the ages; He was pleased to become flesh, partook of incorruption, and rose on the third day!

*Troparion 2:* In Unity let us glorify the Trinity, and the Trinity in Unity, an undivided Godhead: the Father with the unoriginate, and the Savior co-enthroned, with the All-Holy Spirit let us glorify!

*Troparion 3:* In singing the unoriginate and unapproachable Unity in three Persons, having, as it were, a vision [p39LA] distinct in hypostases, yet united and undivided in Godhead and glory!

*Theotokion:* O undefiled Sovereign Lady, with trembling we hymn thee: give ear unto my voice, and drive away the demonic darkening, O pure one, that rises up against me; for thou art my protector and mediatress, and holy salvation!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Enlighten...*[^43]

*Troparion 1:* One kingdom and dominion in three hypostases let us glorify without division; for the Father unoriginately begat the Only-begotten Son, co-eternal and consubstantial, Whom, having risen from the dead, let us glorify!

*Troparion 2:* With the archangels let us sing praise unto the Trinity, undivided in Godhead, yet divided in known personal properties: the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit, in the thrice-perfect Godhead glorify the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit!

*Troparion 3:* In Moses' bush, as He appeared in visible darkness, the Father's Word was called an angel, manifesting His coming unto us, for Whose sake Thou hast proclaimed the dominion of the divine origin of the One in three hypostases!

*Theotokion:* Hope of all the ends of the earth, O blessed Virgin Theotokos: in the time of my life deprive me not of rich compassions, but abundantly grant me thy tender mercy, that I also with faith may venerate thine image, O all-pure one!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: Held fast in the abyss of sin...*[^44]

*Troparion 1:* One God in three properties, distinct in persons, yet undivided in essence [p40LA] giving life unto the ends [of the earth] by His Nativity!

*Troparion 2:* One Light in three Persons, in the Father, and the Son, and the Holy Spirit, three indivisibly, and glorified in three properties, and known as worshipped in unity!

*Troparion 3:* The unbegotten Mind is the Father, and wisely shining forth as the image is the Word, the co-essential unoriginate Son; and the co-enthroned Spirit, [Who wrought] the Incarnation of the Creator in two essences according to the Word!

*Theotokion:* Having given birth unto the Light-giver of the world, enlighten my darkened soul, O Lady; entreat Christ Who was crucified and rose from the tomb on the third day!

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*[^45]

Rising at midnight, with heart and lips we offer an angelic hymn unto Thee: enlighten us by Thy Resurrection; in the day of great joy, the day of enlightenment, save us who sing unto Thee, O Holy Trinity, have mercy on our souls!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* All creation worthily hymns thee, O Virgin Mary Theotokos; yet human mind cannot attain to praise thee worthily, for the divine fire made its abode in thee: burn up our transgressions, and save those who hymn thee!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: The godless command...*[^46]

*Troparion 1:* One essence in three hypostases, O Most Holy consubstantial Trinity, venerated by us, branches planted by God from the divine Source, perfectly inseparable: Father, Son, and Spirit, worshipped together!

*Troparion 2:* Illumined by all-radiant beams, O Most Holy Trinity, in one dominion preserve Thy singers [p41LA] from adverse assaults, that we may unceasingly glorify Thee: O Father, Son, and Holy Spirit, glorified in one Godhead!

*Troparion 3:* Theologizing the three-leaved glory of the one Lordship, the Seraphim stand before Thee with awe-inspired reverence, glorifying the three-hypostatic Godhead; with them, rightly rejoicing, we sing: Father, Son, and Holy Spirit!

*Theotokion:* Thou art the refuge of Christians, O holy one, a rampart and divine protection, protectress unto all of us who hymn thee with love: deliver Thy servants from passions, O good one, that we may hymn thee and bless: Blessed art Thou, [O God] of our fathers!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: The fiery furnace once...*[^47]

*Troparion 1:* Knowing the uncreated and creative Trinity in three hypostases: O Father, Son, and Holy Spirit, by Whom all creation subsists, preserved by the word of God; therefore renew the first-formed [Adam] by Thy Resurrection!

*Troparion 2:* O sovereign and all-perfect Trinity, having brought all things by the Word out of non-being, giving life by the Spirit: for his sake Thou didst descend to deliver the human race by the third-day rising!

*Troparion 3:* Unharmed, the most glorious children, having seen the immutable and living image of God, sang in the midst of the flame: Let the consubstantial creation sing!

*Theotokion:* Him before Whom all things tremble, thou hast carried upon thine arms, O pure Virgin, and dost nurse Him: a strange mystery and awesome marvel, that of thee was born the Creator of Adam and my God; entreat Him, O pure one, to save [p42LA] all who in song glorify Thee unto ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: The Lord, Who is co-unoriginate with the Father...*

*Troparion 1:* Ruling over all and holding all things together, O most radiant Trinity, through prayers enlighten my darkened soul; O Word Who art unoriginate, absolve my transgressions, that glorifying Thy holy Resurrection, we may magnify Thee!

*Troparion 2:* By the Word Thou hast established [all things], O Father, in wisdom, and by the Holy Spirit: heaven and earth and all the airborne creatures; Thou hast quickened the ministering spirits, and formed man with Thine own hands; willingly enduring crucifixion, by Thy rising Thou hast renewed our nature!

*Troparion 3:* O God of threefold light, enlighten the hearts of those who hymn Thee; O Holy Spirit of three rays, by Thy light grant unto Thy servants that all may know and behold Thy will, which is good and perfect, and magnify Thy mercy!

*Theotokion:* Grant salvation of the soul from every calamity and distress, O Thou Who art praised in three Persons, ineffable, unique, and almighty: preserve Thine own flock, and deliver [us] for the sake of the Theotokos!

*Then: It is truly meet... as [usual on Sunday]. After that, Trisagion. After Our Father, the troparia written after the Trinity [canon] of the First Tone, and the litany.*[^48]

## SUNDAY MATINS (TONE 2)

*On Sunday at Matins, at "God is the Lord", Troparion, Tone 2: When Thou didst descend unto death... (twice); and as at Vespers [the Theotokion] (once). Then after the first stichology, Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*[^49]

The noble Joseph, taking down Thy most pure Body from the Cross, wrapped it in a clean shroud [p43LA] and with sweet spices laid it closed in a new tomb; but on the third day Thou didst rise, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Who has seen or who has heard a mother without a man bearing her Creator, and nursing Him Who gives food unto all flesh? O marvel! The Cherubic throne has thy womb appeared to be, O Theotokos; O joyful one, pray for our souls!

*And after the second stichology, Sessional Hymn, Tone 2:*[^50]

Unto the myrrh-bearing women standing by the tomb, the Angel cried aloud: Myrrh is fitting for the dead, but Christ has shown Himself a stranger to corruption! Cry aloud singing that He is risen, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Escaping the laws of nature by thy divine childbearing, thou hast united virginity [with motherhood]; for thou alone didst bear the One Who was timelessly begotten of the Father before ages: therefore we magnify thee as Theotokos!

*Hypakoe:* After the Passion, the women going to the tomb to anoint Thy body, O Christ God, saw an Angel in the tomb and were amazed; for they heard a voice from him, that He is risen, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Degrees, Tone 2. Antiphon 1:*[^51]

Unto heaven I lift up the eyes of my heart: unto Thee, O Savior, save me by Thine illumination!

Have mercy on us who sin against Thee greatly at every hour, O my Christ; grant me the manner of repentance before the end!

*Glory... Both now...:* Unto the Holy Spirit it belongs to reign, to sanctify, and to shake creation; for He is God, consubstantial with the Father and the Word!

*Antiphon 2:*

[p44LA] If the Lord had not been in us, who could have been kept whole from the enemy and man-slayer?

Unto their teeth surrender not Thy servant, O Savior; for like sheep they move against me, for they are my enemies!

*Glory... Both now...:* Unto the Holy Spirit belong the origin of life and honor; for as being God He upholds and preserves all created things by the Father through the Son!

*Antiphon 3:*

They who trust in the Lord are like the holy mountain, who shall in no wise be shaken by the assaults of the enemy!

In iniquities let not those who live divinely stretch forth their hands; for Christ will not give the rod unto His inheritance!

*Glory... Both now...:* By the Holy Spirit every wisdom flows: thence grace unto the Apostles, and by sufferings the martyrs are crowned and the prophets see!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 2: Arise, O Lord my God... Verse: O Lord my God, in Thee have I put my hope. Let every breath praise the Lord. Resurrection Gospel. Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ. Psalm 50. Prayer of the Sunday. Through the intercessions of the Apostles... Both now... Through the intercessions of the Theotokos...*

*On Sunday, Resurrection Canon, work of Cosmas the Monk, Tone 2:*[^52]

*Ode 1. Heirmos: In the deep once Pharaoh's host was overwhelmed by the heavily armed power; but the Word made flesh destroyed sins by His almighty strength: the most glorious Lord, for He is glorified!*

*Troparion 1:* The ruler of the world, O Good One, unto whom we were enrolled because we obeyed not Thy commandment, is condemned by Thy Cross: for drawing near unto Thee as unto one mortal, he fell from the dominion of his power, and was shown to be weak [p45LA] and stripped of his power, he was clothed in infirmity!

*Troparion 2:* Deliverer of the human race, and Author of incorruptible life, Thou didst come into the world: by Thy Resurrection having torn asunder the shroud of death, Him we glorify, for gloriously is He glorified!

*Theotokion:* Shown to be higher than all creation, visible and invisible, O most pure one; for thou didst bear the Creator as He willed to become incarnate in thy womb: Whom entreat with boldness to save our souls!

*Another Canon to the Cross, work of Sir John of Damascus. Ode 1. Heirmos: Come, ye people, let us sing a song...*[^53]

*Troparion 1:* Borne upon the Cross willingly, O Christ God, Thou didst slay the destroyer, and pour shame upon his wickedness, and having renewed mankind: for He is glorified!

*Troparion 2:* Commanded with joy, the myrrh-bearing women said unto His disciples: Proclaim My victory over the dead unto ages: for He is glorified!

*Theotokion:* Who can worthily recount of thee the conception beyond word? For thou didst bear God in the flesh, O most pure one, Who appeared for our salvation!

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos the same:*[^54]

*Troparion 1:* The immaterial ladder of old, and the path strangely made dry through the sea, foreshadowed thy childbearing, O pure Lady, which we sing: for He is glorified!

*Troparion 2:* The Power of the Most High, the perfect hypostasis of the Wisdom of God, having become incarnate incorruptibly of thee, drew near unto mankind: for He is glorified!

[p46LA] *Theotokion:* He passed through the impenetrable closed door of thy womb, the Sun of Righteousness, O most pure one, shining forth upon the world: for He is glorified!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: The wilderness hath blossomed as a lily, O Lord, the barren Church of the Gentiles by Thy coming, wherein my heart is established.*[^55]

*Troparion 1:* Creation was altered at Thy Passion, beholding Thee in humble form mocked by the lawless, Who didst establish all things by Thy divine nod!

*Troparion 2:* Having formed me of dust in Thine image with Thine own hand, and broken again into the dust of death by sin, O Christ, having descended into Hades, Thou hast raised me together by Thy Resurrection!

*Theotokion:* The angelic ranks were filled with wonder, O most pure one, and human hearts were awed at thy childbearing; therefore we, the faithful, honor thee as Theotokos!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: Establish me upon the rock of faith...*

*Troparion 1:* Pouring forth water and blood together from the immortal fountain of the divine side, Thou hast given drink and made me glad, and I sing: There is none holy like our God!

*Troparion 2:* Having raised up the fallen [man], and taken me, the lost one, upon His shoulders, risen He brought me back; and I sing: There is none holy like our God, none righteous!

*Theotokion:* Thou alone beyond all the earth wast granted great things above nature; for the God Whom no creation can contain, thou hast contained in thy womb; therefore we all acknowledge thee as Theotokos!

*Another Canon to the Most Pure Theotokos. Heirmos the same:*

*Troparion 1:* He Who is above all times, as the Creator and Maker of time [p47LA] was willingly formed of thee as an infant, O Virgin!

*Troparion 2:* Let us hymn the womb more spacious than the heavens, for whose sake Adam lives rejoicing in the heavens!

*Theotokion:* After childbearing, O undefiled one, thou didst remain even as before childbearing; for thou didst bear God Who saved man by the Tree, O most pure Lady and Protectress of our race!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Coming from the Virgin, neither an intercessor nor an angel, but Thou Thyself, O Lord, didst become incarnate and saved all of me, a man; therefore I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!*[^56]

*Troparion 1:* Standing before the tribunal as one condemned, my God, without crying out, O Lord, Thou didst send forth judgment unto the nations, and by Thy Passion, O Christ, Thou hast wrought salvation for the world!

*Troparion 2:* By Thy Passion the weapons of the enemy failed, and by Thine adversary-crushing descent into Hades the strongholds were destroyed, and the dominion of death was overthrown!

*Theotokion:* Thee, the haven of salvation and unshakable wall, all we faithful [have acquired], O Lady Theotokos; for by thy prayers thou dost deliver our souls from afflictions!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: Hearing the report of Thee...*

*Troparion 1:* Alone as in a winepress, O Lord, Thou didst tread down Thine enemies; for seeing Thy manly raiment dyed with strength, the all-destroying enemy fell, and fallen Adam was raised up!

*Troparion 2:* Behold, He came unto the forefather, leading forth the bound: having clothed Himself with our nature as a warrior with armor, He led me forth whole from the all-painful enemy, a captive!

*Theotokion:* Thou alone, O Theotokos, didst become higher than all creation; for thou didst bear the Creator and hold Him in thine arms [p48LA] save those who praise thee, O blessed and all-pure Virgin!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: Lo, the divine mountain is exalted, the house of the Lord above the hosts, the Theotokos manifestly.*

*Troparion 1:* Transcending the laws of nature, having alone borne the Ruler of creation, thou wast accounted worthy of the divine calling!

*Troparion 2:* Marvel! How the fire did not consume thy holy womb, prefiguring the bush of old, O pure one! Marvelous and awesome is thy childbearing, wherein the apostolic choir was magnified!
