*By the Holy Spirit:* The riches of divine theology, contemplation of God, and wisdom: for therein does the Word reveal all the Father's decrees.

*Prokeimenon, Tone 4: Arise, O Lord, help us.*

*Verse: Which we have heard with our ears...*

*Let every breath [praise the Lord]... The Evangelical Resurrection [Gospel]. Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ... Psalm 50: Have mercy on me, O God...*

*Resurrection Canon, Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*

Having traversed the deep of the Red Sea with dry feet, ancient Israel of old vanquished the might of Amalek in the wilderness through the hands of Moses stretched out in the form of a cross.

*Troparion:* Thou hast raised up our fallen state, correcting upon the most pure wood of the Cross the ancient all-destructive ruin, O Master, healing it as Good and Almighty! [^109]

*Troparion:* Bodily in the tomb, but in Hades with Thy soul as God, in Paradise with the thief, and on the throne wast Thou, O Christ, with the Father and the Spirit, filling all things, Thyself uncircumscribed!

*Theotokion:* Without seed by the Holy Spirit, by the Father's will, thou didst conceive the Son of God, and in the flesh didst give birth unto Him Who from the Father is without mother, and for our sake from thee without father!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: I shall open my mouth...*

*Troparion:* Healing the ruin of mankind, renewed by divine Blood, and crushing by might him that had crushed Thy creation of old...

*Troparion:* The resurrection of the dead was wrought by Thy slaying, and strength was given over unto mortification, submitting with eternal life, as we bow down before God incarnate!

*Theotokion:* Higher in beauty than the heavenly hosts, the living temple of God was thine, O Virgin, who didst bear in thy womb our God, the most pure mountain!

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Mother of God. Heirmos: Three staths...*

*Troparion:* The peoples trembled, the nations were troubled, and mighty kingdoms bowed down, O holy one, from the fear of thy childbearing; God the King came, overthrowing the tyrant, and delivered the world from corruption!

*Troparion:* He that dwells on high made thee a habitation for Himself in His descent unto mankind, O Christ, and showed thee unshaken; for alone after childbearing did she preserve virginity, keeping the birth of the Creator!

*Troparion:* Written in the unwritten Father, on the tree of old, and in the Mother's bosom Christ is bodily enrolled; Who after childbearing is preserved as truly Virgin beyond understanding [p99LA] unto Whom let us sing aloud, unto our God, for He has been glorified![^110]

*Ode 3. Heirmos: Thy Church rejoices in Thee, O Christ, crying: Thou art my strength, O Lord, my refuge and my firm foundation!*

*Troparion:* The Tree of life, the noetic true Vine, hangs upon the Cross, gushing forth incorruption unto all!

*Troparion:* How great, how awesome is He Who destroyed the boasting of Hades! As incorrupt God, He is now risen bodily from the tomb!

*Theotokion:* Thou alone on earth wast shown forth as the mediatress of supernatural blessings, O Mother of God: therefore with joy do we cry unto thee!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: Thy singers...*

*Troparion:* Filled with serpentine venom, the jaws struck against Thee, O Savior; and the Creator and Master of all with nails crushed his hand, for there is none holy besides Thee, O Lord!

*Troparion:* Thou didst willingly appear, O Lover of mankind; laid in the tomb, O Life-Giver, Thou didst open the gates of Hades unto eternal souls, for there is none holy besides Thee, O Lord!

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth as the untilled field, giving birth to the living Ear of wheat, becoming the mediatress of immortality unto all that partake of the Holy One Who holily rests in the holy place!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Descending from on high...*

*Troparion:* The earthly nature is cleansed, having conversed with the unbearable divine Fire, as in resurrection, O most pure Virgin, unto Him Who preserved unharmed from thee the unbaked Bread!

*Troparion:* Truly drawing near unto God, as higher than all the angelic ranks, alone in the beauty of virginity, as Mother of the Ruler of all [p100LA] Almighty![^111]

*Troparion:* Marveled at beyond mind, they cannot comprehend thy childbearing, O Virgin; for in thy womb, O pure one, thou didst conceive the Word, the Fore-First, Who established every word!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Beholding Thee, the Sun of righteousness, lifted up...*

*Troparion:* Thou didst come healing by Thy Passion; on the Cross Thou dost suffer in Thy most pure flesh, in which Thou wast willingly clothed: therefore we cry aloud unto Thee: Glory to Thy power!

*Troparion:* Leaving deadened death in the tomb, my Deliverer, bound today, is risen: unto Him let us cry aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Theotokion:* Unwedded didst thou give birth, O Virgin, and after childbearing wast preserved; therefore unceasingly with joy, O Sovereign Lady, in uncondemned faith we cry unto thee!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: Unto the untraceable divine counsel...*

*Troparion:* Israel that received the law understood Thee not, God Who gavest the law; transgressing the law, they nailed Thee to the Cross as lawless, unworthy of the Lawgiver!

*Troparion:* Thy divine soul, having despoiled the dwellings of Hades, raised up together the dead of ages, while Thy life-bearing body became the source of incorruption unto all!

*Theotokion:* Thee, Ever-Virgin, truly the only Mother of God do we glorify, whom the seer Moses beheld prefigured, the unburnt bush that cleaved unto the fire, O most pure one!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Sitting in [glory]...*

*Troparion:* He lived with men, the Invisible becoming visible, in the likeness of the Son [p101LA] comprehended Godhead, and being depicted from thee, O Virgin, alien unto bitterness to those who know thee as the true Mother of God.[^112]

*Troparion:* Immaterially did the Virgin receive the Immaterial One, become an Infant from her amidst material things; therefore in two natures is God recognized as flesh-bearer, and above earthly nature.

*Troparion:* A Virgin after childbearing: He Who dwelt in thee, O Virgin, and was born of thee without seed, the Word, yet remained with the Father; and in childbearing He preserved thee a Virgin, as Master and Creator.

*Resurrection Canon, Tone 4. Ode 5. Heirmos:*

Thou, O Lord, art my light, come into the world, a holy Light turning from the darkness of ignorance those who sing praise unto Thee in faith!

*Troparion:* Thou, O Lord, didst mercifully come down unto earth; Thou didst raise up fallen human nature, being lifted up upon the Tree!

*Troparion:* Thou hast taken from me, O Christ, the condemnation of sins; Thou hast destroyed the pangs of death, O Bountiful One, by Thy divine Resurrection!

*Theotokion:* Thee as an invincible weapon against enemies do we set forth; thee as the fortress and hope of our salvation do we possess, O Bride of God!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: All things were astonished...*

*Troparion:* Senseless Hades received Thee wholly into his mouth, seeing Thee nailed upon the Tree and pierced with a spear, thinking Thee a mere man without living soul; but his teeth were crushed by Thy divine might! He beheld the temple of Thy body destroyed, yet the tomb corrupted [Thee] not, being laid therein by Thine own will; he took both into his grasp, soul and [p102LA] body, while the other sent them down into death.[^113]

*Theotokion:* Fulfilled and brought to an end were the prophetic sayings: for thou, O Virgin, without knowing a man's bed, didst receive in thy womb God Who is over all, and didst give birth unto the ageless Son, granting peace unto all that sing praise unto thee!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Arisen...*

*Troparion:* As a house of glory, and the holy mountain of God, a Bride, a bridal chamber, a temple of sanctification, God the Savior lived in thee, making us upright and all-immaculate.

*Troparion:* Thou didst accept human covering, O Christ, without seed, most purely, inconfusedly, endued with a rational and noetic soul, by active will possessing sovereign mastery.

*Troparion:* The virginal womb put to shame the tyrant's mind; for as an Infant He touched with His hand the asp's soul-destroying den, and cast down the proud apostate, subduing him under the feet of our faithful Prince!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: I shall sacrifice unto Thee with a voice of praise, O Lord, the Church cries unto Thee, having been cleansed from the blood of demons by the blood that mercifully flowed from Thy side!*

*Troparion:* Thou didst ascend upon the Cross, having girded Thyself with power, grappling with the tyrant; casting him down from the heights, Thou didst raise Adam up together with Thee by Thine invincible might!

*Troparion:* Thou didst rise most radiantly from the tomb, O Christ; Thou didst scatter all enemies by divine might, and filled all peoples with joy!

*Theotokion:* Than which of all ancient [p103LA] marvels more marvelous, that as a Virgin in thy womb thou didst unwedded receive the Word Who holds all things together, containing Him without constraint![^114]

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: I shall sacrifice...*

*Troparion:* The senseless one opened his throat and swallowed me, enlarging his jaws; but having descended, O Christ, Thou didst bring up my life, O Lover of mankind!

*Troparion:* Death was vanquished by death, overthrown it fell, and incorruption was granted unto us; unto the women He proclaimed the joy of immortality!

*Theotokion:* A dwelling of the unapproachable Light of the Godhead wast thou shown forth, O pure Maiden, which the heavenly ranks cannot behold without trembling!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: The same.*

*Troparion:* Of old the deceitful serpent took me captive and put me to death through my foremother Eve; but now He that fashioned me, for thy sake hath recalled me from corruption!

*Troparion:* The abyss of ineffable mercies showed thee forth as chosen, O compassionate Maiden; through the marvels wrought in thee the divine Pearl shone forth!

*Theotokion:* O all-praised, the thought of the Apostles and praise of martyrs, the fair and marvelous proclamation of prophets: together with them pray that we be delivered from every affliction!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: In the furnace of fire the Abrahamite children of old, burned rather with longing for piety than with the flame, cried aloud: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy glory, O Lord!*

*Troparion:* Unto incorruption was mankind led up by the divine shed Blood, singing with thanksgiving: Blessed art Thou in the temple of Thy [p104LA] glory, O Lord![^115]

*Troparion:* As life-bearing, as more splendid than Paradise truly, and radiant above any royal bridal chamber was Thy tomb shown forth, O Christ, the fountain of our resurrection!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, consecrated divine dwelling of the Most High! For through thee was joy given unto those that cry unto thee, O Mother of God: Blessed art thou among women, O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: They served not the creature...*

*Troparion:* Humbled upon the Tree, O Christ, pouring out Thy blood upon the earth, Thou didst cast down the adversary: O all-praised God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Troparion:* By Thy might raise up the horn of us who serve Thee, O Christ, Who art risen from the dead, granting rich and divine wealth: O all-praised God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Trinity Troparion:* Following the divine word, we glorify one Godhead in three Persons indivisible, in one flame: O all-praised God of our fathers, blessed art Thou!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: ...*

*Troparion:* Virginal love draws me unto singing, the fire within my heart, to sing unto the Virgin Mother: Blessed art thou...

*Troparion:* One Lordship do I honor in three Persons indivisible, singing the three-hypostatic essence: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth as the orient of the noetic Sun Who appeared bodily, enlightening all, O Mother of God, Sovereign Lady ever-blessed!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: Daniel, stretching out his hands, stopped the yawning mouths of lions in the pit; and the children, lovers of piety, having girded themselves with virtue, quenched the power of fire...*

[p105LA] ...crying aloud: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise the Lord![^116]

*Troparion:* Stretching out Thy hands upon the Cross, Thou didst gather all nations unto Thyself, and showed forth one Church, O Master, singing praise unto Thee, earthly and heavenly together harmonious singing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise the Lord!

*Troparion:* In white guise appeared the Angel of the Resurrection unto the women, flashing with unapproachable light: Why cry ye at the tomb seeking the Living as dead? Behold, Christ is risen, unto Whom we sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Theotokion:* Thou alone among all generations wast shown forth, O Virgin, all-pure Mother; thou wast the habitation of the Godhead, O all-immaculate, unconsumed by the fire of the divine Light: therefore do all of us bless thee, O Mary, Bride of God!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: The Children...*

*Troparion:* Beholding the unjust slaying, creation wept in terror, the sun was clothed in darkness; and we unceasingly cry aloud unto Thee: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise the Lord!

*Troparion:* Having descended in might unto Hades, Thou didst make paths of resurrection unto all; Thou didst ascend carrying Adam upon Thy shoulders and brought him unto God: therefore we cry aloud: Praise the Lord!

*Trinity Troparion:* Knowing one God... we proclaim Thee the Word of the unoriginate Father... the Holy Spirit as Witness, worshiping the Trinity together: Bless the Lord!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: The same.*

*Troparion:* He that fashioned thee from the rib of Adam is incarnate of thy virginity, singing unto Whom [p106LA] we cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, praise the Lord![^117]

*Troparion:* All the ends of the earth beheld the mystery in thee, O Virgin, singing of Thine incarnate salvation: Bless the Lord!

*Troparion:* The Children equal in number, O Savior... the virginal company trampled the flame, the youths crying: Bless the Lord!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: A cornerstone was cut out from an unhewn mountain, from thee, O Virgin, even Christ, Who hath joined together the separated natures: therefore rejoicing do we magnify thee, O Mother of God!*

*Troparion:* Restoring me wholly, O Savior, having endured crucifixion in the flesh, O Christ, only Merciful One!

*Troparion:* Thy disciples, beholding Thy tomb opened, and the God-bearing winding sheets emptied by Thy resurrection, spoke with the Angels: Truly Christ is risen!

*Trinity Troparion:* The unity of the divine essence in three Hypostases: the unoriginate Father, the Word, and the divine Spirit do we praise, the incarnate Word, one God in two natures!

*Another Canon to the Cross. Heirmos: As an animate ark...*

*Troparion:* Thou didst ascend, O Christ, upon the Tree of the Cross, and didst call all nations, willing to save the corrupt nature of Adam; therefore with thanksgiving we cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy Crucifixion, O Lord!

*Troparion:* Early the Myrrh-bearing women came unto Thy tomb with haste, weeping to anoint Thee, O Christ; and He appeared risen from [p107LA] the dead, granting this joy unto all that glorify Thy Resurrection![^118]

*Theotokion:* Through thee, O pure Mother of God, have we known the God of all, and we have become sons of the Most High, ever crying unto thee: Rejoice, thou that art full of grace, the Lord is with thee!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: The same.*

*Troparion:* Passing as a lily clothed in purity, O most pure one, by the Holy Spirit, shining in the midst of thorns and filling with fragrance those who sincerely magnify thee!

*Troparion:* Having assumed corrupt earthly nature from thine all-immaculate womb, the Incorrupt One showed it forth in Himself as incorrupt in His tender mercy: therefore do we magnify thee as the Mother of God!

*Theotokion:* O thou that rulest over all creatures, grant unto thy people victorious vanquishing, putting the unsubmissive under the feet of our Prince, that we may magnify thee as the Mother of God!

*Praises Stichera, Tone 4:*

Thou Who didst endure crucifixion and death, O Savior, and didst rise from the dead, O Almighty Lord: Glory to Thee!

*Sticheron:* By Thy Cross, O Christ our Savior, deliver us from the snare of the enemy; and risen from the dead, raise us up who have fallen from sins, stretching forth Thy hand, O Lord Lover of mankind, by the intercessions of Thy saints!

*Sticheron:* By Thy Cross, O Christ, we have been set free from the ancient curse; and by Thy death Thou didst bring to naught the devil who tormented our nature, and by Thy Resurrection [p108LA] Thou didst fill all things with joy: therefore do we cry unto Thee: O Thou Who art risen from the dead, Lord, glory to Thee![^119]

*Sticheron:* Thou didst accept death in the flesh, and didst grant us immortality, O Savior; and Thou didst dwell in the tomb that Thou mightest free us from Hades, raising us up together with Thyself: Thou didst suffer indeed as man, yet didst rise as God: therefore do we cry unto Thee: Glory to Thee, O Life-Giver, Lord, only Lover of mankind!

*Other Stichera of the Resurrection:*

*Sticheron:* The rocks were split, O Savior, when Thy Cross was set up at the Place of the Skull; the gatekeepers of Hades were terrified when Thou wast laid in the tomb as dead; for having abolished the power of death, Thou didst grant incorruption unto the dead by Thy Resurrection, O Life-Giver, Savior, Lord: Glory to Thee!

*Sticheron:* Rejoice today, all creatures! Rejoice, for Christ is risen, having shattered the brazen gates and broken the iron bars, and brought Adam forth from the gates of death, and freed mankind from corruption! Where is thy victory, O Hades? Let us the faithful cry aloud: Where is thy sting, O death? Thou hast destroyed the arrogance of Hades, O Master; for as Ruler and Lord of all, Thou didst expose the devil by the Cross, and didst grant resurrection unto the dead, and didst make known ineffable life in the Gospel, being the only Good and Lover of mankind!

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.*

*Sticheron:* Where is Jesus Whom ye kept watching? Tell us, O Judeans: Where is He Whom ye laid in the tomb and sealed the stone? Give up the dead Man, Whom ye deprived of life! Give up the Buried One, or believe in Him Who is risen! For even if ye keep silence [p109LA] the stone rolled away from the tomb shall cry aloud of the Lord's Resurrection! Great is Thy mercy, great is the mystery of Thy dispensation, O our Savior: Glory to Thee![^120]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*

*Sticheron:* Where are the seals of the tomb? Where are Pilate's soldiers guarding the sepulchre? O how was the treasure stolen? How was the Buried One moved? Slander not the Resurrection of Christ! The Lord was willingly crucified, and He is risen even as He willed, granting life and great mercy unto the inhabited world!

*Glory: Evangelical Sticheron. Both now: Most blessed art thou, O Virgin Mother of God...*

*At the Liturgy. The Beatitudes, Tone 4:*

*Troparion:* On account of the tree was Adam banished from Paradise; but through the tree of the Cross did Christ establish the thief in Paradise: for the one, having tasted, transgressed the commandment of the Creator; but the other, being crucified, confessed Thee as God, crying: Remember us, O Savior, in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion:* Living in Paradise, Adam transgressed; but the thief, beholding Thee nailed upon the Cross, O Master, confessed Thee as God, seeking the heavenly Kingdom, and with these words cried unto Thee, O Lord Lover of mankind, our God: Remember us!

*Troparion:* Ascending upon the Cross, Thou didst destroy the power of death and wiped out the handwriting of old against us, O Master; as Thou didst accept the repentance of the thief, grant it also unto us, O Lover of mankind, who in faith cry unto Thee, O Lord, merciful Christ: Remember us!

*Troparion:* Ascending upon the Cross, Thou didst destroy the dominion of death; laid in the tomb [p110LA] Thou didst raise up all those in Hades, becoming the Firstborn from the dead, and eternal life was bestowed: therefore by Thy Resurrection save us, O our Deliverer: Remember us![^121]

*Troparion:* Thy disciples, O Christ, having been eyewitnesses of Thy sufferings and burial and divine resurrection, caught the nations and as fishes brought all in faith: with them we cry unto Thee: Remember us!

*Trinity Troparion:* The Father and the Son and the Holy Spirit do we all with one mind glorify, worthily praying unto the Unity of the Godhead in three Persons, abiding unconfusedly, simple, indivisible, and unapproachable; worshiping Him we sing, being delivered from torment: Remember us!

*Theotokion:* Thy Mother, O Christ, who in the flesh bore Thee without seed, and remained in truth an incorrupt Virgin: her do we bring unto Thee in prayer, O Master; grant forgiveness of sins as Merciful unto those that cry unto Thee: Remember us, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy Kingdom!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 4: How magnified are Thy works, O Lord! In wisdom hast Thou made them all (LXX Ps. 103:24).*

*Verse: Bless the Lord, O my soul: O Lord my God, Thou art very great (LXX Ps. 103:1).*

*Epistle: To the Hebrews [2 Cor. 6:16–18]:*

Brethren, ye are the temple of the living God; as God hath said: I will dwell in them, and walk in them; and I will be their God, and they shall be My people. Therefore come out from among them, and be ye cleansed, says the Lord, and touch not the unclean thing; and I will receive you, and will be a Father unto you, and ye shall be My sons and daughters, says the Lord Almighty [p111LA] having therefore these promises, dearly beloved, let us cleanse ourselves from all filthiness of the flesh and spirit, perfecting holiness in the fear of God.[^122]

*Alleluia, Tone 4. Kinonikon: Praise... Gospel according to Mark [Mk. 8:34–9:1]:*

The Lord said: Whosoever will come after Me, let him deny himself, and take up his cross, and follow Me. For whosoever will save his life shall lose it; but whosoever shall lose his life for My sake and the Gospel's, the same shall save it. For what shall it profit a man, if he shall gain the whole world, and lose his own soul? Or what shall a man give in exchange for his soul? Whosoever therefore shall be ashamed of Me and of My words in this adulterous and sinful generation, of him also shall the Son of Man be ashamed, when He comes in the glory of His Father with the holy angels. And He said unto them: Truly, truly, I say unto you, that there be some of them that stand here, who shall not taste of death, till they have seen the kingdom of God come with power.

*Kinonikon: Praise the Lord from the heavens... (LXX Ps. 148:1).*

## SUNDAY EVENING VESPERS (TONE 4)

*On Sunday evening, Stichera of Repentance, Tone 4. Podoben: "Give us..." (Даждь нам):*

*Troparion:* I have sinned, O Lover of mankind, according to nature as man; grant me forgiveness, as Thou art beyond man and above nature: for having fallen beneath the laws of nature in mind, Thou didst accept to become man for our sake; possessing supreme love for mankind, have mercy on him that turns unto Thee!

*Troparion:* Thou hast set [p112LA] forth, O Pre-good Savior Christ, repentance for sinners and for the unrighteous: the parable of Judas, the thief and the prodigal, Manasseh and the harlot, the persecutor and the tax collector, and the publican; so that it is not easy to despair, beholding Thy pre-good love for mankind, unto those who turn: and I become of good hope, that Thou mayest come and have mercy![^123]

*Troparion:* Grant me compunction, and estrangement from evil, and perfect amendment, who am now sunk in bodily passions and far removed from Thee, O God, King of all, having no hope: save me, the prodigal and lost one, for the sake of Thy mercy, for Thou art Almighty God, and the Savior of our souls!

*Tone 4, Podoben: "As noble among the martyrs" (Ꙗко добля въ мученицѣхъ):*

*Troparion:* Receptive habitations of divine brightness and all-radiant vessels hast Thou made the angelic hosts and divine compositions, beholders and dwellers of Thy glory, O Immortal One, having established them in strength to accomplish Thy word and fulfilling Thine all-powerful and all-good desire.

*Troparion:* Willing to reveal the abyss of goodness, O Good and Unoriginate One, Thou didst first create by Thine almighty intellectual nod the angelic choirs, powers, and ranks; for truly it was fitting for the Good to pour forth and proceed, so that many might be recipients of Thy goodness, O Master.

*Troparion:* Six-winged Seraphim, many-eyed Cherubim, most exalted [p113LA] Thrones surround Thee, proclaiming in their midst the glory of Thine all-effecting strength: Dominions, Principalities, and Powers, Archangels and Angels, divine Powers, praising Thy glory, O Almighty, and praying unto Thee for us.[^124]

*Theotokion:* Transcending the angelic ranks, O all-immaculate one, ever pray together with the Angels unto Him Who rules over all creation, that He grant us remission of transgressions, and deliverance from passions, and make us then spiritually singers of His glory and heirs of incorruptible delight.

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of Repentance, Tone 4:*

*Sticheron:* I desired to wash away the handwriting of my transgressions with tears, O Lord, and for the rest of my life to please Thee through repentance; but the enemy deceives me and wars against my soul: O Lord, before I perish utterly, save me!

*Sticheron:* Who that is tempest-tossed and flees unto this haven is not saved? Or who that is sick and falls down before this medicine is not healed, O Creator of all and Physician of the ailing? O Lord, before I perish utterly, save me!

*Sticheron:* Glorifying and crying aloud of Thy passions, O Christ God, and moved to compunction by them: send down upon us Thy great mercy!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, cloud of light! Rejoice, golden lampstand! Rejoice, vessel that contained the manna! Rejoice, rod of Aaron! Rejoice, unburnt bush! Rejoice, bridal chamber! Rejoice, throne! Rejoice, holy mountain! Rejoice, refuge! Rejoice, divine table! Rejoice, mystical door! Rejoice, joy of all!

---

# TONE 5

## SATURDAY EVENING: VESPERS (TONE 5)

[p114LA] *On Saturday evening, at "Lord, I have cried," we set 8 stichoi, and sing the Stichera of the Resurrection, Tone 5:*[^125]

*Sticheron:* By Thy precious Cross, O Christ, Thou hast put the devil to shame; and by Thy Resurrection Thou hast blunted the sting of sin, and saved us from the gates of death: we glorify Thee, O Only-begotten One!

*Sticheron:* He that grants resurrection unto the human race was led as a sheep to the slaughter: the princes of Hades were terrified of Him, and the mournful gates were lifted up; for the King of glory, Christ, entered, saying unto the prisoners: Go forth! and unto them that sit in darkness: Be enlightened!

*Sticheron:* A great marvel! The invisible Creator, through love of mankind, having suffered in the flesh, is risen immortal! Come, families of the nations, let us worship Him; for being delivered from deception by His tender mercy, we have learned to sing of one God in three Persons!

*Other Stichera, Anatolian:*

*Sticheron:* An evening worship do we offer unto the never-setting Light, Who at the end of the ages, as in a glass, shone forth in the flesh upon the world, and descended even unto Hades, and dispelled the darkness there, and showed the light of the Resurrection unto the nations: O Giver of light, Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron:* Glorify Christ, the Author of our salvation; for when He rose from the dead, the world was saved from deception: the choir of Angels rejoices, demonic deception flees, fallen Adam [p115LA] arises, and the devil is brought to naught![^126]

*Sticheron:* Those of the guard were instructed by the lawless: Conceal the Resurrection of Christ; take silver and say: While we were asleep, the dead man was stolen from the tomb. Who ever saw or who ever heard of a dead man being stolen, especially one anointed and naked, leaving his grave clothes in the tomb? Be not deceived, O Judeans! Learn the prophetic proclamations and understand that He is truly the Redeemer of the world and Almighty!

*Sticheron:* O Lord Who didst lead Hades captive and abolish death, O our Savior, Who hast enlightened the world by Thy precious Cross: have mercy on us!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion [Dogmatikon]:* In the Red Sea the image of the unwedded Bride was inscribed of old: there Moses was the divider of the water; here Gabriel was the minister of the miracle: then Israel crossed the deep dryshod; now the Virgin hath given birth to Christ without seed. The sea after the passage of Israel remained impassable; the Immaculate One after the birth of Emmanuel remained incorrupt: O Thou Who art and Who wast before, and hast appeared as Man, O God, have mercy on us!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 5:*

*Sticheron:* Thee, Christ the Savior, Who wast incarnate and yet wast not parted from heaven, do we magnify in the voice of song: for Thou didst accept the Cross and death for our race, as the Lord Lover of mankind, overthrowing the gates of Hades and rising on the third day, saving our souls!

*Other Stichera, Alphabetical:*

*Sticheron:* From Thy pierced [p116LA] side, O Life-Giver, Thou didst pour forth a stream of forgiveness unto all, life and salvation; and accepting death in the flesh, Thou didst grant us immortality; and dwelling in the tomb, Thou hast freed us, and gloriously raised us up together with Thyself as God: therefore do we cry unto Thee: O Lord Lover of mankind, glory to Thee![^127]

*Sticheron:* Awesome is Thy Crucifixion and Thy descent into Hades, O Lover of mankind: for leading it captive, Thou didst gloriously raise up with Thyself the ancient prisoners as God, and opened Paradise, and accounted them worthy to receive it: therefore unto us also who glorify Thy third-day rising, grant cleansing of sins, and vouchsafe us to be dwellers of Paradise, as the only Compassionate One!

*Sticheron:* Having accepted death in the flesh for our sake, and risen from the dead on the third day: heal the passions of our flesh, and raise us up from grievous transgressions, O Lover of mankind, and save us!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Thou art the temple and the door, the palace and the throne of the King, O all-pure Virgin; through thee my Redeemer, Christ the Lord, appeared unto those sleeping in darkness, being the Sun of Righteousness, willing to enlighten those whom He fashioned in His image with His own hand: therefore, O all-hymned one, as thou hast motherly boldness toward Him, pray unceasingly that our souls may be saved!

*Troparion of the Resurrection, Tone 5:* Let us the faithful praise and worship the Word, co-unoriginate with the Father and the Spirit, Who was born of the Virgin for our salvation; for He was well pleased to ascend the Cross in the flesh, and to endure death, and to raise [p117LA] up the dead by His glorious Resurrection![^128]

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, impassable gate of the Lord! Rejoice, rampart and protection of those who flee unto thee! Rejoice, stormless haven of our salvation, who in the flesh didst bear Thy Creator and God! Cease not to intercede for those who sing of and worship in faith thy childbirth!

## SUNDAY MIDNIGHT OFFICE (TONE 5)

*Canon to the Holy and Life-Originating Trinity, Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos: The horse and the rider... (Коня и всадника...):*

*Troparion:* Singing of the dominion of the unitary and three-sunned splendor, we cry aloud: Enlighten our mind, O Almighty God, and raise it up, O Master, unto Thine ineffable glory!

*Troparion:* On high the Angels and transcendent intellects unceasingly glorify in three counsels the three-sunned Unity, identically fashioned, supersubstantial and almighty.

*Troparion:* Grant unto my soul the sweetest, light-giving divine drink of Thy love, O Trinity, all-radiant Unity, a divine cleansing compunction, O Master, the only mercy of all creation!

*Theotokion:* As dew upon the fleece came down from heaven, as rain into thy womb, without seed, O all-pure one, the Word without experience of man.

*Ode 3. Heirmos: Establish... (Утверди...):*

*Troparion:* Having established the noetic essences of intellects as unceasing singers of Thy Godhead, O all-radiant God-originating Unity: receive also the praise and supplication of the faithful born of earth, as Thou art merciful!

*Troparion:* Thou Who art naturally unreceptive to any addition, from the singers on earth receive praise, O unfathomable [p118LA] fountain of Thy goodness, and grant remission of sins, O Sun of Glory, Who art most pure![^129]

*Troparion:* Glorify the Father, and the Son, and the Spirit, in the boundless splendor of the Godhead, and Thee, the unitary, thrice-radiant God of all, even as the Prophets and Apostles clearly taught concerning Thee!

*Theotokion:* Thou hast appeared unto us, O Father, together with the Son and the Spirit, as the Angel of Great Counsel of the Almighty, manifesting Thy dispensation through the Virgin, O Word of God, through Whom Thou hast enlightened us!

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 5.*

*Podoben: "The Word co-unoriginate" (Собезнача́льное Сло́во):*

*Theotokion:* O Virgin Theotokos, thou hast given birth unto One of the Trinity, O most beautiful Paradise, salvation of the earthly: protect with thy covering those who piously sing of thee; for thou didst bear Him of Whom the Prophets spoke, for thou didst carry Him Who upholds all things!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Thy divine... (Божественное Твое...):*

*Troparion:* Knowing the thrice-radiant light of the one Lordship, Daniel beheld God the Judge upon the throne, the vision foreshadowing the Son and the Spirit.

*Troparion:* Let the choir of the faithful sing with their lips of the supersubstantial God in three Hypostases, yet unitary in essence, and make them worthy of angelic glory!

*Troparion:* One dominion, one Godhead in three Persons do I indivisibly glorify: O Father, Son, and Spirit, enlighten my wretched [p119LA] soul![^130]

*Theotokion:* Thee, the pure mountain which Habakkuk beheld of old, from whence the Holy One came forth, and who foresaw the conception of thy childbirth, O Virgin!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Clothed with light... (Одеяйся свѣтом...):*

*Troparion:* Thou Who in Thy goodness didst create man according to Thine image, make an abode in me, O my thrice-radiant God, as Thou art good and compassionate!

*Troparion:* Do Thou guide me, O three-sunned Unity, unto the paths of divine salvation, not of the flesh, which essence is in us of unnumbered strength.

*Troparion:* O indivisible Light, contemplation of the forms divided in essence, thrice-radiant and never-setting: enlighten my heart with light!

*Theotokion:* Even as the Prophet, beholding thee of old, O all-pure one, saw an unentered door facing toward the east, an uncomprehended dwelling!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: Surging... (Вълнящееся...):*

*Troparion:* The thrice-radiant essence of the God-originating Hypostases, shining unitarily, as co-fashioned and of equal power, according to essence and will.

*Troparion:* Sufficiently did the Prophet declare unto the morning light: beholding by the Spirit the one God of all, yet not a single Person.

*Troparion:* Having the unceasing power of essence in three Hypostases, one dominion and power, whereby every creature consists and is renewed.

*Theotokion:* Deliverance from transgressions and temptations, O unitary and thrice-radiant divine Power: send down Thy peace upon Thy singers through the prayers of the Mother of God!

*Sessional Hymns, Tone 5.*

*Podoben: "The Word co-unoriginate" (Собезнача́льное Сло́во):*

*Troparion:* Have mercy on me, O my Savior, have mercy on me [p120LA] and reprove me not in Thy wrath, O Merciful One, neither enter into judgment with Thy servant; for I know in truth my foul transgressions which surpass the sands: therefore crying unto Thee with sighs, have mercy on me![^131]

*Troparion:* Despairing, I have fled unto Thee, the hope of all and refuge of sinners; humbly I cry unto Thee: I have sinned! I abide in evil, unfeeling and wretched: have mercy on me, turn me back before the end, and deliver me from eternal torment!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: Highly exalted... (Превозносимый...):*

*Troparion:* Having the bottomless abyss of mercy, unfathomable, O Compassionate One, have mercy on him that sings unto Thee, the one thrice-radiant God, unto the ages!

*Troparion:* Mystically singing and honoring the incomprehensible, one-hypostatic and thrice-radiant God: grant unto Thy servants cleansing of transgressions!

*Theotokion:* Thou hast brought forth a branch, from the unoriginate Father the Flower of the Godhead, an ever-existing shoot, O Virgin, Who grants life unto all mankind!

*Theotokion:* Thou wast shown forth as the propitiation of nature, thou alone blessed one, who didst bear in thine arms God Who sits upon the shoulders of the Cherubim, Who grants life unto all mankind!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: Unto Thee, the Creator of all, the youth...*

*Troparion:* That Thou mightest reveal of old the mystery of the one Lordship, O my God, the Trinity of Persons appeared in human likeness unto Abraham, who sang of Thy dominion unto ages!

*Troparion:* Vouchsafe me to behold Thy beneficent rays, O [p121LA] unapproachable, O Father Almighty, Word, and Spirit, that I may ever be well-pleasing unto Thee, O Lord of all![^132]

*Troparion:* Thou hast shone forth the thrice-radiant glory, O all-hymned One, Christ and Lord, calling all to hymn the one Godhead in three Persons unto the ages.

*Theotokion:* The gold-gleaming lampstand foreshadowed thee, who didst receive the ineffable, unapproachable Light; with light thou enlightenest the earthly, Whom we highly exalt unto the ages.

*Ode 9. Heirmos: Rejoice, O Isaiah! For a Virgin hath conceived...*

*Troparion:* Earthly hymns cannot fittingly hymn Thee, O unoriginate Trinity; but as Thou art good and the Lover of mankind, since we dare by faith, O God-originating and co-enthroned Unity, we offer glory and praise unto Thy dominion!

*Troparion:* Equally honoring Thee, the Seraphim hymn Thee with most holy lips as the one-originate and thrice-radiant God; with them receive also us sinners, O Lord, who sing of and magnify Thy dominion!

*Troparion:* Look upon our debts, Thou Who in three God-originating Hypostases art the one true God of glory, and grant comfort unto Thy servants, through the prayers of the all-pure one and Mother of God, holy above all!

*Theotokion:* As pure and immaculate Virgin, thou hast given birth unto the Savior Who delivers us from corruption, the immutable God; but now also send down upon us, O all-pure one, remission of our transgressions!

*Then 4 [hymns] of the other [choir], and the rest as indicated in the first Tone.*

## SUNDAY MATINS (TONE 5)

*At "God is the Lord," Troparion, Tone 5: [written at Vespers]. After the first stichology:*

[p122LA] *Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:* Let us praise the Cross of the Lord, and honor His holy burial with hymns, and glorify His Resurrection; for as God He raised the dead from the tombs, and brought to naught the dominion of death and the strength of the devil, and shone light upon those in darkness![^133]

*Sessional Hymn:* O Lord, Thou wast called dead, Who didst slay death; and Thou wast laid in a tomb, Who didst empty the tombs; above, soldiers guarded the tomb, while below Thou didst raise up those who slept from the ages: O Almighty and Incomprehensible Lord, glory to Thee!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Who can declare thy greatness? For thou didst bear the Creator of all creation, Who suffered for our sake, and rose from the dead, and raised [mankind], O Pure One, from the corruption of deceitful passions: O ecclesial throne of the Lord, chosen earth, and overshadowed mountain, amendment of sinners!

*After the second stichology, Sessional Hymn, Tone 5:* O Lord, the lawless condemned nailed Thee in their midst, and with a spear they pierced Thy side, O Merciful One; and Thou didst accept burial, shattering the gates of Hades; and Thou didst arise on the third day. The women ran to see Thee, and having seen Thee, they proclaimed the Resurrection unto the Apostles: O highly exalted Savior, Whom the Angels hymn, blessed art Thou, O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sessional Hymn:* O Lord, on the third day of Thy Resurrection, at the veneration of the Apostles, Peter cried aloud unto Thee: The women showed boldness, but I was afraid; the thief confessed Thee, but I denied Thee: wilt Thou call [p123LA] me henceforth Thy disciple, or wilt Thou make me once more a fisher of the deep? But receive me in repentance, O Christ God, and have mercy on me![^134]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O unwedded Mother, pure Theotokos, who didst turn Eve's sorrow into joy, we the faithful sing of and worship thee, for thou hast led us up from the ancient curse; and now pray unceasingly, O all-pure one, that we may be saved!

*Hypakoe, Tone 5:* Perplexed in mind by the angelic vision, yet enlightened in soul by the divine Resurrection, the Myrrh-bearers brought good tidings unto the Apostles: Proclaim the Resurrection among the nations, as the Lord works together with miracles, and grants unto us great mercy!

*Hymns of the Degrees, Tone 5. Antiphon 1. Verse:* When I am afflicted, David-like do I sing unto Thee, O my Savior: deliver my soul from a deceitful tongue!

*Verse: Blessed is the life of those who dwell in the wilderness, who are borne aloft by divine desire!*

*Glory... Both now...:* By the Holy Spirit all things visible and invisible are upheld; for being truly Sovereign, He is truly One of the Trinity.

*Antiphon 2. Verse:* Arise unto the mountains, O my soul; go thither, whence thy help shall come!

*Verse: May Thy right hand, O Christ, outstretched unto me, preserve me from all deception!*

*Glory... Both now...:* Theologizing, let us say unto the Holy Spirit: Thou art God, life, desire, light, and intellect; Thou art goodness, Thou reignest unto the ages!

*Antiphon 3:*

[p124LA] *Verse: Over those who said unto me: Let us go into the courts of the Lord, I was filled with great joy, sending up prayers!* [^135]

*Verse: In the house of David fearful things are accomplished: for there is a fire burning every shameful intellect.*

*Glory... Both now...:* By the Holy Spirit is the life-originating dignity, from Whom every living creature is inspirited, as from the Father and together with the Word.

*Prokeimenon, Tone 5: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up (LXX Ps. 9:33). Verse: I will confess Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart (LXX Ps. 9:2). Let every breath... The Matins Gospel: The Resurrection of Christ... Psalm 50: Have mercy on me, O God...*

*Canon of the Resurrection, Tone 5. Ode 1. Heirmos: The horse and the riders... (Коня и всадники...):*

*Heirmos:* The horse and the riders in the Red Sea, He Who shatters wars with a high arm, Christ shook off, and saved Israel, as they sang a triumphal hymn.

*Troparion:* Thee, O Christ, the thorn-bearing synagogue of the Hebrews, having preserved no love toward Thee, the Benefactor, crowned with thorns as Ancestor, Who didst annul the penalty of thorns!

*Troparion:* Thou didst raise me who had fallen into the pit, bowing down, O Life-Giver; having sought me out who was foul-smelling, and having endured the stench without experiencing sin, O Christ, Thou hast perfumed me with divine myrrh!

*Theotokion:* The curse is dissolved, and sorrow hath ceased: for the blessed and joyfully graced one hath shone forth joy and blessing unto the faithful, bearing Christ, the Flower, unto the whole universe!

*Another Canon of the Cross. Heirmos: Unto the Savior... (Спасителю...):*

*Troparion:* Nailed in the flesh unto the Cross of Thine own will, Thou hast freed by the wood him that had fallen through the ancient denial: let us sing unto Him alone, for He is glorified!

*Troparion:* Nailed in the flesh upon the Cross, by the wood [p125LA] having destroyed the dominion of death, and descended into Hades, Thou art risen from the dead, O God, and hast reconciled the world in peace![^136]

*Theotokion:* O all-pure Mother of God, unceasingly pray unto Him Who was incarnate of thee, and Who was not parted from the bosom of the Father, to deliver our souls from every assault!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: The same (Спасителю...):*

*Troparion:* The Light dwelt in thee, O all-pure one, and enlightened the world with the splendors of the Godhead: pray unto Christ to enlighten all who praise thee, O Virgin Mother!

*Troparion:* As thou wast adorned with the beauty of virtues, O rejoiced one, working good beauty, thou didst receive spiritual radiance, O all-pure one, [bearing] Him Who adorned all things!

*Troparion:* The Sinaitic bush foreshadowed thee of old, remaining unconsumed, O Theotokos, when touched by fire: for as a Virgin thou didst give birth, and as a Virgin thou didst remain, beyond comprehension, O Bride of God!

*Ode 3. Heirmos: Establishing the earth upon nothing... (Утверждей землю ни на чемже...):*

*Heirmos:* Thou Who didst establish the earth upon nothing by Thy command, and suspended its uncontainable weight: on the immovable rock of Thy commandments, O Christ, establish Thy Church, O only Good One and Lover of mankind!

*Troparion:* Gall they sought [to give Thee], they who had sucked [honey from the rock] in the wilderness for Him Who poured it forth; they brought Thee vinegar, O Christ, instead of manna; such thankfulness did the senseless children of Israel render unto Thee!

*Troparion:* Those who of old were covered by the luminous cloud laid Christ the Life in the tomb; but rising by Thine own sovereign power, Thou didst grant unto all the faithful the spiritual radiance mystically overshadowing from above!

*Theotokion:* Thou, O Mother of God, without [p126LA] wedlock didst become [Mother] unto Him Who shone forth from the incorrupt Father, and without maternal pangs: therefore do we orthodoxly proclaim thee Theotokos, for thou didst bear the incarnate Word![^137]

*Another Canon of the Cross. Heirmos: By the power of the Cross... (Силою Креста...):*

*Troparion:* Risen from the tomb, O Christ, and delivering from the corruption of death: we hymn Thy third-day Resurrection, O Life-Giver!

*Troparion:* To anoint Thy body, O Christ, the Myrrh-bearers came unto Thy tomb, and finding it not, they returned with joy, glorifying Thy rising!

*Theotokion:* Pray unceasingly, O Pure One, unto Him Who came forth from thy loins, to deliver from the deceit of the devil those who praise thee as the Mother of God!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: The same (Силою Креста...):*

*Troparion:* The ladder whereby the Most High came down unto us to restore corrupted nature, He visibly showed thee naturally unto all: for through thee the Pre-good One was well pleased to unite Himself unto the world!

*Troparion:* The mystery commanded of old by the Spirit, foreseen before the ages by all-seeing God, in these last of ages received fulfillment in thy womb, O all-pure one, and was manifested!

*Troparion:* The ancient condemnation of the curse was dissolved through thy mediation, O pure Virgin; for from thee God appeared, pouring forth blessing unto all as the Pre-good One!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Perceiving Thy divine condescension with prophetic insight, O...*

*Heirmos:* Perceiving Thy divine dispensation, marveling, Habakkuk cried aloud unto Thee with trembling, O Christ: Thou art come for the salvation of Thy people, to save Thy servants!

*Troparion:* The bitter waters of Marah [p127LA] as foreshadowing the image of Thy most pure Cross, O Lord, putting to death the condemnation of sin, Thou didst sweeten by the wood![^138]

*Troparion:* The Cross instead of the tree of knowledge, gall instead of food, the soldiers brought unto Thee, O Christ, while Thou didst pour out Thy divine blood, incorruptible in death!

*Theotokion:* Without wedlock thou didst conceive, incorruptibly receiving in the womb, and thou didst bear God in the flesh, and after childbirth thou wast preserved a Virgin!

*Another Canon of the Cross. Heirmos: I heard, [O Lord,] the report... (Услышах слух...):*

*Troparion:* When Thy Cross was planted upon Calvary, O Christ, then the gates of Hades were shattered, and the eternal gatekeepers cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion:* When Thou didst descend, O Christ, unto the bound as dead, Thou didst raise up with Thyself the dead from the ages, and they cried aloud: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Theotokion:* O Virgin, thou didst give birth, and knewest not man, but wast known as the Mother of God, and remainedst a Virgin, as we praise thee: Rejoice, in hymns, O Theotokos!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: The same (Услышах слух...):*

*Troparion:* With heart and mind, with soul and lips do we faithfully confess thee truly to be the Theotokos, O Pure One, receiving unfeigned salvation, O Virgin, through thy prayers!

*Troparion:* He Who created all things out of nothingness was clothed in flesh from thee, as the Benefactor was well pleased, for the salvation of those who sing of thee with faith and love, O all-pure one!

*Troparion:* Praising thy childbirth, O all-hymned one, the transcendent choirs rejoice in hymnody, beholding thee as the Theotokos nurturing an infant, O ecclesial Virgin!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Clothed with light as with a garment, unto Thee do I rise early and cry: Enlighten my darkened soul, O Christ, as Thou art merciful! (Одеяйся свѣтом яко ризою, к Тебѣ утренюю и к Тебѣ вопїю: душу мою просвѣти омраченную, Христе, яко Милостивъ!):*

[p128LA] *Troparion:* The glorious God, in marvelous form, hangs willingly upon the tree, stripped of all honor, providing for me ineffable divine glory![^139]

*Troparion:* Thou hast shown forth ever-shining incorruption, O Christ, having incorruptibly tasted mortal corruption in the flesh, rising from the tomb on the third day!

*Theotokion:* Thou who didst bear Christ without seed as salvation and righteousness unto us, freest ancestral dominion from the curse!

*Another Canon of the Cross. Heirmos: Rising early... (Утренюю...):*

*Troparion:* By Thy three-day burial Thou didst overthrow Hades, and manifestly didst raise all, as a merciful Lord!

*Troparion:* By Thy Cross, O Passionless One, Thou hast abolished death, O Christ God, and slain our sins!

*Theotokion:* Cease not to pray for us unto Him Whom thou didst bear, O Theotokos, that He may save the souls of us who praise thee, O Holy One!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: The same (Утренюю...):*

*Troparion:* All the Prophets proclaimed beforehand concerning thee, that thou shouldest be the Mother of God, O all-pure Theotokos; for thou alone wast holy and seen to be immaculate!

*Troparion:* A luminous cloud, raining down living water unto us, having rained down the incorruptible Christ, known by unhoped-for tokens, O Pure One!

*Troparion:* As undefiled in immaculate beauty, He purely loved thy virginity to remain incorrupt, He Who dwelt within thee as the only Good and Compassionate One!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: Surging with the soul-destroying tempest, O Master Christ, calm the sea of passions and lead me up from corruption, as Thou art merciful! (Волнящееся бурею душепагубною, Владыко Христе, похотное море укроти, и от тли возведи мя, яко Милостивъ!):*

*Troparion:* Into corruption fell the forefather, O Master Christ, having tasted of the forbidden food, and was raised unto life through Thy Passion [^140]

*Troparion:* As Life Thou didst draw near unto Hades, O Master Christ, and becoming incorruptible unto the corruptible, Thou didst grant Resurrection unto all!

*Theotokion:* As a Virgin thou didst give birth, and after childbirth thou remainedst Pure; and in thine arms, O true Virgin-Mother, thou didst carry Him Who upholds all things!

*Another Canon of the Cross. Heirmos: The abyss encompassed me... (Обыде мя бездна...):*

*Troparion:* Thou didst stretch out Thy palms upon the Cross, gathering the scattered nations, O Christ our God, by Thy life-bearing Cross and Resurrection!

*Troparion:* Thou didst overthrow death, and shattered the gates of Hades, and released Adam who was bound; and they cried aloud: Thy right hand hath saved me, O Lord!

*Theotokion:* The unburnt bush, the spiritual mountain and ladder, and the heavenly gate, do we worthily glorify, O glorious Mary, praise of the Orthodox!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: The same (Обыде мя бездна...):*

*Troparion:* The Cause of all things, Who granted existence unto all, accepted a cause by being incarnate for our sake, O immaculate Mother of God!

*Troparion:* Pouring forth a soul-nourishing fountain of healing, O Sovereign Lady, unto those who come in faith beneath thy glorious shelter, we brightly glorify thee!

*Troparion:* Thou hast given birth unto the Cause of salvation, the Life-Giver, granting eternal deliverance unto those who confess thee in truth to be the Theotokos!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: Highly exalted Lord of the fathers, Who quenched the flame and refreshed the children, who sang in one voice: Blessed art Thou, O God! (Превозносимый отцев Господь, пламень угасивый, отроки прохладивый, единогласно поющыя: Боже, благословенъ еси!):*

*Troparion:* Clothed in the flesh, Thou didst shatter by divine power upon the fishhook [of Thy flesh] the snares of the devil; Thou hast raised up those who cry unto Thee: Blessed art Thou!

[p130LA] *Troparion:* United in one Person, the Unapproachable One is concealed in the flesh within the tomb, unsearchable, unto Whom we sing: Blessed art Thou, O God![^141]

*Theotokion:* O all-immaculate one, thou didst give birth unto the incarnate God, one [Person] in two natures, unto Whom we all sing: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Another Canon of the Cross. Heirmos: He Who in the fiery furnace... (Иже в пещи огненнѣй...):*

*Troparion:* By the wood of the Cross Thou didst tear down the deceit of idols: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Troparion:* Risen from the tomb, having raised the dead from Hades: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Theotokion:* As we all possess thee as salvation, O Theotokos, pray unto Him Who was incarnate of thee to save us!

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos: The same (Иже в пещи огненнѣй...):*

*Troparion:* Remaining immaculate and unchanged according to fleshly constitution, yet changed in condescension as merciful toward thee, the Holy One, the only blessed God of our fathers!

*Troparion:* As an immaculate bride, O Theotokos and Sovereign Lady, do we harmoniously glorify thee, the throne and dwelling of thy Master, Whom we all hymn!

*Troparion:* Mother of the King of all, sanctified by the Spirit, O Virgin, didst thou become unto Him Who created thee, unto Whom we all sing: Blessed art Thou, O God!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: Thee, the Creator of all, the children in the furnace, forming a universal choir, did sing: Hymn the Lord, all ye works, and highly exalt Him unto the ages! (Тебѣ, Вседѣтелю, в пещи отроцы, всемирный ликъ составльше, пояху: Дѣла вся, Господа пойте и превозносите Его во вѣки!):*

*Troparion:* Thou didst endure voluntary saving suffering; Thou didst pray concerning the cup, bearing according to intellect two wills and two operations, O Christ, in two natures: Hymn the Lord, all ye works!

*Troparion:* At Thine all-effecting descent, O Christ, Hades was mocked, and spewed forth the dead whom of old it had swallowed up through deceit, who sing and highly exalt Christ unto the ages!

*Troparion:* Thee, beyond comprehension [p131LA] who didst bear the Word in God-manly manner, being a Virgin: Hymn the Lord, all ye works, and highly exalt Him unto the ages![^142]

*Another Canon of the Cross. Heirmos: Him Who before the ages of the Father... (Отца прежде вѣк...):*

*Troparion:* Nailed upon the Cross of Thine own will, O Christ, stretching out Thy palms, and destroying the dominion of death: Ye priests, hymn, and highly exalt Him!

*Troparion:* Him Who descended into Hades and abolished death, O Christ, and rose in glory, and raised the dead: Ye priests, hymn, and highly exalt Him!

*Theotokion:* Higher than the Cherubim wast thou shown to be, O holy one, and in thine arms thou didst carry Christ: Ye priests, hymn and highly exalt Him!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: The same (Отца прежде вѣк...):*

*Troparion:* With us the bodiless host also sings, O Virgin, of thine incomprehensible childbirth, forming a united choir in love, highly exalting thee!

*Troparion:* Bright water of the immortal Spirit, coming forth from thee, wash away the filth of long ages for those who sing and highly exalt in faith!

*Troparion:* The living in truth and light-bearing throne and tables of grace confess thee, O Virgin, who didst receive the Word from thee; highly exalt Him!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: Rejoice, O Isaiah: a Virgin has conceived in the womb, and given birth unto a Son, Emmanuel, both God and man; Orient is His name; Whom magnifying, with the One Who gave birth we bless thee! (Исаие, ликуй, Дѣва имѣ во утробѣ, и породи Сына Еммануила, Бога же и человѣка, Восток имя Ему, Егоже величающе, с Рождшею тя ублажаем!):*

*Troparion:* Thou didst take up fallen man, O Master Christ, from the virgin womb, as Thou wast united unto the whole [human nature], yet taking no part in any sin; and me entirely hast Thou freed from passions by Thy most pure passions!

*Troparion:* Thy God-flowing blood, poured forth, O Master Christ, from Thy most pure and life-creating ribs, a sacrifice for [p132LA] the destruction of idols; and the whole earth was filled with Thy praise![^143]

*Theotokion:* Not an incorporeal God, nor again a mere man, did the pure and immaculate one bear thee, but a complete man and truly perfect God, manifesting two natures!

*Another Canon of the Cross. Heirmos: Thee, beyond comprehension... (Тя паче ума...):*

*Troparion:* Thou didst accept passions in the flesh, and having delivered the whole world from deceit, O Merciful One, with unceasing hymns we magnify Thee!

*Troparion:* Thee, Who didst rise from the tomb on the third day, and cast down Hades, and enlighten the world, we the faithful unanimously magnify!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, O Virgin, Mother of Christ God, Whom thou didst bear; pray unto Him to grant remission of sins unto those who praise thee in faith!

*Another Canon to the Theotokos. Heirmos: The same (Тя паче ума...):*

*Troparion:* From thy pure blood was curdled the supernatural flesh for the Creator of all and for the only-begotten Son of His Father, not changing what He was, but taking an animate and ensouled nature, O Theotokos!

*Troparion:* Thou didst halt the circuitous, mortal, and restrained onset, having in truth given birth in the flesh, beyond comprehension, unto eternal Life, which when bitter Hades touched, it was shattered, O most holy Virgin Mother!

*Troparion:* Sitting upon the throne, Thy Son, the Master, placed thee at His right hand in glory, O pure one, shining with golden fringes and divine virtues, granting, O most blessed one, as Mother, boldness before the Lover of mankind!

*Then the Exapostilarion. At the Praises, Stichera of the Resurrection, Tone 5:*

[p133LA] *Sticheron 1:* O Lord, while the tomb was sealed by the lawless, Thou didst come forth from the tomb, even as Thou wast born of the Theotokos; Thy bodiless Angels understood not how Thou wast incarnate; the soldiers guarding Thee perceived not when Thou didst arise; for both were sealed unto those who enquire, and the wonders were revealed unto those who worship the mystery in faith; which we hymn: render also unto us joy and great mercy![^144]

*Sticheron 2:* O Lord, having shattered the eternal bars and broken the bonds asunder, Thou didst arise from the tomb, leaving behind Thy burial linens as a witness of Thy true and three-day burial; and Thou didst go before into Galilee, Thou Who wast guarded in a cave: Great is Thy mercy, O incomprehensible Savior, have mercy and save us!

*Sticheron 3:* O Lord, the women ran unto the tomb to see Thee, Christ, Who didst suffer for our sake; and having arrived, they found an Angel sitting upon the stone, rolled away through fear; and he cried aloud unto them, saying: The Lord is risen; say unto His disciples that He is risen from the dead, saving our souls!

*Sticheron 4:* O Lord, even as Thou didst come forth from the sealed tomb, so didst Thou enter while the doors were shut unto Thy disciples, showing them the bodily passions which Thou didst accept, O longsuffering Savior; as from the seed of David Thou didst endure wounds; as the Son of God Thou didst free the world: Great is Thy mercy, O incomprehensible Savior, have mercy and save us!

[p134LA] *Other Stichera, Anatolian:*

*Sticheron 5:* O Lord, King of the ages and Creator of all, having accepted crucifixion and burial for our sake, that Thou mightest free us from death: Thou art our God, for beside Thee we know no other![^145]

*Sticheron 6:* O Lord, who shall declare Thy shining wonders? Or who shall proclaim Thy fearful mysteries? For having become incarnate for our sake as Thou Thyself didst will, Thou didst manifest the strength of Thy power; for by Thy Cross Thou didst open Paradise unto the thief, and by Thy burial Thou didst destroy the strongholds of the devil, and by Thy Resurrection Thou hast enlightened all: O Lover of mankind, glory to Thee!

*Verse: Arise, O Lord my God, let Thy hand be lifted up; forget not Thy poor unto the end.*

*Sticheron 7:* Unto the God-receiving tomb of Thine, the guards cried aloud: O your senseless counsel! Attempting to guard the Uncircumscribed, ye labor in vain to conceal the Resurrection of the Crucified, Who wills to appear in radiance! O your vainly-counseling council! Why do ye take counsel again to hide what cannot be hidden? Rather hear from us and believe in the truth of what has come to pass: an Angel lightning-flashing came down from heaven and rolled away the stone; seized by fear and dread of him, he cried aloud with strength unto the myrrh-bearing women, saying: Behold ye not the guards slain, and the seals broken, and Hades emptied, and the sting of death shattered? Why seek ye the Living as dead? Go quickly, proclaim the good news of the Resurrection unto the Apostles, crying aloud in joy: Truly [p135LA] the Lord is risen, having great and rich mercy![^146]

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*

*Sticheron 8:* The Myrrh-bearing women, having come unto Thy tomb very early, sought to anoint Thee, the immortal Word and God; and having been instructed by the Angel's words, they returned in joy and openly proclaimed unto the Apostles that the Life of all is risen, granting unto the world cleansing and great mercy!

*Glory... Sticheron of the Gospel of the Sunday. At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 5:*

*Troparion 1:* The thief on the cross believed Thee to be God; he confessed Thee truly in his heart, calling aloud: Remember me, O Lord, in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion 2:* When Thou wast crucified, O Christ, between two condemned thieves, the one blaspheming Thee was condemned, O Lord, while the other theologizing Thee was settled in Paradise: Remember us, O Savior, in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion 3:* From the wood of the Cross the life-source gushed forth unto the human race, and having shattered the ancient curse: let us hymn Christ as Savior and Benefactor of us!

*Troparion 4:* Risen from the dead, and having destroyed the dominion of death, O Christ, and raised Adam with Thyself: Remember me, O Lord, when Thou comest in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion 5:* Unto the Myrrh-bearing women, O Christ, Who didst rise from the tomb, when they came unto Thy tomb held by sorrow, and found unspeakable joy...

*Troparion 6:* Unto Thy tomb, O Christ, came the venerable women, seeking to anoint Thee, the Life-Giver; and an Angel appeared [p136LA] crying: The Lord is risen! Remember me, O Lord![^147]

*To the Trinity:* We worship Thee, O Trinity, unoriginate Unity, honored equality; we glorify Thee with the supernal powers: Holy art Thou, O unoriginate Trinity, our God in Unity: Remember us!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, animate Church of God and impassable gate; rejoice, unburnt bush and fiery throne; rejoice, Mother of the true Christ our God!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 5: Thou, O Lord, shalt keep us and shalt preserve us from this generation and unto the ages (LXX Ps. 11:8). Verse: Save me, O Lord, for the godly man has ceased (LXX Ps. 11:2).*

*Epistle [Rubric: To the Hebrews; Witness: Ephesians 4:7 & 1 Corinthians 12:7–11]:* Brethren, unto each one of us grace is given according to the measure of the gift of Christ. But the manifestation of the Spirit is given to profit withal: for to one is given by the Spirit the word of wisdom, and after this the word of knowledge by the same Spirit; to another faith by the same Spirit; to another the gifts of healing by the same Spirit; to another the working of powers and power; to another prophecy; to another kinds of tongues. But all these things the one and same Spirit works, dividing unto each one as He wills.

*Alleluia, Tone 5: Thy mercies, O Lord, will I sing unto the ages (LXX Ps. 88:2). Verse: For He said: Mercy shall be built up unto the ages (LXX Ps. 88:3).*

*Gospel [Rubric: Gospel according to John; Witness: Matthew 10:1, 5–6]:* At that time, Jesus called His twelve disciples, and gave them power over unclean spirits, to cast them out, and to heal every infirmity and every disease among the people. These twelve Jesus sent forth, saying: Go not into the way of the Gentiles, and enter not into a city of the Samaritans; but go rather unto the lost sheep of the house of Israel; [p137LA] and as ye go, preach, saying: The Kingdom of heaven is at hand! Heal the sick, raise the dead, cleanse the lepers; freely ye have received, freely give![^148]

*End. Praises.*

## SUNDAY EVENING VESPERS (TONE 5)

*On Sunday Evening, Stichera at "Lord, I have cried," Penitential, Tone 5, Prosomoia:*

*Sticheron 1:* Possessing fountains of tender mercy, and pouring forth an abyss of compassions and torrents of bounty: O most good Father, Word and Son, and Holy Spirit, uncreated Nature: receive our supplication and prayer, granting forgiveness unto all who are in transgressions, as Thou art a compassionate God and Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 2:* Thou possessest by nature as God a heart of bounties and a treasury of goodness; therefore we pray Thee, O Christ our Savior, and falling down we ever call aloud, crying: Grant unto Thy servants remission of debts and transgressions, and forgiveness of all things wherein we have all sinned, as Thou art a compassionate God and Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 3:* Desiring to save all as God, for whose sake Thou didst appear in human form as man: save us who worship Thy commandments; for Thou camest not, O Lover of mankind, to save the righteous, but those bound in transgressions; and us, who are shackled by many sins, do Thou release by the grace of divine baptism, as Thou art a compassionate God and Lover of mankind!

*Other Stichera to the Bodiless Powers.*

*Podoben: "The same" (то́йже):*

*Sticheron 4:* Enlightened by the radiant rays of Light, the ranks of the Bodiless [p138LA] hymn the Master of all, the All-Bountiful, Who made all things out of non-existence by wisdom, the Archangels with the Angels: by their prayers, as the only All-Good God, send peace unto all the world, and great mercy unto our souls![^149]

*Sticheron 5:* Ever beholding the face of God, enlightened by Him with thrice-radiant beams: enlighten us, and deliver us from the passions and temptations of the ages, O holy Archangels: by their prayers, O God, grant peace unto all the world, and great mercy unto our souls!

*Sticheron 6:* The Seraphim and Cherubim, and all Angels and Archangels, Thrones, Principalities, Dominions, and Powers unceasingly glorify the Divine Trinity, knowing one Lordship, with immaterial lips: pray ye unto Christ to grant peace unto all the world, and great mercy unto our souls!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Fearful and most glorious is the great mystery: the Uncontainable was contained within thy womb, and as Mother after childbirth thou remainedst still a Virgin; for thou didst bear God Who came down and was incarnate for our sake! Unto Him let us cry aloud, unto Him let us sing a hymn, chanting with the Angels: Holy art Thou, O Christ God, Who wast made man for our sake; O Lord, glory to Thee!

*At the Aposticha, Penitential Stichera, Tone 5:*

*Sticheron 1:* O Lord, sinning I cease not; made worthy of Thy love for mankind, I understand not: overcome my darkening, O only Blessed One, and have mercy on me!

*Sticheron 2:* O Lord, I fear Thy dread judgment, yet doing evil I cease not; who at the judgment-seat [p139LA] fears not the judge? Or who that desires healing provokes the physician, as I do, O longsuffering Lord? Have compassion upon the weakness of my soul, and have mercy on me![^150]

*Martyrikon:* Disregarding the things of the earthly age, and manfully daring unto tortures of your own will: O blessed ones, ye missed not your hope, but became inheritors of the heavenly Kingdom, O all-praised Martyrs! Having boldness unto the merciful God, ask peace for all the world, and great mercy for our souls!

*Theotokion:* O rejoice-filled Mediatress: by thy prayers, without ceasing, ask a multitude of bounties and cleansing from our many stumbling-blocks!

---

# TONE 6

## SATURDAY EVENING: VESPERS (TONE 6)

*On Saturday Evening, at "Lord, I have cried," set 8 stichoi, and sing the Resurrection Stichera, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1:* Having the victory over Hades, O Christ, Thou didst ascend upon the Cross, that Thou mightest raise with Thyself those who sit in darkness and the shadow of death: free among the dead, and pouring forth life from Thine own light: O Almighty Savior, have mercy on us!

*Sticheron 2:* Today Christ, having trampled down death, is risen even as He said, and has granted rejoicing unto all the world, that all, crying aloud, may send up a hymn: O Fountain of life, Unapproachable Light, Almighty Savior, have mercy on us!

*Sticheron 3:* Whither shall we sinners flee from Thee, O Lord, Who art throughout all creation? In heaven, there Thou Thyself dwellest; in Hades, Thou didst trample down death; into the depths of the sea [p140LA] there also is Thy hand, O Master! Unto Thee do we flee, and falling down before Thee, we pray, O Thou Who didst rise from the dead: O Savior, have mercy on us![^151]

*Anatolian Stichera:*

*Sticheron 4:* In Thy Cross, O Christ, do we boast, and Thy Resurrection we hymn and glorify; for Thou art our God, for beside Thee we know no other!

*Sticheron 5:* Glory to Thy power, O Lord, for Thou didst destroy the dominion of him that held the power of death; Thou hast renewed us by Thy Cross, and granted unto us life and incorruption!

*Sticheron 6:* Truly blessing the Lord, we sing of His Resurrection; for He endured crucifixion, and by death He destroyed death!

*Sticheron 7:* Thy burial, O Christ, having shattered the bonds of Hades, loosed them; and Thy Resurrection from the dead enlightened the whole world: O Lover of mankind, glory to Thee!

*At the Entrance, Dogmatikon Theotokion, Tone 6:* Who blesses thee not, O most holy Virgin? Or who sings not of thy pure childbirth? For the timeless only-begotten Son, Who shone forth from the Father, the Same came forth from thee, O pure one, having ineffably become incarnate: being God by nature, and having become man by nature for our sake; not divided into two Persons, but known in two natures without confusion: Him entreat, O pure and most blessed one, that our souls may have mercy!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1:* Thy Resurrection, O Christ Savior, the Angels hymn in the heavens: make us also worthy upon earth with pure heart to hymn and glorify Thee!

*Verse: The Lord is King, He is robed in majesty.*

*Sticheron 2:* The gates of Hades Thou didst shatter, O Lord, and the bars of Hades [p141LA] having shattered [them] as God all-powerful, the human race that was fallen[^152] Thou hast raised up by Thy Resurrection. Therefore we, singing, cry aloud: O Thou Who didst rise from the dead, Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron 3:* Desiring to correct the corruption of ancient ruin, Christ was nailed upon the Cross and laid in the tomb. Him the myrrh-bearing women sought with tears, and weeping they said: "Woe to us, O Savior, King of all! How didst Thou deign to dwell in a tomb? And having dwelt there of Thine Own will, how wast Thou stolen away? What manner of place hid Thy life-bearing body? Yea, appear, even as Thou didst promise us, appear unto us, and stay our tearful weeping!" But while they wept, an Angel cried aloud unto them: "Cease from weeping! Go, tell the Apostles that Christ is risen, granting resurrection and great mercy unto the world!"

*Theotokion:* My Creator and Deliverer, O pure one, Christ the Lord, coming forth from thy womb, having clothed Himself in me, freed Adam from the ancient curse. Therefore unto thee, O most pure one, as truly the Mother of God and a Virgin, we cry aloud unceasingly: Rejoice, as the Angel [said]! Rejoice, O most pure Protectress, and Shelter, and Salvation of our souls!

*Troparion, Tone 6:* The angelic hosts were upon Thy tomb, and the guards became as dead; and Mary stood at the tomb, seeking Thy most pure Body. Thou didst overthrow Hades, not being tempted of it; Thou didst meet the Virgin, granting life. O Thou Who didst rise from the dead, Lord, glory to Thee! *(Twice)*

*Glory... Both now...*

[p142LA] *Theotokion:* O Thou Who didst call Thy Mother blessed, Thou camest to Thy Passion by Thine Own will, ascending upon the Cross and shining forth upon the Cross, desiring to seek out Adam, saying unto the Angels: "Rejoice together with Me, for the lost drachma is found!" O Thou Who hast ordered all things in wisdom, Lord, glory to Thee!

## SUNDAY MIDNIGHT OFFICE (TONE 6)

*Canon to the Holy and Life-Originating Trinity, Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:* As Israel walked on dry land...

*Troparion:* Let us hymn the thrice-hypostatic Godhead of the unified Deity, the unchangeable Image: O good God, Lover of mankind,[^153] grant unto us cleansing of our transgressions!

*Troparion:* O supersubstantial One, both Single and Thrice-radiant, being in unity of form: enlighten us who are unworthy [to partake] of Thy divine illumination![^154]

*Troparion:* Paul, who adorned the Church of the Gentiles as a bride, taught [her] to worship Thee, the one Thrice-hypostatic God, from Whom and through Whom and in Whom all things came to be.

*Theotokion:* From thy womb came forth the Noetic Sun, O Mother of God, and shone upon us the thrice-radiant ray of the Godhead; singing of Whom, we piously call thee blessed.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* There is none holy like Thee, O Lord my God...

*Troparion:* O Thrice-radiant One, having adorned the heavenly ranks, Thou hast ordained that they sing to Thee with thrice-holy voices: together with them receive us also, as we sing of Thy goodness!

*Troparion:* Hymning the one immutable, equal Triune Unity, O Principle of Godhead, we fervently entreat Thee: send down upon us forgiveness of our many transgressions!

[p143LA] *Troparion:* Timeless Mind, O Father; co-equal divine Word; and Spirit divine, good, and right: as in faith we hymn Thy dominion, preserve us, as Thou art merciful!

*Theotokion:* Having [delivered our] pasture from corruption, and having become man by nature, O my God, Thou didst dwell within the womb of the most pure one, and didst deliver [us] from the ancient first condemnation of our forefather.

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6.*

*Podoben: "The Angelic Powers" (А҆́гг҃льскія си́лы):* [^155]

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O pure Virgin, look down from heaven and behold the grievous wounds of our passions, and have compassion, O most pure one, and heal [our] hidden infirmity, distilling thy mercy upon thy servants: if thou art with us, none shall be against us!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Christ is my power...

*Troparion:* Elevating our thoughts, O Thrice-radiant Unity, quickly lift up the soul and heart of Thy singers, and make them worthy of Thine illumination and brightness!

*Troparion:* Change and transform [us] from the evil of the passions unto virtue, O one immutable and unchangeable Trinity, enlightening us with Thy rays!

*Troparion:* In Thy pre-eternal counsel Thou didst establish the angelic ranks as ministers of Thy goodness, O Thrice-hypostatic Unity: together with them receive also our praise!

*Theotokion:* The uncreated divine Nature, co-eternal, having assumed created human nature [p144LA] in thy womb, O Mother of God, Ever-Virgin!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* By Thy divine light, O Good One...

*Troparion:* Having recognized the Principle of Godhead to be by nature providential and saving for all, O Master, Thrice-radiant yet One: unto Thee do we rise early, asking forgiveness of transgressions!

*Troparion:* O Father, with the unoriginate Son, and co-eternal Son, [and] Holy Spirit: establish, O Monarchical Trinity, Thy singers [free] from every temptation and affliction!

*Troparion:* Ordering Thy beneficent providence, guiding us ever unto the pleasing of Thy Thrice-hypostatic Godhead, make us partakers of the heavenly Kingdom!

*Theotokion:* O unchangeable Word of God, Who upholdest and preservest all things by Thine almighty hand: preserve and protect us who magnify Thee, by the prayers of the Mother of God who bore Thee!

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Beholding the sea of life...

*Troparion:* Grant wisdom and understanding unto Thy singers, O Thrice-radiant Principle of Godhead, and vouchsafe that the rays of the beauty and light-giving brightness of Thy goodness may illumine us all!

*Troparion:* O indivisible Light, thrice-radiant by nature, almighty and unapproachable: illumine the hearts of those who faithfully sing of Thy dominion, and give them wings unto divine love!

*Troparion:* One Light in three Persons, in Father, Son, and Holy Spirit: Three indivisibly, praised as three-in-one, and known as worshipped as One!

*Theotokion:* He Who dwelt within thee, O Ever-Virgin, as the Almighty [p145LA] taught men to worship [the one Thrice-radiant Godhead]!

*Sessional Hymn, Tone 6.*

*Podoben: "Fulfilling the dispensation for our sake" (Е҆́же ѡ҆ на́съ):*

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* O Virgin, look upon us, O all-hymned Mother of God; enlighten with light our darkened hearts, preserve thy flock, O most pure one; for as much as thou wilt, thou art able, as being the Mother of thy Creator, for those who call upon thee and love thee: if thou art with us, none shall be against us!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The angel made the furnace produce dew...

*Troparion:* Grant me manly understanding, O Unity of thrice-radiant form, to keep and preserve Thy divine commandments, ever to sing unto Thee faithfully: Blessed is the God of our fathers!

*Troparion:* Praised as one by nature, and God unutterable by nature, yet bearing a threefold number in Persons: preserve us all from diverse temptations!
