*Ode 3. Heirmos: The Affirmation...* Thee, the unapproachable God and King of glory, high upon the throne the heavenly [powers] beheld, Whom Cherubim and Seraphim glorify with unceasing hymns, the One Principle and Three-Hypostatic!

*Troparion:* The Word, Who from the Father alone was born as from Mind, and the Spirit proceeding incomprehensibly by all human thoughts: receiving through scriptural teachings, we revere the One God, Thrice-radiant!

*Troparion:* He Who is, the unbegotten Father, having timelessly begotten of His own Essence the Son, the Savior, Light from Light, brings forth the co-originating Light and Spirit, all-effecting and equal in honor!

*Theotokion:* Thou didst appear as the Church, O pure Virgin, Mother of the King of all, Who omnipotently and most wisely ordered and established the habitations; Whom entreat for me with thy maternal boldness!

*Sessional Hymn to the Trinity, Tone 8:*[^214] The power of the Thrice-radiant and thrice-shining unoriginate Principle let all us faithful hymn, and with one desire let all the angelic heavenly choirs and earthly ecclesiastical ranks assemble to cry: "Holy, Holy, Holy art Thou, O most good God: glory and praise unto Thy dominion!"

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Having given birth unto the ineffable and changeless God, Who ever delivers from sins and falls [p193LA] grievous, receive from Him change of sorrow, O good one, and by thy morning prayers [deliver me], that now with thanksgiving I may cry unto thee, O Mary, Mother of God, according to the mercy which thou hast obtained, O most immaculate one!

*Ode 4. Heirmos: Thou art Christ God, Thou art...*[^215]

*Troparion:* The Dawns of the Godhead Thou didst manifest unto those in darkness, brightly dispersing all the soul's night, and the Sun of righteousness shone forth: simple indeed in Essence, but thrice-radiant in Persons, Whom we hymn in everlasting glory!

*Troparion:* Him Who is hymned by the mouths of the Seraphim, we faithful also glorify with our lips: the one Lord of glory, singing unto Thee in unity and Hypostases; O Compassionate One unto all, grant unto Thy servants, O only Good One, remission of sins!

*Troparion:* All-holding, all-knowing, invisible Essence, most bountiful, compassionate, and long-suffering, honorable and God-originating Trinity: forget not me, Thy servant, unto the end, nor annul the covenant which Thou didst establish with Thy servants, for the sake of Thine ineffable mercy!

*Theotokion:* Fair alone did the all-effecting and God-originating Word find thee from the ages, loving thy beauty, and dwelt within thee, renewing all human nature: Whom entreat unceasingly to deliver us from every torment!

*Ode 5. Heirmos: Why hast Thou cast me off...*[^216]

*Troparion:* The co-eternal Three Persons of glory, the divine Essence dividing and uniting Thee as the One God, we faithfully cry: O One-Principle Holy Trinity, deliver Thy servants [p194LA] from every affliction and sorrow!

*Troparion:* I weep for the weakness of my mind, how against my will I suffer the change of lust; therefore I cry unto Thee: O Holy God-originating Trinity, direct me unto good by Thy mercy!

*Troparion:* Weighed down with the slumber of sin, and cast down unto death by sinful sleep; but as the Lover of mankind, most good and all-merciful, O Holy God-originating Trinity, have compassion and have mercy!

*Theotokion:* O Mother of God, Maiden Virgin most pure and immaculate, graced of God: by thy prayers entreat thy Son and God, O all-honorable one, swiftly to deliver thy servants now from passions!

*Ode 6. Heirmos: Cleanse me, O Savior...*[^217]

*Troparion:* Imitating the ranks of heavenly minds, O all-ruling Trinity, supersubstantial unto all, we glorify Thee with thrice-holy hymns with our faithful lips!

*Troparion:* Worshipping and honoring the Three-Hypostatic [Godhead], Who created man according to His own image and brought him into being out of nothingness as was spoken of old, I sing and magnify!

*Troparion:* Almighty God, One and unapproachable, dwell within me according to Thine ineffable mercy, O Thrice-radiant Master: enlighten me and give me understanding, as God and compassionate!

*Theotokion:* Having become a pure temple unto God, show me forth as a temple of divine grace, O most pure Lady; preserve me unharmed by thy prayers!

*Kontakion, Tone 8, Podoben (*подобенъ*):*[^218] Confessing in truth, ye faithful, the Father without beginning, and the Son without beginning, and the Divine Spirit: indivisible [p195LA] uncircumscribed, and ineffable, O simple, holy, and divine Trinity, singing unto Thee with the Angels: "Holy art Thou, O Father and Son, with the Holy Spirit in Persons! Have mercy upon those whom Thou didst create according to Thine image, O Master!"

*Theotokion:* We ever give thanks unto God for thee, we magnify [thee] faithfully and worship, unceasingly hymning thy childbirth, O thou full of grace; we unceasingly hymn thee, saved, O all-merciful Virgin: that by God [thou mayest deliver us] from the snatching of demons and from the trial of the dread Day of Judgment, that we thy servants may not be put to shame!

*Ode 7. Heirmos: The condescension of God...*[^219]

*Troparion:* By Thine ineffable wisdom, show forth the order of goodness, that Thy servant may freely be shown mercy; look upon my prayer, and ever free and deliver [me], O Trinity-Unity God, granting deliverance from sins and passions!

*Troparion:* Father, the unbegotten Mind, and the Word born of Him, and Holy Spirit, being co-ruling: we hymn Thee as Thrice-radiant!

*Troparion:* Unoriginate Trinity, indivisible in Essence, enthroned together in unity, inseparate in glory, primordial Essence and Kingdom: save those who believe in Thee!

*Theotokion:* Fallen into mortification through sins, O most pure one, I flee unto thee with faith, O Most Pure, Originator of life: by thy prayers make thy servant alive, and deliver from all temptations and passions!

*Ode 8. Heirmos: Sevenfold...*[^220]

*Troparion:* Being the unwaning Light, thrice-shining and thrice-radiant, One Principle, One Dominion [p196LA] simple, incomprehensible God: enlighten now my dark-figured heart, make it radiant and light-bearing to hymn Thee!

*Troparion:* With sacred divine wings the Seraphim cover their faces in godly fear, not enduring the glory of the thrice-radiant beauty of the God-originating, co-ruling and God-beginning Thrice-Holy Trinity; yet we also, daring, hymn and glorify unto ages!

*Troparion:* Lordly Origin without beginning, all-powerful and all-effecting goodness, and all-perfect Beginning, deifying and unceasing, innocent Cause, creative, intentional, provident, and saving unto all: we glorify the Unity in Essence, the Trinity, unto ages!

*Theotokion:* The unwaning Sun shone upon earth, for Whose sake, from thy virginal birth, O most pure Lady, He delivered mankind from idolatrous darkness: therefore enlighten with light and preserve thy servants who hymn thee!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: Thee, unwedded...*[^221]

*Troparion:* O King of glory and All-Holder of all, primordial Essence, from the beginning and Life-originating, Who wast well-pleased to bring [us] forth from non-being, compassionate, loving mankind: we sinners now glorify Thee, the unoriginate Trinity, asking remission, profound humility, and oneness of mind for the Churches!

*Troparion:* O Single Lordship thrice-shining, single God-Head in Three Persons: accept Thy singers in goodly beauty, and deliver us from transgressions and grievous temptations [p197LA] grant humility, and unto the Churches goodly order and assembly!

*Troparion:* Distinguishing Thee in Persons of Hypostasis, Thrice-Holy, in one Dominion we ever worship; all things standing by Thy word of command: grant forgiveness of sins, that we may worthily glorify Thee!

*Theotokion:* In the womb of the pure Virgin, O Christ, Thou didst make Thy dwelling; Thou wast manifestly shown forth unto all as God and Man, without change, without division, in truth: O God, ever protect Thy servants to be [Thee] unto ages, by the prayers of her that bore Thee; grant peace and humility unto all the world!

*Triadika Hymns, Tone 4: In truth... and the rest:*[^222]

## SUNDAY MATINS (TONE 8)

*At Matins, at "God is the Lord", the Resurrection Troparion; repeat it.*

*After the First Stichologia, Sessional Hymns:*[^223]

*Stanza 1:* Wonderfully was Thy tomb sealed, and an Angel rolled away the stone from the doors; and the women, having beheld Thee risen from the dead, announced the good tidings unto Thy disciples in Sion: that the Life of all is risen, and has broken the bonds of death: Lord, glory to Thee!

*Stanza 2:* With ineffable brightness appearing unto the Myrrh-bearing women, the Angel, arrayed in radiant garments of joy, spoke the word: "The sad dominion of death is destroyed, the joy of incorruption has shone forth, and having risen from the dead, the Savior has saved the human race!"

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Let us hymn the heavenly gate, and ark, the most holy mountain, radiant cloud, the unburnt bush, the rational paradise, and the deliverance of Eve, the great hope of the whole universe [p198LA] for salvation for the world was wrought in her, and remission of ancestral transgressions; therefore unto her [we cry]: O Mother of Christ God, grant remission of transgressions, for we thy servants have thee now as our Protectress!

*After the Second Stichologia, Sessional Hymns, Tone 8:*

*Stanza 1:* Risen from the dead art Thou, the Life of all, and a radiant Angel said unto the women: "Cease from tears and announce the good tidings unto the Apostles; cry aloud, singing: Christ the Lord is risen, Who was well-pleased as God to save the human race!"

*Stanza 2:* Unto that [sepulchre] the women brought burial [myrrh], and they heard the angelic voice from the tomb: "Cease from tears, receive joy, cry aloud, singing: Christ is risen, Who was well-pleased as God to save the human race!"

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:*[^224] All creation rejoices in thee, O thou full of grace: the angelic assembly and the human race; hallowed temple and rational paradise, virginal boast, of whom God was incarnate and became a child, [yet] changeless, our God Who exists before the ages: for He made thy womb a throne, and made thy womb more spacious than the heavens; all creation rejoices in thee, O thou full of grace: glory to thee!

*Hypakoe:* The Myrrh-bearing women, standing at the tomb of the Life-Giver, sought the Master among the dead, the Immortal One; and receiving the joy of the good tidings from the Angel, they announced the good tidings unto the Apostles: Christ is risen, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Hymns of Degrees, Tone 8. Antiphon 1:*[^225]

*Stanza 1:* From my youth the enemy tempts me and inflames me with pleasures [p199LA] but trusting in Thee, O Lord, I vanquish that one!

*Stanza 2:* Ye that hate Sion, let them become as the grass before it is plucked up; for Christ shall cut down their necks with the cutting of torment!

*Glory... Both now...*

*To the Holy Spirit:* To live ever: Light of Light, great God; with the Father and with the Word let us hymn Him!

*Antiphon 2:*

*Stanza 1:* When I call, O Lord, give ear, and incline Thine ear unto me crying, and cleanse me before Thou takest me away hence!

*Stanza 2:* Unto his mother [the earth] [turning], everyone having hope shall not be terrified then, when Thou shalt judge all by fire and torment!

*Glory... Both now...*

*To the Holy Spirit:* Theology of the Thrice-Holy Unity: the Father without beginning, from Whom was born the timeless Son, and the Spirit co-enthroned, identical in form, shining forth with the Son from the Father!

*Antiphon 3:*

*Stanza 1:* Let my heart be covered with Thy fear, being humble-minded, lest being lifted up it fall away from Thee, O All-Compassionate One!

*Stanza 2:* Everyone entering into earth as into his mother shall immediately depart thither, to receive either torments or honors for how he lived!

*Glory... Both now...*

*To the Holy Spirit:* Everyone who divinely beholds and speaks forth higher wonders, singing of the One God in Trinity; for even if Thrice-shining, He is of one authority in the Godhead!

*Antiphon 4:*

*Stanza 1:* Behold now, what is good or what is pleasant, but for brethren to dwell together? For concerning this the Lord promised life eternal!

*Stanza 2:* Concerning His raiment, He Who clothes the lilies of the field commands the world to take no thought for anything!

*Glory... Both now...*

*To the Holy Spirit:* In Whom abiding not everyone has, Giver of peace [p200LA] for He is consubstantial with God the Father and the Son!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 8: Arise, O Lord, into Thy rest (LXX Ps. 131:8).*[^226] *Verse: Remember David, O Lord, and all his meekness (LXX Ps. 131:1).*

*Let every breath praise the Lord. Verse: Praise God in His saints (LXX Ps. 150:1).*

*Resurrection Gospel, Reading 1.*

*Having beheld the Resurrection of Christ...*

*Psalm 50. Have mercy upon me, O God... and Refrain.*

*And the Prayer: "Save, O God, Thy people..." and the rest.*

*Canon of the Resurrection, Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:*[^227] The weapon-bearing Pharaoh did the wonderworking rod of Moses of old submerge, striking crosswise and dividing the sea, and saving fleeing Israel, walking on foot, sending up a hymn unto God!

*Troparion:* How shall we not marvel at the almighty Godhead of Christ? For from the passion He pours forth dispassion and incorruption unto all, and from His holy rib a fountain dripping immortality, and life from the tomb, co-essential!

*Troparion:* As in goodly beauty the Angel now appeared unto the women, bearing the radiant immaterial forms, showing forth the immaterial order in appearance, crying: "Light has risen, the Lord is risen!"

*Theotokion:* Most glorious things were spoken concerning thee throughout generations of generations, who didst receive the Word of God in thy womb, yet remaining pure, O Theotokos Mary: therefore we glorify thee, our true protectress after God!

*Another Heirmos: Having traversed the water as upon dry land...*[^228]

*Troparion:* The painful gates were lifted up, and the gatekeepers of Hades were terrified, beholding the Most High of all nature [descending] unto the nethermost parts!

*Troparion:* The angelic ranks marveled, beholding upon the throne [p201LA] *(Lacuna: Physical leaf p201 is missing from this surviving printed exemplar (archival binder's notice: "ЭТОГО ЛИСТА В КНИГЕ НЕТ").)*[^229] [p202LA] [Lord,] Thou art the life of earthly ones; [the creation] hymns Thee in spirit!

*Troparion:* Thou art the resurrection and rejoicing unto us all, and Thee do we glorify unceasingly, O Plenteous-in-mercy!

*Theotokion:* Thee all we Christians have as our refuge and wall, and thee do we glorify unceasingly, O Unwedded One!

*Another, to the Theotokos, Heirmos, of the same Tone:*

*Troparion:* Grant us help by thy prayers, all-venerable one, repelling the onslaught of bitter griefs!

*Troparion:* Thou wast the restoration of Eve our foremother, giving birth to Christ the Author of life, O all-spotless Theotokos!

*Theotokion:* Gird me with power, O all-pure one, who didst give birth from the womb in the flesh to the true God, the hypostatic Power!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^230] Thou art my Lord, O Christ, Thou art also my power, Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing, Who leaving not the Father's bosom, hast visited our poverty in mercy; therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind!

*Troparion:* Loving me that was hostile, Thou in marvelous condescension didst come down to earth, O Savior,[^231] not rejecting the reproach of my extreme poverty; and remaining in the height of Thine all-pure glory, Thou didst glorify him that was dishonored before!

*Troparion:* Who, beholding Thee, O Master, marvels not: death dissolved by Thy passion, and corruption by the Cross, and through death the wealth of Hades emptied by Thy goodly power? I hymn the deed of Thee Who wast crucified, O Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion:* Thou art the boast of the faithful, O unwedded Virgin; thou art our protection and shelter; thou art the refuge of Christians, a wall and a ha [p203LA] ven; for unto thy Son thou dost bear prayers, O spotless one, saving from perils those who with faith and love know thee as pure Theotokos!

*Another Canon, of the Cross. Heirmos: I have heard...*[^232]

*Troparion:* The lawless children nailed Thee to the Cross, O Christ, whereby, O Merciful Savior, Thou didst save those who glorify Thy marvelous deeds!

*Troparion:* Having risen from the tomb, Thou didst raise those condemned in the tombs, who glorify Thy rising, O only Almighty Lifegiver!

*Theotokion:* Pray to thy strong God, O all-pure one, to save His flock from affliction and sorrows, and from every harm of the adversary!

*Another, to the Theotokos. Heirmos: Having brought forth the lifegiving ear of corn, O untilled field, which gives life to the world, O Theotokos, save those who praise thee!*

*Troparion:* All proclaim thee, all-pure Theotokos, as enlightenment; for thou, ever-Virgin, hast given birth to the Sun of Righteousness!

*Theotokion:* Grant us cleansing of our ignorances, as Sinless One, God of all the world, by the prayers of her who gave birth to Thee!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^233] Why hast Thou cast me away from Thy face, O Never-setting Light, and an alien darkness has covered me, the wretched one? But turn me unto the light of Thy commandments, and direct my ways, I pray Thee!

*Troparion:* Enduring to be clothed in the purple robe of reproach, covering the ill-formed blessedness of the first-fashioned, Thou wast nailed naked upon the Cross, O Christ, stripping off the garment of mortality!

*Troparion:* Him of dust who had fallen by death, Thou didst raise again, [clothing our] nature in incorruption [p204LA] renewing once more the churchly image, shining forth life in incorruption!

*Theotokion:* Having maternal boldness toward thy Son, O all-pure one, despise not our race, we pray thee; for thee alone do we offer unto the Master as prayerful propitiation!

*Another Canon, of the Cross. Heirmos: Enlighten us by Thy commandments...*[^234]

*Troparion:* Guide us by the power of Thy Cross, O Lord, Lover of mankind; for unto Thee do we fall down: grant us Thy peace, O only Plenteous-in-mercy!

*Troparion:* O Helmsman of our life, O Christ our God, unto those who praise Thy rising grant also Thy peace, O only Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion:* Pray to thy Son and our God, O unwedded Bride, pure Mother, to send down great mercy upon the faithful!

*Another, to the Theotokos, of the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion:* Quench the uncontrollable surging of my passions, for thou didst give birth to the Helmsman and Lord!

*Troparion:* The angelic ranks and gatherings of men serve thy childbearing, O pure Mother of God!

*Theotokion:* O Mary, Bride of God, destroy the armies of the enemy, and make those who hymn thee to rejoice!

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^235] Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities, and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray Thee; for unto Thee have I cried, and hear me, O God of my salvation!

*Troparion:* By a tree did the ancient evil one mightily cast me down; but Thou Thyself, being hanged, O Christ, didst mightily cast him down by the Cross, and didst raise up the fallen, [created in Thine] image!

*Troparion:* Thou hadst compassion on Sion, shining forth from the tomb, a new [p205LA] covenant fulfilling in prayer by divine Blood; and Thou art sacrificed therein unto all ages!

*Theotokion:* May we be delivered through thee from bitter transgressions, prayer of mercy, O pure Theotokos; and may we obtain, O all-pure one, divine illumination through the Son of God ineffably incarnate of thee!

*Another Canon, of the Cross. Heirmos: I will pour out my prayer unto the Lord...*[^236]

*Troparion:* Stretching out Thy palms upon the Cross, Thou didst heal the unrestrainedly outstretched hand of the first-fashioned in Eden; and of Thine own will Thou didst taste gall, O Christ, and didst save from corruption, as Good One, those who praise Thee, O Lord!

*Troparion:* The Deliverer tasted death of the first condemnation, that He might destroy the kingdom of corruption; and having descended into Hades, O Christ Savior, save as Mighty One those who hymn Thy resurrection!

*Theotokion:* Cease not to pray for us, O all-pure Theotokos Virgin, for thou art the confirmation of the faithful, and by hope in thee are we strengthened; therefore do we glorify God Who was incarnate of thee!

*Another, to the Theotokos. Heirmos: Grant me a robe...*

*Troparion:* The faithful proclaim thee as the Church and Ark of God, the ensouled bridal chamber, and the Heavenly Gate, O Theotokos!

*Troparion:* Having become the Destroyer of pagan shrines as God, He that was born of thee, O Mary, Bride of God, is worshipped with the Father and the Spirit!

*Theotokion:* The Word of God showed thee, O Virgin, unto men as Queen of barren virgins; for through thee the Most High came down unto us, as Protectress!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^237] At God's condescension the fire was ashamed in Babylon of old; therefore the Children in the furnace, dancing with joyful foot [p206LA] as in light, sang: Blessed [art Thou, O God of our Fathers]!

*Troparion:* At the glorious condescension of the divine wealth, at Thy poverty, O Christ, the angelic ranks are amazed, beholding Thee nailed upon the Cross, that Thou mightest save those crying in faith: Blessed is the God of our Fathers!

*Troparion:* By Thy divine descent the nethermost parts were filled with light, and the darkness that before pursued was driven away; therefore the captives from the ages cried unto the Risen One: Blessed [art Thou]!

*Troparion:* Proclaiming orthodoxly from the Father the Lord of all, the Only-begotten Son from the One Father, we have beheld the one upright Spirit proceeding from Thee, co-natural and co-eternal!

*Another Canon, of the Cross. Heirmos: He Who from the Father...*[^238]

*Troparion:* Spreading out upon the Tree like a fruitful vine, O Master, pouring forth the wine of immortality, O Christ, gladdening the hearts of those crying in spirit: Blessed art Thou, O God of our Fathers!

*Troparion:* Having risen from the tomb as from sleep, O Compassionate One, and having destroyed death, all creation believed as the Apostles proclaimed the resurrection: Blessed art Thou, O God of our Fathers!

*Theotokion:* Incarnate from the virginal womb, Thou didst appear for our salvation; therefore, entering by Thy Trinity, we cry orthodoxly to the Theotokos: Blessed art Thou, O God of our Fathers!

*Another, to the Most Holy Theotokos, of the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion:* Incarnate of the virginal womb, Thou didst appear for our salvation; therefore, knowing Thy Mother to be the Theotokos, we cry thankfully: [Blessed art Thou], O God of our Fathers!

*Theotokion:* A rod didst thou appear, O Virgin, from the root of Jesse, [bearing] the all-divine, light-bearing, saving Fruit, unto the wife of faith [p207LA] crying unto Thine: Blessed art Thou, O God of our Fathers!

*Theotokion:* The hypostatic Wisdom of the Most High filled all the divine powers with wisdom, for the sake of the Theotokos, of those crying in faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our Fathers!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*[^239] Sevenfold did the Chaldean tyrant fiercely heat the furnace for the pious children; but beholding them saved by a better power, he cried unto the Creator and Deliverer: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt Christ unto the ages!

*Troparion:* The co-essential Godhead, the all-divine power, has divinely shone upon us; for having manifestly tasted death in the flesh, by the Crucifixion He destroyed the strength of Hades; Whom we unceasingly hymn: Ye children, bless; ye priests, hymn; ye people, supremely exalt!

*Troparion:* The Crucified is risen; the boastful one is fallen; the fallen and broken one is restored, and darkness is driven away, and incorruption has blossomed forth; for by life death was swallowed up: Ye children, bless...

*Troparion:* The Thrice-radiant Godhead, shining in one ray from the one three-hypostatic nature: the unoriginate Father, the Word consubstantial with the Father, and the reigning consubstantial Spirit, we hymn: Ye children, bless; ye priests...

*Another Canon, of the Cross. Heirmos: Unto the Victor...*[^240]

*Troparion:* Having stretched out His hands upon the Tree, naked, calling [all] to be warmed by His noble nakedness: Bless and supremely exalt...

*Troparion:* Having honored Him Who raised fallen Adam from the nethermost parts unto high-enthroned glory: Bless, all ye works...

[p208LA] *Theotokion:* Thou didst appear, O Virgin, as daughter of fallen Adam; for thou art the divine [mother] of Him Who renewed our nature, Whom we hymn: Bless...

*Another, to the Most Holy Theotokos. Heirmos: The King of Heaven, Whom...*

*Troparion:* Quench the assault of the adversaries and the fiery burning arrows, that we may hymn thee, pure one, unto the ages!

*Troparion:* Beyond nature hast thou given birth to the Creator and Savior, God the Word, O Virgin; therefore do we hymn thee, pure one, unto the ages!

*Theotokion:* With radiant splendor, He Who is unapproachable Light, dwelling within thee, O Virgin, showed thee forth as light unto all ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:*[^241] Thee, unwedded Mother of God the Most High; thee, who beyond mind didst give birth by the Word to the truly existing God, higher than the all-pure Hosts, in unceasing doxologies do we magnify!

*Troparion:* Thee, Who didst raise up fallen Adam, glorifying him with Thyself in the heavens, O Christ, as King of all and Lord, in unceasing hymns do we magnify!

*Troparion:* Thee, God crucified for our sake of Thine own will, Who didst abolish the dominion of death, O Christ, and didst deliver [us] from the bonds of Hades, in unceasing blessings do we magnify!

*Theotokion:* Thee, who didst bear in thine arms the invisible God, Who is hymned on high by the Hosts, and through thee ever granting us salvation in the resurrection, do we magnify!

*Another Canon, of the Cross. Heirmos: In truth [Theotokos]...*[^242]

*Troparion:* The Tree of Thy Cross was revealed, O Christ, upon which stretching out Thy divine palms, Thou didst plant righteousness in the world, O Christ!

*Troparion:* Descending of Thine own will with Thy Cross into the nethermost parts of the earth, O Merciful God, for Thy tender mercy's sake Thou hast magnified [us]...

[p209LA] *Theotokion:* Receptacle of Light, O blessed of God, for the sake of the Radiance enlighten my heart, driving away the cloud of despondency, and chasing away the darkness of sin, O thou who didst give birth to the Sinless One!

*Another, to the Theotokos, of the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion:* Thy memory fills with joy and gladness those who draw near, pouring forth healings, who piously confess thee as Theotokos!

*Troparion:* With hymns do we praise thee, O highly favored of God, and unceasingly offer: "Rejoice!", for thou hast poured forth joy upon the world!

*Theotokion:* Comely did He become through thy flesh, O Theotokos, all-pure and incorrupt, Mediator of life unto those who hold fast, who magnify thee in faith!

*Holy is the Lord our God (LXX Ps. 98:9). Evangelical Exapostilarion, Reading 1, Resurrectional.*

*At the Praises, Stichera of the Resurrection, Tone 8:*[^243]

*Sticheron 1:* Lord, even though Thou didst stand at the judgment seat, judged by Pilate, yet Thou didst not withdraw from the throne, sitting with the Father; and having risen from the dead, Thou didst free the world from the servitude of the stranger, as only Compassionate One and Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 2:* Lord, Thou hast given us Thy Cross as a weapon against the devil; for he trembles and shakes, not able to look upon its power; for it raises the dead and has abolished death: therefore we worship Thy burial and Thy resurrection!

*Sticheron 3:* Lord, even though the Jews laid Thee in the tomb as dead, yet soldiers guarded Thee as a sleeping king; and as the treasury of life they sealed it with a seal: and Thou didst rise and grant incorruption unto our souls!

*Sticheron 4:* Lord, when Thou wast nailed upon the Cross and didst stretch out Thy hands [p210LA] then didst Thou abolish the dominion of death by Thy strength, and having descended into Hades didst free the souls, co-raising and enlightening our souls by Thy resurrection!

*Anatolian Stichera:*[^244]

*Sticheron 5:* Thou didst suffer on the Cross, being impassible in Thy Godhead; Thou didst receive burial for three days, that Thou mightest free us from the servitude of the enemy, and having made us immortal, make us alive, O Christ, through Thy resurrection, O Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 6:* I worship and supremely glorify, and hymn, O Christ, Thy rising from the tomb, whereby Thou hast freed us from the insoluble bonds of Hades, and hast granted unto the world, as God, incorruption and eternal life, as Good One and Lover of mankind!

*Verse: Arise, O Lord, help us, and redeem us for Thy name's sake (LXX Ps. 43:26).*

*Sticheron 7:* Him Who is risen from the dead let us glorify: God the Word, Who appeared dwelling in Himself upon earth, for our sake received reproach from the lawless and blows on the cheek; Whose kingdom He destroyed as Almighty God: therefore He was nailed to the Cross in the flesh, for our sake as Man, and opened Paradise unto the thief as Creator of all, Deliverer and Savior of our souls!

*Verse: I will give thanks unto Thee, O Lord, with my whole heart; I will speak of all Thy marvelous works.*

*Sticheron 8:* Being the incomprehensible Creator of heaven and earth, Thou didst suffer upon the Cross, that Thou mightest pour forth impassibility unto me, as God; Thou didst receive three-day burial, and didst rise in glory, co-raising Adam as Almighty!

*Then the Evangelical Sticheron, Reading 1. Both now... Most blessed art thou (Theotokion).*

## SUNDAY LITURGY: THE BEATITUDES (TONE 8)

*At the Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 8: Remember us, O Lord, when Thou comest into Thy kingdom...*

[p211LA] *Troparion:* O Christ, Savior of the world, as Thou didst remember the thief upon the Tree, vouchsafe also unto me, O only Compassionate One, Thy heavenly kingdom!

*Troparion:* Nailed of Thine own will upon the Tree, O our Savior, by the Tree Thou hast delivered Adam from the curse, restoring as Compassionate One that which was in His image unto the habitation of Paradise![^245]

*Troparion:* Today, having risen from the tomb, Thou didst pour forth incorruption unto all the dead, and granting joy unto the myrrh-bearing women, Thou didst rise as Merciful!

*Troparion:* Today Thou hast abolished the dominion of death by Thy power, O Giver of life, and hast freed the souls from the dead, [O Thou Who art the] Resurrection, our only Savior!

*Troparion:* As in heaven the multitude of Angels, so also on earth the human race celebrates Thy most holy Resurrection, [praising] Thy goodness!

*Troparion:* Rejoice, ye wise myrrh-bearing women, who first beheld the Resurrection of Christ and announced joy unto the Apostles and the Resurrection unto the whole world!

*Glory:* Trinity without beginning and indivisible Essence, co-enthroned Unity, unseparated in glory, transcendent Nature: save those who believe in Thee!

*Both now:* Thee in truth, O Virgin Mother of God, let all the faithful with one accord hymn, as strong champion and intercessor, and the salvation of our souls!

*Prokeimenon [Tone 8: Pray, and give glory unto the Lord our God (LXX Ps. 75:12). Verse: Known in Judea is God; in Israel His name is great (LXX Ps. 75:2)].*[^246]

*Epistle to the Corinthians [1 Cor. 1:10–18]:*[^247] Brethren, I beseech you by the name of our Lord Jesus Christ, that ye all speak the same thing, and that there be no divisions among you; but that ye be perfectly joined together in the same mind and in the same judgment. For it has been declared [p212LA] unto me of you, my brethren, by them of the household of Chloe, that there are contentions among you. Now this I say, that every one of you says: "I am of Paul," and "I of Apollos," and "I of Cephas," and "I of Christ." Is Christ divided? Was Paul crucified for you? Or were ye baptized in the name of Paul? I thank my God that I baptized none of you, but Crispus and Gaius, lest anyone should say that I baptized in mine own name. And I baptized also the household of Stephanas; besides, I know not whether I baptized any other. For Christ sent me not to baptize, but to preach the Gospel: not in wisdom of words, lest the Cross of Christ should be made of none effect. For the word of the Cross is to them that perish foolishness; but unto us who are saved it is the power of God.

*Alleluia: Come, let us rejoice in the Lord (LXX Ps. 94:1). Verse: Let us come before His presence with thanksgiving (LXX Ps. 94:2).*[^248]

*Gospel according to John [John 15:17–16:2]:*[^249] The Lord said unto His disciples: These things I command you, that ye love one another. If the world hate you, know that it hated Me before it hated you. If ye were of the world, the world would love its own; but because ye are not of the world, but I have chosen you out of the world, therefore the world hates you. Remember the word that I said unto you: The servant is not greater than his lord. If they have persecuted Me, they will also persecute you; if they have kept My word, they will keep yours also. But all these things will they do unto you for My name's sake, because they know not Him Who sent Me. If I had not come and spoken unto them, they would not have had sin; but now they have no excuse for their sin. He that hates Me hates My Father also. If [p213LA] I had not done among them the works which none other man did, they would not have had sin; but now have they both seen and hated both Me and My Father. But this comes to pass, that the word might be fulfilled that is written in their law: "They hated Me without a cause." But when the Comforter is come, Whom I will send unto you from the Father, even the Spirit of truth, Who proceeds from the Father, He shall testify of Me; and ye also shall bear witness, because ye have been with Me from the beginning. These things have I spoken unto you, that ye should not be offended. They shall put you out of the synagogues; yea, the time comes, that whosoever kills you will think that he offers service unto God.

---

# WEEKDAY OFFICES OF THE OCTOECHOS (MONDAY THROUGH SATURDAY)

*On Sunday Evening, Stichera to our Lord, of Repentance, Tone 8, Podoben: "Lord, even though at the judgment seat" (Господи аще на судищи):*[^250]

*Sticheron 1:* O good Shepherd, Who didst lay down Thy life for the sheep, Who alone art good, knowing the hidden things and deeds wrought by me: shepherd me wisely away from the wandering one, and snatch me from the wolf, O Lamb of God, and have mercy on me!

*Sticheron 2:* Weighed down by frightful phantasms through the deceit of sin, grant me also the morning day of repentance, enlightening the eyes of my mind, O Christ, the Enlightenment of our souls, and save me!

*Sticheron 3:* Entangled in the gloom of sin and worldly pleasures, alas, O my wretched soul! And various passions hold sway, and I have no thoughts of compunction. But, O Savior, have compassion on my weakness, and give me tears of compunction, that I also may bewail myself before the end; I cry unto Thy tender mercy, O Lord: O Christ, my Savior, save me who am in despair, and [p214LA] [deliver me from the enemy who destroys] souls!

*Other Stichera to the Angels, Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "The same" (то́йже):*

*Sticheron 1:* Lord, the bodiless choirs unceasingly glorify Thee, delighting in Thy beauty, Thine ineffable goodness; with thrice-radiant rays they most richly praise Thee: by whose prayers, O Savior, save our souls!

*Sticheron 2:* Lord, Thou hast established the all-encompassing ranks of Angels, O all-powerful Word, granting them a habitation of unwaning radiance through the all-holy Spirit: by whose prayers preserve the blessings of the Church!

*Sticheron 3:* Lord, the Seraphim, Principalities, and Angels, Dominions, and Powers, and Archangels, and the Cherubim praise Thee awesomely, sending up praise unto Thee: by whose prayers, O Savior, save our souls!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* What virtue, or what praise is worthy of thee, O Mary? For bowing the heavens, He descended and was pleased to be born of thee, the pre-eternal God; for whose sake all things upon earth today are filled with joy, the idolatrous shrines are brought low, and the pagan altars are destroyed, for Christ is come into the world, our God Who became incarnate: glory to Thee!

*Prokeimenon: Behold now bless the Lord, all ye servants of the Lord (LXX Ps. 133:1). Verse: Ye that stand in the temple of the Lord (LXX Ps. 133:2).*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of Repentance [Kneeling]:*

*Sticheron 1:* Thee, King and God, the Angels unceasingly hymn; but I fall down before Thee, crying unto Thee as the publican: O God, be gracious unto me and have mercy on me!

*Sticheron 2:* Having entered into [the sea of life], O Immortal One, I am submerged by worldly waves; but rising up, crying unto my Benefactor: O God, be gracious unto me and have mercy on me!

*Sticheron 3:* Martyrs of the Lord, in every place [p215LA] ye sanctify and heal every infirmity; and now watching, deliver our souls from the snare of the enemy, we pray!

*Theotokion:* I, O holy Virgin Theotokos, flee unto thy protection; I know that I shall find salvation, for thou art able to help me!

*Troparion, Tone 4:* Supreme Commanders of the heavenly hosts, we the unworthy now beseech you: that by your prayers you may shelter us under the shadow of the wings of your immaterial glory, preserving us who fall down and cry diligently: Deliver us from perils, as leaders of the higher powers![^251]

*Theotokion:* Knowing that thou art superior to all creatures, O Theotokos, we draw near to hymn thee with our soul in perils; therefore we pray thee: have mercy on us!

*At the First Stichologia, Sessional Hymns of Repentance, Tone 1:*[^252]

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Make haste to open unto me the Father's embrace; prodigally have I squandered my life, but looking unto the wealth of Thy tender mercies, despise not now an impoverished heart; unto Thee, O Lord, in repentance I cry: I have sinned, Father, against heaven and before Thee!

*Sessional Hymn 2:* If the righteous scarcely be saved, where shall I, a sinner, appear? I have not borne the burden and the heat of the day, but number me with the laborers of the eleventh hour, O God, and have mercy on me!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, joy of our forefathers, glory of our race, and protection of those who run unto thee!

*At the Second Stichologia, Sessional Hymns, Tone 1:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* I have zealously imitated the prodigal in sins, but have not acquired his repentance; yet as the all-good Son, O Lord, turn me who have willfully wandered, and cleanse me from the defilements of my transgressions for the sake of the Theotokos; I cry unto Thee: I have sinned, Father, against heaven and before Thee!

*Sessional Hymn 2:* When Thou comest in power [p216LA] upon earth with glory, and all things tremble, a river of fire flows before the judgment seat, and the books are opened, and secret things are made manifest: then deliver me from the unquenchable fire, and vouchsafe me to stand on Thy right hand, O righteous Judge!

*Martyrikon:* These soldiers of the great King resisted the commands of the tyrants manfully; they were heedless of tortures, and having abolished all deceit, ye were worthily crowned; and they ask of the Savior peace and great mercy for our souls!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, O Virgin Theotokos, for thou didst bear the heavenly King, the Savior and Enlightener of our souls!

*On Monday [at Matins], Canon of Repentance [to our Lord Jesus Christ], Ode 1, Tone 1. Heirmos:*[^253] Thy victorious right hand has been glorified in strength in a manner fitting for God; for that immortal [right hand], being all-powerful, shattered the adversaries, renewing a path through the deep for the Israelites, for He has been glorified!

*Troparion:* Consume the multitude of my sins by the multitude of Thy compassions, and grant me a thought of conversion, that I may glorify Thy goodness which surpasses the mind!

*Troparion:* Fittingly imitating the deadness of Christ Who suffered of His own will, O all-praised passion-bearers, ye endured many various deaths, O glorious ones; therefore, O martyrs, ye have been likened unto the immortal life!

*Theotokion:* The ranks of bodiless powers praise thee, O all-hymned Virgin, who didst bear the uncircumscribed God incarnate, and didst remain a virgin, even as before childbirth, O all-pure one!

*Another Canon to the Angels, Tone 1. Heirmos: Delivered from bitter slavery...*

[p217LA] *Troparion:* Standing before the throne, before the Master, O all-holy Angels: entreat the Word, the Angel of great counsel, Who with the Father without beginning is in the Spirit, for us who praise you in hymns!

*Troparion:* Light-bearing mirrors, receiving the outpourings of the thrice-radiant light, all ye ranks of Angels, having first conceived the divine arrangements as far as is attainable!

*Troparion:* Illumined by the divine light, enlightened by the Holy Spirit, deliver us from passions and eternal pitfalls!

*Theotokion:* God, Who adorned the ranks of the higher powers, took up His abode in thy most pure womb, O Theotokos, higher than the Seraphim, and was made flesh without change!

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*[^254] O Thou Who alone knowest the weakness of human nature, and hast mercifully clothed Thyself therein: gird me with power from on high, that I may sing unto Thee, the spiritual temple of Thine ineffable glory, O Lover of mankind!

*Troparion:* Have mercy on me, O only Good One, have mercy! As the publican I cry unto Thee with fear, bearing a multitude of sins with myself, and covered with the weight of transgressions, and filled with immeasurable shame!

*Troparion:* Confessing the Unity of the Holy Trinity, ye destroyed the deceit of the devil by divine faith, and were shown forth as luminaries, illumining the hearts of all with divine rays!

*Theotokion:* O holy, all-pure one, thou didst bear the Holy One Who rests in the saints, the Son and Word co-beginningless with the Father, Who sanctifies the world and shines forth with goodness!

*Another Heirmos, the same:* To the divine light the Seraphim drawing near, filled with abundant [p218LA] satisfaction, having shone forth first in brightness, as secondary lights they are deified by the Godhead!

*Troparion:* Desiring diligently to sing angelically in light, granted help and faithfully asking with purity of mind, and with most pure lips, to receive their illuminations!

*Troparion:* Have mercy on me, O only Good One, save me as the publican! With fear I cry unto Thee, bearing [sins] with myself, and covered with the weight of transgressions, filled with immeasurable shame!

*Theotokion:* Gabriel was counted worthy to behold the transcendent Word, O all-hymned Virgin, and brought unto thee the joyful voice, plainly proclaiming the conception of the Word and declaring the ineffable childbirth!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*[^255] Seeing with far-seeing eyes the mountain shadowed by the grace of God, Habakkuk proclaimed that the Holy One of Israel should go forth from thee, for our salvation and renewal!

*Troparion:* Unto whom shall I liken thee, O my wretched soul, working wicked and grievous deeds? But turn and cry unto Him Who willfully became poor for thy sake: O Knower of hearts, have mercy and save me!

*Troparion:* Great champions, true martyrs, ye endured the fire of torment and bitter suffering; therefore, O spiritual ones, deliver us who faithfully glorify thee from alien torment!

*Theotokion:* He Who of old was begotten of the Father, the Savior, born of thee in time beyond time, O Virgin, taking flesh from thee, was pleased to blot out the ancient curse, as Merciful!

*Another Heirmos, the same:* Thrones, Cherubim, and Seraphim fulfilling the primary beauty [p219LA] flashing with divine rays, receiving the divine ministrations, with songs they praise Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion:* They praise the thrice-radiant Unity of the Godhead, by threefold holy sanctifications, making known an unceasing voice; and the most pure Seraphim mystically bless and gloriously instruct!

*Troparion:* Sigh, O my soul! Weep, O my soul! But cease from evil deeds and unseemly actions, that by the prayers of the holy Archangels thou mayest find the Judge merciful!

*Theotokion:* He Who is higher than all creation, Who formed man on earth wisely and by divine hand, illumined with divine splendor, took up His abode in thy womb, O all-hymned one!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*[^256] Our enlightenment by Thy coming, O Christ, and having illumined the ends of the world with the Cross, enlighten the hearts of those who orthodoxly hymn Thee with the light of Thy knowledge!

*Troparion:* Thou knowest the hidden and secret things of my heart, O God my Creator; judge me not, O Lord, in the day of judgment, when Thou shalt judge the whole world!

*Troparion:* Strengthened by the hope of things to come, O martyrs, enduring tearings easily and manfully, by unflagging endurance ye gave no rest unto the multifaceted enemy!

*Theotokion:* The mouth cannot tell of thy dignity, nor recount ineffable beauty, O unwedded one; ineffably thou givest birth, and bearest in thy hands Him Who holds all things in His hand!

*Another Heirmos, the same:* Kindled with divine love, Dominions and Powers [p220LA] and secondary ranks, with unceasing mouth praising the divine origin, [glorify] one power, one essence!

*Troparion:* The leadership of Archangels, ranks of spiritual Angels, Principalities and innumerable hosts, shining with threefold unity, are most brightly taught to honor the one Essence!

*Troparion:* As Thou art the Physician of souls and bodies, O Master, heal the grievous and heavy sickness of my soul, for the sake of the Angels and Archangels, O Christ, by their God-pleasing prayers, as Thou art all-good!

*Theotokion:* Thou wast adorned more than all angelic comeliness, having conceived the Creator of Angels, O most pure Mother of God, receiving flesh and bearing the Incarnate One, O all-immaculate one!

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*[^257] The uttermost abyss has encompassed us, and there is none to deliver; we are accounted as sheep for the slaughter: save Thy people, O our God, for Thou art the strength and restoration of the weak!

*Troparion:* Wandering on the path of destruction, and fallen into the pit of transgressions: turn me back, O Christ, unto the path of Thy commandments and unto the light of Thy righteousness, that I may glorify Thee!

*Troparion:* Showered with stones as with truly precious stones, ye did not reject the Rock of life, nor did ye sacrifice unto the idols carved of stone, O glorious and forever existing martyrs, invincible ones!

*Theotokion:* According to the divine will thou didst bear One of the Trinity, begotten of the Father, O immaculate one, serving the One Essence: Whom pray unceasingly without ceasing!

*Another Heirmos, the same:* The bodiless ranks, O Lord, standing before the throne of Thy glory, O Merciful One, with unceasing voices thrice [p221LA] [holy hymn bringing unto Thee: for] Thou art our strength, and the boast of Christians![^258]

*Troparion:* Beholding the incomprehensible beauties of Thy face, O most divine Angels, and flashing with divine rays through Thy beauty, they are espoused [unto Thee], for Thou art our light and rejoicing, O Master!

*Troparion:* Raising the dead to life, leading the blind unto the light, and opening a remedy for those who are grievously infirm, which brought near the day of salvation: such is your intercession, O holy Angels and Archangels!

*Theotokion:* The transcendent Bodiless One, the Word from thee, O all-pure one, was incarnate, He Who by His will created and brought forth the bodiless hosts from non-existence, as All-powerful!

*Kontakion of the Archangels, Tone 2:* Archangels of God, ministers of divine glory, leaders of the Angels and guides of the bodiless [powers]: ask that which is profitable for us, and great mercy, as archangels of the bodiless hosts!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*[^259] Thee, O Mother of God, we faithful perceive as a noetic furnace; for as He saved the three youths, the Exalted and Renovating One was wholly contained in thy womb, the God of our fathers Who is praised and exceeding glorious!

*Troparion:* As of old Daniel tamed the lions by fasting, and armed himself with good deeds: imitate him, O my soul, for the roaring one seeks ever to devour me as a lion; and falling down before God, pray that He may make him of none effect!

*Troparion:* The God-radiant martyrs, enlightened with the light of the most rich Trinity, passed through the dark gloom and deceitful mist, singing with praises: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

[p222LA] *Theotokion:* In accordance with the law, nature is renewed in thee; for beyond the laws of the flesh thou, O immaculate one, didst bear the Lawgiver, Who established deliverance for all who cry: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Another Heirmos, to the Bodiless Powers, the same:*[^260] Being the unoriginate Light, O Master, Thou didst shine forth as Light from the Father; Thou hast established the angelic hosts, who receive the unwaning radiance as mirrors, praising: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion:* Neither tongue nor mind, O Master, can recount Thy wonders and God-befitting deed; for Thou hast adorned beyond measure the beauty of the heavenly hosts, praised by the fathers!

*Troparion:* Shining with radiant beams, O Angels and Archangels, enlighten the whole inhabited world; therefore also I pray: enlighten my darkened soul by your prayers!

*Theotokion:* In the flesh from thee, O holy one, the Son, Who is before the ages from the Father without mother, came for our sake in our image, Whom the bodiless hosts serve unceasingly, as [One] praised: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* In the furnace the children of Israel, as in a crucible, by the beauty of piety shone purer than gold, saying: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn and exalt Him above all forever!

*Troparion:* O Christ my Deliverer, Merciful One, deliver me who am held fast by the mist of sin and all passions, as I hymn Thee: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, hymn and exalt Him above all!

*Troparion:* When Thou shalt come, O Christ, in glory to judge the world, number me among the standing of Thine elect, as I hymn and say: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

[p223LA] *Theotokion:* Desiring to save the world, having proclaimed Christ unto thee, that thou shouldest be the Mother of Him Who made all things, Whom we hymn, O all-pure one, praising and exalting Him above all!

*Another Heirmos, to the Bodiless Powers [Tone 1]:*[^261] The angelic hosts hymn Thee, as the Source of all good brings forth secondary lights by divine power without corruption, who receive the transcendent light, singing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion:* The pre-eternal Mind and Creator, having super-essentially established the transcendent minds, drawing near unto Himself, is hymned: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion:* The choirs of light, the angelic hosts, were illumined with the rays of thrice-radiant beauty; we faithful, as is fitting, hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Theotokion:* Him Who was begotten beyond word from the Father before the ages, incarnate ineffably, thou didst bear for us, O all-hymned Virgin, Whom we hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*We praise, we bless, and we worship the Lord:*

*Troparion:* The angelic hosts hymn Thee, and the human race glorifies Thee, sacred servants, souls of the righteous, as Creator and Lord, throughout all ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* The burning bush revealed unconsumed the image of thy pure childbearing; and now we pray thee to quench the furnace of tribulations raging against us, that we may unceasingly magnify thee, O Mother of God!

*Troparion:* As the Canaanite woman I cry unto Thee: have mercy on me! For I have a soul that suffers distress from demonic assaults, and foolishly commits unrighteousness, not fearing Thy fear, O longsuffering Lord!

*Troparion:* Having already passed through the turbulent water of tribulations, O choirs of martyrs, and the bitter torment of that time, ye have reached the eternal havens of the wisdom [p224LA] of God, and the enjoyment of everlasting calm!

*Theotokion:* Thou bearest, O Lady, Him Who bears all things, and dost nurse Him Who gives food to all; great and awesome beyond understanding is thy mystery, O ark of the honorable sanctuary, Virgin Mother of God: therefore in faith we bless thee!

*Another Heirmos, the same [to the Bodiless Powers]:*[^262] Partakers of ineffable glory Thou hast made the bodiless minds, O Savior; and now, O Good One, preserve Thy people who with love run unto Thee, O Merciful One: and Thee, O Savior, we magnify!

*Troparion:* Praising this with joy, instill in Thy church, O Master, the heavenly sweetness and beauty, and quietly grant good order, that we may unceasingly magnify Thee, O Savior!

*Troparion:* Preserving Thy servants from on high, O Almighty One, preserve Thy flock in peace; Thou Who alone art peace and love, keeping our spiritual faith, and destroying every demonic power by Thy might!

*Theotokion:* The ranks of Angels, O pure one, also now unceasingly glorify thy childbearing, standing in their order, looking on with fear, filled with His greatness: thee they unceasingly magnify!

*Sessional Hymn [Photogogikon / Exapostilarion], Tone 1:* Archangels and Angels, Principalities, Powers, and Authorities, Thrones, many-eyed Cherubim and Seraphim, and six-winged living creatures: pray unto the Lord, that we may be delivered from tribulations and unquenchable fire!

*Theotokion:* Thou art my protectress, O all-pure one, and a firm refuge; thou art the mediatress of the world, and falling down before thee I cry: deliver me from cruel evils, O thou who didst bear the Joy of the world [p225LA] and snatching from eternal fire those who hope in thee, O Virgin!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of Repentance, Tone 1:*[^263]

*Sticheron 1:* Behold what awaits thee, O my soul! For the day is about to expose thy secret evil deeds; therefore, see that thou canst not escape, and preventing it, cry aloud unto the Only One: O God, cleanse me as the publican, and save me!

*Sticheron 2:* Heal me, O Lord, and I shall be healed; save me, and I shall be saved! I always promise to repent, yet I turn to worse things. Therefore I pray Thee, Who wast born of the Virgin: set me upright, O Lord, and have mercy on me!

*Martyrikon:* O all-praised martyrs, the earth did not hide you, but heaven received you; the gates of Paradise were opened unto you, and being within, enjoying the Tree of life, pray unto Christ, to grant unto our souls peace and great mercy!

*Theotokion:* Joy of the heavenly ranks, and strong helper of men upon earth, O pure Virgin: save us who flee unto thee, for our hope after God, O Mother of God, have we placed in thee!

*On Monday, Beatitudes at the Hours, Tone 1:*

*Troparion 1:* For the sake of food the enemy led Adam out of Paradise, but by the Cross Christ led the thief into it: Remember me, crying in Thy kingdom!

*Troparion 2:* Grant me fountains of compunction from Thy tender mercy, O Christ God; cleanse me from every evil defilement, and make me a partaker of Thy kingdom, O Good One!

*Troparion 3:* Bringing the ranks of the Archangels unto prayer, O Savior, have compassion on us for their sake, as Thou art all-good, overlooking all our [p226LA] sins committed in knowledge and in ignorance!

*Troparion 4:* By the stream of your blood, O saints, ye manifestly drowned the noetic Pharaoh; and now ye pour forth streams of wonders, drying up the deep of infirmities: therefore ye are blessed!

*Glory:* Let us worship the Father, and let us glorify the Son, and let us faithful hymn the all-holy Spirit, calling out and saying: O all-pure Trinity, consubstantial, save us!

*Both now:* O thou who didst bear the unwaning Light, enlighten my soul darkened by the assaults of demons, O all-immaculate one, and free me from the coming fire by thy mediation!

*Prokeimenon [Tone 4]: Who makes His angels spirits, and His ministers a flame of fire (LXX Ps. 103:4). Verse: Bless the Lord, O my soul (LXX Ps. 103:1).*[^264]

*Epistle to the Hebrews [Heb. 2:2–10]:* Brethren, if the word spoken by angels was steadfast, and every transgression and disobedience received a just recompense of reward, how shall we escape, if we neglect so great a salvation; which at the first began to be spoken by the Lord, and was confirmed unto us by them that heard Him; God also bearing them witness, both with signs and wonders, and with various miracles, and distributions of the Holy Spirit, according to His own will? For He did not put in subjection unto the angels the world to come, whereof we speak. But one in a certain place testified, saying: "What is man, that Thou art mindful of him? or the son of man, that Thou visitest him? Thou madest him a little lower than the angels; Thou crownedst him with glory and honor, and didst set him over the works of Thy hands; Thou hast put all things in subjection under his feet." For in that He put all in subjection under him, He left nothing that is not put under him. But now [p227LA] we see not yet all things put under him. But we see Jesus, Who was made a little lower than the angels for the suffering of death, crowned with glory and honor; that He by the grace of God should taste death for every man. For it became Him, for Whom are all things, and by Whom are all things, in bringing many sons unto glory, to make the Captain of their salvation perfect through sufferings.

*Gospel according to John [sic! Matthew 13:24–30, 36–43]:*[^265] The Lord spoke unto His disciples this parable: The kingdom of heaven is likened unto a man who sowed good seed in his field; but while men slept, his enemy came and sowed tares among the wheat, and went his way. But when the blade was sprung up, and brought forth fruit, then appeared the tares also. So the servants of the householder came and said unto him: "Sir, didst not thou sow good seed in thy field? from whence then does it have tares?" He said unto them: "An enemy has done this." The servants said unto him: "Wilt thou then that we go and gather them up?" But he said: "Nay; lest while ye gather up the tares, ye root up also the wheat with them. Let both grow together until the harvest; and in the time of harvest I will say to the reapers: Gather ye together first the tares, and bind them in bundles to burn them; but gather the wheat into my barn." And His disciples came unto Him, saying: "Declare unto us the parable of the tares of the field." He answered and said unto them: "He Who sows the good seed is the Son of man; the field is the world; the good seed are the children of the kingdom; but the tares are the children of the wicked one; the enemy that sowed them is the devil; the harvest is the end of the world; and the reapers [p228LA] are the angels. As therefore the tares are gathered and burned in the fire; so shall it be in the end of this world. The Son of man shall send forth His angels, and they shall gather out of His kingdom all things that offend, and them which do iniquity; and shall cast them into a furnace of fire: there shall be wailing and gnashing of teeth. Then shall the righteous shine forth as the sun in the kingdom of their Father. He that has ears to hear, let him hear."

*Communion Verse [Kinonikon]: Who makes His angels spirits, and His ministers a flame of fire. Alleluia (LXX Ps. 103:4).*[^266]

## MONDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR TUESDAY)

*On Monday Evening, Stichera at "Lord, I have cried", Tone 2.*

*Podoben: "When from the Tree" (Е҆гда̀ ѿ дре́ва):*

*Sticheron 1:* Lament bitterly, O my soul, and repent of the many sins which thou hast committed in life; and implore the multitude of the heavenly [powers] with sighing and tears, that time for repentance may be granted thee, lest like the barren fig tree thou be cut down into the fire of Gehenna, O all-wretched one!

*Sticheron 2:* I have not kept even a single commandment of Thine on earth; how shall I appear before Thee seated upon the throne, and give an account concerning all things which I have done in knowledge and in ignorance, O my Christ? Therefore I cry unto Thee: by the prayers of Thy ministers, save me, the prodigal!

*Sticheron 3:* Who will not weep for me, who have transgressed the commandment of the Most High? For the sake of intemperance I have dwelt in Hades instead of Paradise, through the sight of sweet food which brought about death; I have been manifestly alienated from the glory and life of God; but receive [p229LA] me weeping, as Merciful and Lover of mankind, O Lord, for the sake of Thy great mercy!

*Other Stichera to the Forerunner, Tone 2.*

*Podoben: "The same" (то́йже):* [^267]

*Sticheron 1:* A morning ray running before the sun, thou didst appear as a luminary, O Baptist, enlightening the universe, healing infirmities, and driving away demons; therefore I pray thee: enlighten me also, the wretched one, with radiant beams, and disperse the mist of sin; for after God I have thee as a fervent protector and good refuge, O Baptist!

*Sticheron 2:* Thou art swift to help, and strong in tribulations, and a fervent protector, O all-pure Baptist, consolation of the sorrowful: deliver me who am held fast by many passions; for I have thee as hope and refuge; therefore enlighten me who am grievously darkened, and by thy prayers stir me up unto God!

*Sticheron 3:* A tempest of sins evilly and the waves of life submerge me, tribulations and passions drown me; but by thy prayers guide me unto a calm haven, I pray thee, onto the path of repentance; deliver me from eternal fire and torment; when the Judge shall sit upon the judgment seat, pray unto Him, O holy one, that He may deliver me from all evils!

*Glory, to the Forerunner, Tone 2, the same:* Be ever a protection and defense unto the sorrowful, O Forerunner of Christ, driving away tribulations and passionate sicknesses; therefore with compunction I cry unto thee, asking cleansing for mine innumerable offenses, whereby I have estranged myself from God; but by thy prayers, O holy Forerunner, reconcile me, asking forgiveness, and direct me onto the path of repentance!

*Both now, Theotokion, Tone 2:* In thee our hope [p230LA] have we placed, that we may not fall from our expectation: save us from tribulations, who have no help; scatter the counsels of adversaries; for thou art our salvation, O blessed one!

*Prokeimenon [Tone 2]: Thy mercy, O Lord, shall pursue me all the days of my life (LXX Ps. 22:6). Verse: The Lord is my shepherd, and I shall want nothing (LXX Ps. 22:1).*[^268]

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of Repentance, Tone 2:*

*Sticheron 1:* I have sinned against Thee, O Christ Savior, as the prodigal son: receive me who repent, and have mercy on me, O God!

*Sticheron 2:* I cry unto Thee, O Christ Savior, with the voice of the publican: cleanse me as him, and have mercy on me, O God!

*Martyrikon:* When the holy martyrs pray for us, and hymn Christ, all deceit ceases, and men are saved through faith!

*Theotokion:* As a fruitful olive tree, O Virgin, thou hast brought forth for us the Fruit of life, to bear the fruit of Thy rich mercy unto the whole world, O Lord!

*Dismissal Troparion, Tone 4:* [Troparion of the Forerunner]

*Tuesday Matins, Kathisma 1 Sessional Hymns, Tone 2:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Overlook mine iniquities, O Lord Born of the Virgin, and cleanse my heart, and make it a temple of Thy Holy Spirit, and cast me not away from Thy presence, as Thou hast unmeasured and great mercy!

*Sessional Hymn 2:* "Have mercy on me," said David; and I cry unto Thee: I have sinned against Thee, O Savior; cleanse my sins through repentance, and have mercy on me!

*Theotokion:* Being a fountain of tender mercy, vouchsafe forgiveness unto us, O Mother of God; look upon the people who have sinned; show forth ever thy power; for we bring thee help and greeting, as of old did Gabriel, the bodiless archangel!

*At the 2nd Stichologia, Sessional Hymns of Repentance, Tone 2:*

*Sessional Hymn 1:* Have mercy on me, O God, have mercy on me! Over two sins David wept [p231LA] but I cry unto Thee from the darkness of transgressions; he drenched his bed with tears, but I have not acquired even a single drop; yet not despairing of Thy tender mercy, have mercy on me, O God, according to Thy great mercy![^269]

*Sessional Hymn 2:* I am an unfruitful tree, O Lord, bearing no fruit of repentance at all, and I fear the hewing down, and dread that unquenchable fire; therefore I pray Thee: before the end, turn me and save me!

*Martyrikon:* The ranks of the martyrs opposed the tyrants, saying: We wage war for the King of hosts; even if ye deliver us unto fire and torments, we deny not the power of the Trinity!

*Theotokion:* Rejoice, our hope, O pure Mother of God; rejoice, thou who didst receive joy from the angel; rejoice, thou who didst bear the Father's Son for us, O blessed one; rejoice, most pure Virgin, alone all-hymned; all creation glorifies thee unto ages of ages!

*On Tuesday, Canon of Supplication to the Holy Forerunner, Tone 2. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Come, ye people, let us sing a song unto Christ God, Who divided the sea and guided through the people whom He released from the bondage of Egypt, for He hath been glorified!

*Troparion 1:* An angel in the flesh, as though bodiless, wast thou revealed upon earth, O Forerunner and Baptist of Christ, brought forth beyond expectation from barrenness!

*Troparion 2:* Having acquired wealth of virtue and despised worldly nature, O Baptist, thou wast revealed as a prophet and dweller of the wilderness, and wast made a partaker of the glory of Christ!

*Theotokion:* Davidically, O Sovereign Lady, as a Queen, thou standest at the right hand of the Almighty, adorned with beauty, and dost save those who call upon thee in faith!

[p232LA] *Another Canon to the Lord, of Compunction. Ode 1. Heirmos: As to Moses...*[^270]

*Troparion 1:* With showers of tears bring to repentance my insensible soul; I fall into temptation; have compassion, O Savior, upon my weakness!

*Troparion 2:* Desiring to show forth His goodness, the Most High took the form of a servant, to endure insults and reproaches, having the likeness of human nature, O Beauty of the faithful!

*Martyrikon:* Having mingled with the fiery torment, through the divine dew, O youths of Christ, deliver from the lake of fire him who is beset with grievous passions!

*Theotokion:* O fire-receiving tongs, which Isaiah beheld of old, burn up the defilements of my heart and the bodily passions, O Virgin Mother, and consume them utterly!

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Establish me upon the rock of faith, and enlarge my mouth against mine enemies; for my spirit hath rejoiced to sing unto Thee: Holy art Thou as our God, and there is none righteous beside Thee, O Lord!

*Troparion 1:* Nourished in the wilderness by an angel, thou camest into the world preaching the coming of Him Who takes away the sins of the world, O Forerunner, pointing with the finger: Behold the Lamb of God, and He shall baptize you with the Holy Spirit!

*Troparion 2:* Plunge my sins, O Forerunner, into the abyss of God's tender mercy, away from the raging depth of my grievous transgressions, and by thy prayers cast down the enemy who persecutes me!

*Theotokion:* Thou alone above all generations wast granted wonders transcending nature: thou didst bear in thy womb God Whom all creation cannot contain; therefore we ever acknowledge thee as the Mother of God!

*Another Heirmos:* From the barren spirit of condemnation that weighs me down, raise me up unto [p233LA] salvation, O Christ, and to do Thy will in repentance![^271]

*Troparion 1:* Being Holy by nature, alone united with the flesh of a servant, Thou didst work out our manifold salvation, O Christ, providentially providing otherwise and becoming visible in figures!

*Martyrikon:* Having become imitators of the sufferings of Christ, O noble and most glorious martyrs, heal the grievous passions of my soul!

*Theotokion:* Thou nursest maternally the Nourisher of all, and dost carry upon thy hands, O most pure one, Him Who holds all things in His hand!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* I sing of Thee by report, O Lord; hearing thereof, I was astonished, for Thou hast come unto me, seeking me who had gone astray; therefore I cry unto Thee: Glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Troparion 1:* Grant me a shower of tears, O Forerunner of Christ, washing away the defilement of the evil deeds wrought by me, that I may unceasingly praise thee in hymnody and celebrate thy falling asleep!

*Troparion 2:* With archangels and prophets, O Forerunner of Christ, and with apostles and martyrs, with hierarchs and monastics, pray together unto Christ to grant remission of sins in the day of judgment!

*Theotokion:* I have defiled my soul, O Sovereign Lady, with filthy sins; have mercy on me, I cry unto thee as a good Mother, I thy sinful servant, and pray thee to be the cleansing of my sins!

*Another Heirmos: Of Thy birth from the Virgin...*

*Troparion 1:* I have defiled my hands and feet with evil deeds which are not seemly to do, and have provoked Thy compassions unto wrath, knowing full well, and despising Thy long-suffering; have compassion on me, O Good One!

*Martyrikon:* As Thou art marvelous in Thy saints, having subdued the unmerciful earth and cast down the altars of the demons, [p234LA] and having inherited the delight of Paradise from which Adam of old was cast out![^272]

*Troparion 1:* Sigh, O my soul! Shed tears, O my soul! Cease from committing evil and unseemly deeds, that through the prayers of the martyrs I may find the Judge merciful!

*Troparion 2:* Spare me, O Christ, spare me when Thou shalt come to judge, and rebuke me not in Thine anger; the pure Virgin who bore Thee entreats Thee, O Christ, with the multitude of the angels and the assembly of the martyrs!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* O Giver of light and Creator of the ages, O Lord, guide us in the light of Thy commandments; for beside Thee we know no other!

*Troparion 1:* Into the calm haven of thy protection, O Forerunner, I flee from perils; grant me repentance; for to thee I fall down, beset with sins: deliver me speedily by thy prayers!

*Troparion 2:* With angels and archangels, O Forerunner and Baptist, who didst emulate the Throne of God, with prophets and apostles of Christ, with martyrs and hierarchs, pray fervently that our souls may find mercy!

*Theotokion:* Heal, O pure Mother of God, the sicknesses and wounds of my soul; for thou hast given birth to the Physician of passions and the Healer of our souls: pray Him to grant me remission of sins!

*Another Heirmos: Dispel the darkness of my soul...*

*Troparion 1:* Suffering disaster like a shipwreck, I lost the cargo which Thou gavest me, O Bountiful One; and now destitute, I cry unto Thee: Despise me not, O Christ!

*Troparion 2:* As Thou art the Physician of souls, O Master, heal the grievous and heavy sickness of my soul, for the sake of Thy prophets and disciples, O Christ, by their God-pleasing prayers, as Thou art all-good!

[p235LA] *Martyrikon:* Despising earthly glory, O holy martyrs, ye were counted worthy of heavenly glory, being with Christ![^273]

*Theotokion:* Daniel called thee a great mountain, foreseeing thee by the Spirit, O Mother of God, from which the stone was cut without hands, incorruptibly!

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Engulfed in the abyss of sins, I call upon the unfathomable abyss of Thy tender mercy: lead me up from corruption, O Good One!

*Troparion 1:* By the swiftness of thy power, pluck me out of the depth of sin which hath enslaved me, and free me wholly from corruption!

*Troparion 2:* Cleanse my transgressions by thy prayers, O prophet and Forerunner, beloved friend of the Lord!

*Theotokion:* Having received the abyss of God's tender mercy, O Sovereign Lady, thou dost grant healing freely unto those who come unto thy protection!

*Another Heirmos: In the deep of transgressions...*

*Troparion 1:* As the Canaanite woman of old, I cry unto Thee: O Christ, Son of God, have mercy on me and take pity upon my soul which suffers grievously; I pray Thee, call me unto repentance!

*Troparion 2:* Shining richly with most radiant rays, O prophets with the apostles, illuminated with Trinitarian beams: enlighten my wretched soul by your prayers!

*Martyrikon:* The bodiless choirs of angels were astonished at Him Who gave you strength, O holy passion-bearers, and at the overthrow of the power of the demons!

*Theotokion:* As the table that held the Bread of Life, of Which whosoever eats shall live, we know thee, O most pure one, to be the Bearer of God and our Nourisher!

*Tone 3 Kontakion to the Holy Forerunner.*

*Podoben: "Today the Virgin" (Дѣ́ва дне́сь):*

[p236LA] the prophets proclaimed, upon Him in the Jordan he laid his hand, and was shown forth as the prophet of God the Word, herald and Forerunner together![^274]

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The godless decree of the lawless tyrant raised a towering flame; but Christ spread spiritual dew over the pious children, He Who is blessed and supremely praised!

*Troparion 1:* Despise me not, O Forerunner of Christ; the snares of sin have entangled me; many passions and afflictions plunge me into peril: deliver me from these cruelties, and cleanse me!

*Troparion 2:* A multitude of unholy spirits plunges my wretched soul into the depth of despair; but I flee unto the calm haven, O Baptist, crying: Cleanse me!

*Theotokion:* The barren woman, carrying in her womb the Great Forerunner, the shoot of barrenness, when the Virgin was carrying God the Word becoming incarnate, recognized Him with divine leaping, and rejoicing, bowed down!

*Another Heirmos: Emulating the Cherubim...*

*Troparion 1:* I was conceived in iniquities, O Lord, as David I cry unto Thee; I lived upon earth and as a servant provoked the good God unto anger: leave me not to perish, O Savior, according to Thy love for mankind!

*Troparion 2:* Having acquired Thee, O Christ Savior, as our fervent intercessor and mediator before all, we are saved from all tribulations, crying unto Thee in faith: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Martyrikon:* Manfully the assembly of martyrs struggled, and manfully they were crowned by the life-bearing right hand; for truly having loved Christ God, unto death [p237LA] they kept Thy words, and in the heavens they delight in the crowns of their inheritance![^275]

*Theotokion:* The Cherubim glorify thee and the Seraphim sing praises unto thee; the Thrones, Dominions, and Powers ever extol thee, O Mary all-hymned; for thou alone hast given birth to God in the flesh: pray Him, O pure one, to save those who in love call thee blessed!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The fiery furnace of old in Babylon divided its actions by divine command, scorching the Chaldeans but refreshing the faithful: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Troparion 1:* Most radiant beam of the spiritual Sun, O herald and Forerunner of Christ, preaching repentance unto all the people, saying: Repent, for the kingdom of the heavens is at hand! Him all the works of the Lord hymn and supremely exalt!

*Troparion 2:* With most radiant rays enlighten my darkened soul by thy prayer, O Forerunner of Christ; with prophets and apostles, pray unceasingly for us, that we may be delivered from sins and perils!

*Theotokion:* My wretched soul has grown weak through the many assaults of the wicked adversary: heal it with remedies, O Virgin, and make me whole, that supremely exalting thee, I may hymn thee unto the ages!

*Another Heirmos: With the light...*

*Troparion 1:* Having become incarnate for the sake of tender mercy, Thou didst deify those bereft of understanding and enslaved by pleasures; but by Thy goodness, O Christ, turn and save all!

*Troparion 2:* Thou art the Good Shepherd, O Word: having sought and found the sheep that went astray upon the mountains through transgression, turn and save my wretched soul, and let not the deceitful enemy devour me, [p238LA] woe is me, who have wrought much evil! Woe is me, who have provoked the all-good God to anger! O Baptist of Christ, help me; grant remission of my sins and release from my debts through thine intercession![^276]

*Theotokion:* That He might deify us, the Word became incarnate of thy pure blood, O Maiden, and became visible, O Mother of God: pray Him ever for those who honor thee!

*We praise, we bless, we worship the Lord:*

*Troparion 4:* Light-bearing in soul, shining brighter than snow, the holy youths were not burned in the midst of the flame, but calling all creation together with themselves unto the divine song, rejoicing they sang: All ye works of the Lord, sing ye!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* What are thy wonders and glorious mystery! Daughter of Adam and Mother of the Most High, who alone hast boldness toward God: thee do we worthily magnify!

*Troparion 1:* What are thy fearsome and glorious wonders, O Forerunner, heavenly citizen and angel in the flesh! Deliver my soul which is beset with sins, and enlighten it, that I thy servant may unceasingly praise thee, rejoicing!

*Troparion 2:* Who can tell of thy marvelous greatness, that with thy hand thou didst touch the crown of the head of the Master of all, Who desired to wash away the defilements of all! Therefore now I pray thee: fervently intercede for us, and cleanse my defilements!

*Theotokion:* Great refuge of Christians and holy covering of thy protection, gladden us and fill us with joy, O pure one, that with unceasing praises [p239LA] we, the faithful, magnify thee as the Mother of God![^277]

*Another Heirmos: Who of the earthborn ever heard such a thing...*

*Troparion 1:* How fearsome is Thy judgment, O Lord, before which all must appear! In heedlessness, O Master, I have spent all my years; but turn me, O Christ, only Creator, even as Thou didst turn Manasseh who had sinned!

*Troparion 2:* Ye were revealed as God-illumined stars, spiritually enlightening the whole inhabited earth, O prophets with the martyrs and hierarchs: therefore enlighten my soul, darkened by passions and afflictions, by your prayers!

*Martyrikon:* Steadfast victors and chosen host, the sacred assembly of holy martyrs and blessed company with the choirs of bodiless angels: by their prayers, O Christ, make us partakers of Thy kingdom!

*Theotokion:* With radiant rays from Him Who shone forth from thy womb upon us, Who dispersed the deep night of godlessness, O Virgin Mother Mary, enlighten those who honor thee in faith, and deliver us from the gloomy darkness, for we worthily magnify thee as the shrine of the God of Jacob, more honorable than the Cherubim!

*Tuesday Matins Exapostilarion to the Holy Forerunner.*

*Podoben: "Give ear, ye women" (Же́ны оу҆слы́шите):*

*Theotokion:* All the prophets proclaimed thee, O Virgin: one called thee a mountain, another an ark, and a lampstand, a ladder and a bridge, a jar bearing the manna, and a rod that budded; and we worthily honor thee as the Mother of God!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of Repentance, Tone 2:*
