[p240LA] *Sticheron 1:* Reflecting on the evil, unseemly deeds wrought by me, I flee unto Thy compassions, emulating the publican who repented and the harlot who wept; therefore I fall down before Thee, O Merciful One: before Thou judgest me, spare me, O God, and have mercy on me![^278]

*Sticheron 2:* I have spent my life in lawlessness; I have defiled both soul and body with earthly thoughts, O Lord; I entreat and pray Thee, crying unto Thee like the prodigal son: I have sinned before Thee, O Master; as Thou art the Lover of mankind, save me!

*Martyrikon:* Great is the glory which ye acquired through faith, O holy ones; for not only during your sufferings did ye conquer the enemy, but even after death ye drive away evil spirits and heal the sick, physicians of souls and bodies: pray unto the Lord that our souls may find mercy!

*Theotokion:* O impassable gate, mystically sealed, blessed Virgin Mother of God: receive our prayers and bear them unto Thy Son and our God, that for thy sake He may save our souls!

*On Tuesday, The Beatitudes, Tone 2:*

*Refrain:* We bring unto Thee the voice of the thief, and we pray Thee: Remember us, O Lord!

*Troparion 1:* Overlooking my manifold transgressions, O Lover of mankind, make me a partaker of the heavenly brightness, O Bountiful One!

*Troparion 2:* O Forerunner, who didst immerse the Abyss in the waters of mercy: dry up the abyss of mine evils by thy prayers!

*Troparion 3:* Having dried up the streams of deceit by the shedding of your blood, O passion-bearers of Christ, ye are worthily praised!

*Glory:* Let us glorify the Holy Trinity, praising the one Godhead in three Persons!

[p241LA] *Theotokion:* The Fruit of thy womb hath made me, who stumbled through the fruit [of the tree], once again an inhabitant of paradise!

*Prokeimenon [Tone 7: The righteous man shall be glad in the Lord. Verse: Hear my voice, O God (LXX Ps. 63:11, 2)].*

*Epistle from the Acts of the Apostles [Acts 13:25–33]:* In those days, when John was finishing his course, he said: Whom think ye that I am? I am not He; but behold, there comes One after me, the strap of Whose sandals I am not worthy to unloose. Men, brethren, sons of the family of Abraham, and whosoever among you fears God, unto you is the word of this salvation sent. For they who dwell in Jerusalem, and their rulers, having not understood Him, nor the voices of the prophets which are read every Sabbath day, have fulfilled them by condemning Him. And having found no cause of death in Him, they desired of Pilate that He should be slain. And when they had fulfilled all that was written of Him, they crucified Him; and taking Him down from the Tree, they laid Him in a tomb. But God raised Him from the dead; Who was seen for many days by those who came up with Him from Galilee unto Jerusalem, who are His witnesses unto the people. And we declare unto you the good tidings of the promise which was made unto the fathers, that God hath fulfilled the same unto their children, having raised up Jesus for us.

*Alleluia [Tone 4: The righteous man shall flourish like a palm tree. Verse: Planted in the house of the Lord (LXX Ps. 91:13–14)].*

*Gospel according to John [John 1:29–34]:* At that time, John saw Jesus coming unto him, and says: Behold the Lamb of God, Who takes away the sins of the whole world! This is He of Whom I said: After me comes a Man Who was before me, for He was prior to me. And I knew Him not; but that He should be made manifest unto Israel, and John bore witness, saying:[^279]

[p242LA] I saw the Spirit descending from heaven like a dove, and It abode upon Him. And I knew Him not, but He Who sent me to baptize with water, He said unto me: Upon Whom thou shalt see the Spirit descending, and abiding upon Him, the same is He Who baptizes with the Holy Spirit. And I saw, and bore witness that this is the Son of God.

*On Tuesday, Troparion, Tone 2:* The memory of the righteous is celebrated with hymns of praise, but for thee the witness of the Lord suffices, O Forerunner; for thou wast shown forth truly as the most honorable of the prophets, since thou wast accounted worthy to baptize in the streams Him Whom thou didst proclaim. Therefore, having suffered for the truth, rejoicing thou didst proclaim the good tidings even to those in hades of God manifest in the flesh, Who takes away the sin of the world and grants us great mercy.

*Glory, both now:* We have become partakers of the divine nature through thee, O Virgin Mother of God, for thou hast borne for us God incarnate; therefore, as is our duty, we all piously magnify thee![^280]

*Kinonikon:* In memory eternal shall the righteous be! (LXX Ps. 111:6)

*On Wednesday at Vespers, Stichera of the Cross:*

*On "Lord, I have cried", set 6 stichera: 3 of the Cross and 3 of the Theotokos. And we sing the Sticheron, Tone 3, Automelon:* O ye nations, coming together let us worship the cursed Tree whereon eternal righteousness was wrought; for he who deceived forefather Adam by a tree is deceived by the Tree of the Cross, and falls, cast down by a marvelous fall, he who so long held sway over the kingdom of creation; by divine Blood the venom of the serpent is washed away, and the curse of righteous judgment is undone, the righteous judgment [p243LA] pronounced upon the righteous condemned; for it was fitting that a tree should be healed by a Tree, and by the Passion of the Passionless One, he who had been condemned to suffering should be loosed from the tree; and glory to Thee for the dispensation of Thy compassion, whereby Thou hast saved all, as Lover of mankind!

*Sticheron 2:* O ye nations coming together: the divine treasure hidden in the earth, the Cross of the Life-giver, appearing in heaven unto the pious Emperor [Constantine], plainly foretelling victories over enemies according to the scripture; receiving which with joy, in faith and love... it lifted us up from the deep earth unto the heights, granting deliverance unto the world and salvation unto our souls!

*Sticheron 3:* O ye nations coming together, understand the power of the Cross: with God-receiver Moses, all the righteous according to the Law rejoice today; with Elijah the prophets and the divine choir, assembling all together, sing in love; for today the Cross clearly foreshadowed by them manifests from on high the coming of the fearsome Judgment unto us; therefore, meeting it with fear, let us entreat the Judge of all through the prayers of the Mother of our God, to save our souls!

*Other Stichera to the Most Holy Mother of God.*

*Podoben: "Great is the power of Thy Cross, O Lord" (Ве́лія Крⷭ҇та̀ твоегѡ̀ Гдⷭ҇и):*

*Sticheron 1:* Knowing thy great mercy, O Sovereign Lady, with voice of compunction we cry unto thee, asking deliverance from our countless offenses; for thee alone do we, thy sinful servants, have as helper![^281]

*Sticheron 2:* Stretch forth thy compassionate right hand, O Sovereign Lady, and snatch me not [p244LA] away, [who have fallen] into the mire of sins and have a heart darkened by incurable passions; therefore I pray thee: enlighten me, for thou didst bear the Giver of light, Christ, unto the world!

*Another Sticheron:* No other helper have I besides thee, O pure Mother of God, for in thee dwelt the transcendent Word of God, Who destroyed the dominion of the enemy by thy prayers, O all-pure Mother of God!

*Glory, both now. Staurotheotokion, the same Tone:* Beholding Thee nailed unto the Tree of the Cross, O Jesus our Savior, she who knew not wedlock wept and said: O sweet Child, why hast Thou left me alone, [Thou Who wast] born Light of unapproachable Light from the Father without beginning? Make haste and be glorified, that they who glorify Thy divine Passion may receive divine glory!

*Prokeimenon [Tone 4: Thy mercy, O Lord, shall pursue me (LXX Ps. 22:6). Verse: The Lord is my shepherd (LXX Ps. 22:1)].*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 3:*

*Sticheron 1:* We worship Thy precious Cross, O Christ, the guardian of the whole world, salvation unto us sinners, and great cleansing, the victory of the Church, and the boast of the whole inhabited earth![^282]

*Sticheron 2:* Prefiguring the Cross, Moses stretching forth his hands on high conquered Amalek; and Thou, O Savior, having stretched forth Thy hands upon the precious Cross, didst receive me, delivering me from the bondage of the enemy, and hast given me a sign of life to flee from the bow of the adversaries; therefore, O Word, we worship Thy precious Cross!

*Sticheron 3:* Great is the power of Thy Cross, O Lord; for it was set up in the place [p245LA] and works throughout the whole world; it showed forth fishermen as Apostles, and martyrs from among the Gentiles, that they may pray for our souls!

*Glory, both now:* Without seed thou didst conceive by the Holy Spirit, and ceaselessly glorified thou art sung: Rejoice, most holy Virgin!

## WEDNESDAY MATINS: THE PRECIOUS CROSS AND THE THEOTOKOS

*On Wednesday at Matins, at "God is the Lord", Troparion of the Cross, Tone 1:* Save, O Lord, Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance; grant victory unto our princes over barbarians, and by Thy Cross preserve Thy people![^283]

*Theotokion:* Having acquired thy protection, O all-pure one, and being delivered from afflictions by thy prayers, preserved everywhere by the Cross of thy Son, as is our duty we all piously magnify thee!

*At the 1st Stichologia, Sessional Hymn of the Cross, Tone 3:* The Cross was set up on earth, and salvation [directed] unto heaven, as a Tree reaching unto the heights, unto God Who upon It fills all things: O Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sessional Hymn 2:* At Thine infinite power and the manner of Thine enduring suffering, the angelic hosts marvelled beholding how the Invisible One received wounds in the flesh, desiring to deliver mankind from corruption; therefore they cry unto the Giver of life: Glory to Thy compassion, O Christ!

*Theotokion:* We have thee as a haven of salvation, O Virgin Mother of God, who labor in the sea of life; therefore pray to God incarnate of thee without seed, Who ineffably became man, to save our souls!

*At the 2nd Stichologia, Sessional Hymn of the Cross, Tone 3:* Through the tree we found death, and again through Thy Cross we found life; destroying the cunning enemy, Thou art crucified and dost save [p246LA] me, and leadest me unto glory, O my Redeemer, glory to Thee without ceasing!

*Sessional Hymn 2:* The Cross was held fast upon the earth, and when Thou wast lifted up upon It, Adam was delivered from death, and the whole world was set free from tyranny; no longer does the power of the enemy war against us; therefore nailed upon the Cross, O only Good One, Thou hast given a sign unto those who fear Thee, to hymn and worship Thy Passion: O our Savior, glory to Thee!

*Sessional Hymn 3:* Great is the power of Thy Cross, O Lord, for it was set up in a place, and works throughout the whole world; it showed forth fishermen as Apostles, and martyrs from among the Gentiles, that they may pray for our souls!

*Theotokion:* O Mother of God, helper of all who pray to thee: in thee do we hope, and in thee do we boast; in thee is all our expectation; pray to Him Who was born of thee for thine own servants!

*On Wednesday, Canon of the Cross to 4, and of the Mother of God to 4, Tone 3. Ode 1. Heirmos:* The sun once traversed the dry land that gave birth to the deep; for the water was congealed like a wall on both sides, while the people on foot traversed the deep, singing in a manner pleasing unto God: Let us sing unto the Lord, for gloriously hath He been glorified![^284]

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* The waves of the sea which Thou didst fiercely congeal, and leddest the people through, prefigured the power of the Cross, wherewith Thou didst strike the deceitful water, and [didst bestow] the knowledge of God on earth, O Savior, unto all who in faith hymn Thy power!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* By Thy command Thou didst make the abyss, held back above by the waters and not overflowing; and [as Thou didst hang] the earth upon the waters, Thou art hung upon the Tree, O Good One, shaking [p247LA] all creation by Thy will, and strengthenest the hearts of all, O Lord!

*Theotokion (Cross):* Most exquisitely adorned Virgin Lady, beholding the Son Whom thou didst bear lifted up upon the Tree, weeping and crying out sorrowfully: O Source of grace, how dost Thou suffer these things?

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Cut off from the shore...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Enlighten my soul evilly darkened by sins and unseemly deeds, O thou who didst receive in thy womb the Father's glory and radiance, and ineffably didst bear Christ our God: pray Him to save our souls!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Having thee as refuge and confirmation, and consolation in sorrows, unto thee do we flee, thy sinful servants, O all-pure champion Lady: deliver my passionate soul from torment, that I may magnify thee!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* By the sprinkling of the divine Blood of thy Son poured forth, O pure one, wash away the sores of my heart, that I may magnify thee and duly glorify thee, O ever-blessed and immaculate one!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Higher than the heavenly ranks wast thou revealed, having borne on earth God the Word Who came in the flesh, O all-pure one; and by His suffering and precious Cross He led all up unto heaven by His goodness!

*Katavasia:* Cut off from the shore was the sea of old by the rod, and Israel passed through as through a wilderness, the path prefiguring the Cross; therefore let us sing a song unto our marvelous God, for He hath been glorified![^285]

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* Thou confirmation of those who put their trust in Thee: confirm, O Lord, Thy Church which Thou hast acquired [p248LA] with Thy precious Blood!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Inconfusedly of one essence according to hypostasis, the Word endured the crucifixion and dishonor, for those who honor Thy Passion...

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* We were freed from the earth and from the primal curse, for Thou didst take the curse upon Thyself, O Master, and by the Cross didst pour forth renewal!

*Theotokion (Cross):* Have mercy on me, O only merciful one, who didst ineffably bear the all-merciful God: deliver me from all stumbling-blocks, O all-pure one!

*Another Heirmos of the Mother of God: Mystically Thy...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* From my manifold sins deliver me, O Sovereign Lady, and snatch me away by thy prayers, O all-pure one, fervently praying unto thy Son to have mercy on me!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Many temptations have compassed me about, and heavy burdens have weighed me down; and unto thee do I come, O all-pure one, have mercy on me!

*Staurotheotokion:* Beholding Christ lifted up upon the Cross, the Mother who knew not wedlock weeping said: How hath the Sun of glory set from mine eyes?

*Troparion 4:* Having voluntarily endured wounds and slaughter, O Christ: heal my soul wounded by thievish thoughts and afflicted by demons, through the prayers of her who bore Thee, O Most-merciful One!

*Katavasia:* Mystically did Habakkuk foresee Thy fearsome dispensation, O Lord, saying: Power and strength are Thine; I heard and was astonished; I considered Thy works and glorified Thee, O Lover of mankind![^286]

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* The heavens have been covered by Thy virtue, O Christ; for having come forth from the ark of Thy holy and incorrupt Mother, in the temple of Thy glory Thou wast revealed as an infant carried in arms, and all things were filled [p249LA] with Thy praise!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Beholding the fall of Adam, Thou didst become the New Adam, O Christ; alone wast Thou lifted up upon the Cross; Thou didst have Thy hands and feet nailed, and didst taste vinegar and gall, by reason of Thy tender compassion!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* As a sheep to the slaughter the Prophet sees Thee, and as a blameless lamb, O Word of God, led without guile, voluntarily enduring the crucifixion of Thine own will, that Thou mightest save him who had fallen, by reason of Thy tender compassion!

*Theotokion (Cross):* As the Mother of God we entreat thee: pray unto Christ Who was born of thee, with the bodiless [powers], with Apostles and Prophets, with Hierarchs and Martyrs, ever to give peace unto the world and save our souls!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Heirmos: Unseen on earth...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Stretch forth thine all-pure hands, O Sovereign Lady, and pray unto thine Only-begotten Son, that we perish not evilly, held fast by sinful temptations!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Shatter, O all-pure one, the counsels of the opposing enemies that fiercely assail thy flock, and grant victories unto our Orthodox princes!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* The Father's Word, beyond words, didst thou bear, Who frees mankind from unreason; Him do thou fervently pray, O only Virgin, to deliver me who am stricken by unreasonable passions!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* As a scroll did the Prophet foresee thee of old, O Maiden, wherein by the Father's finger was written the incarnate Word, tearing up the ancestral handwriting of our debt, O all-pure one!

*Katavasia:* Unseen on earth was He seen, and having voluntarily lived among men, the Incomprehensible One: rising early unto Thee[^287] [p250LA] we hymn Thee, O Lover of mankind!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* When Isaiah saw symbolically upon an exalted throne God receiving glorious gifts from the Angels, he cried: Woe is me! For I have foreseen God becoming incarnate and ruling over the unwaning day of light!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Having kindled Thy flesh as a lamp upon the Cross, O Compassionate One, and having sought the lost drachma, Thou didst call together Thy friends, the heavenly hosts, at its finding: we glorify Thy dominion, O Christ our God!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* Behold marvelous wonders, all ye people, and be glad now in spirit, hymning Christ Who hath granted unto us the manifestation of His Cross as a priceless treasure!

*Theotokion (Cross):* Crucified upon the Tree and pierced with a lance in the side, He Who of old fashioned Eve: beholding Him, maternally cried the all-pure Virgin: How dost Thou die, O Source of righteousness?

*Another Heirmos: He Who with Angels...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Unto thee have I fled, O all-pure Sovereign Lady, held fast by incurable sins; grant me forgiveness of my countless evils!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* I have fallen among robbers, O corrupt sin of my soul; stretch forth thy hands unto me, O Sovereign Lady, and lead me back unto repentance!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* O Virgin Mother of God, pray unto thy Son with the Prophets, Apostles, and Martyrs, to deliver me from future torments, O Mother of Christ God!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Offering thee now as intercessor and suppliant unto Him Who was born of thee, being full of every sin, O Virgin my surety: be thou also the correction of my life![^288]

[p251LA] and lead me unto the cultivation of beloved understanding!

*Katavasia (Ode 5):* He Who from the Angels is unutterable hath appeared, a Sun from the virginal womb as a Son, granting the illumination of divine knowledge unto those that wandered in darkness!

*Ode 6, Tone 3. Heirmos:* The Elder, having seen with his eyes the salvation that was come from God unto the peoples, cried out:[^289] Christ, Thou art my God!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Of Thine own will wast Thou slain as a gentle lamb, and him that had died through the tree, of Thine own will Thou didst lead back unto life, O Christ!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* The Dead One hath awakened Life; the Comely One hath approached unto the holy place; for the grievously diseased a healing hath been opened, and the day of salvation hath drawn nigh unto all!

*Theotokion (Cross):* Creation cannot comprehend the mystery that is beyond it; but keep silence, O Virgin: beyond understanding dost thou give birth unto God!

*Another Canon (Theotokos). Ode 6. Heirmos: Draw me up...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Into the sinful abyss have I fallen of my countless stumbling-blocks and foul deeds; but stretch forth thy right hand, O all-pure Sovereign Lady, and raise me up, ere my heart perishes!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* I have been deadened by sinful passions, and they drown my soul; but unto me who flee unto thy help, as unto Peter from the deep, draw me up, O all-pure one!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* Beholding Him dead, the Word Who was incarnate of thy blood, O all-hymned one, maternally weeping thou didst say: Thou art magnified as the Life of all, O Virgin Mother and Sovereign Lady!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Weeping and wailing I cry: Woe is me! whenever I remember the dread judgment seat, for I have acquired evil deeds; O thou that knewest not man, [p252LA] Virgin Mother of God, in the dread day be my Protectress!

*Katavasia (Ode 6):* Draw me up from the abyss, Jonah cried; and do Thou deliver me, O Savior, from the depths of my many evils, and guide me unto the light of Thy commandments!

*Kontakion of the Saint, if there is one; but if not, the Kontakion of the Cross, Tone 4:* O Thou Who of Thine own will wast lifted up upon the Cross, O Christ God, grant Thy compassions unto Thy new city named after Thee; gladden by Thy power our faithful prince,[^290] giving him victories over adversaries, having Thy weapon as an alliance of peace, an invincible trophy!

*Ode 7, Tone 3. Heirmos:* The children blessed Thee Who didst dew the furnace, and hymning God the Word Who took up His abode in the incorrupt Virgin, devoutly praising Him, we sing: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* As the cypress, the pine, and the cedar of old were lifted up, O only Son of the Trinity, Thou hast raised us up who had fallen into the abyss of error, who devoutly sing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* Earthly things rejoice with the heavenly, and the multitude of all the saints is glad; the princes of the Church feast richly in this celebration of the manifestation of Thy divine Cross today, devoutly singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Theotokion (Cross):* Foreshadowing thy childbearing, O pure one, and thine ineffable offspring, seeing the only Master incarnate and crucified in the flesh, praising Him we sing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Another Canon (Theotokos). Ode 7. Heirmos: The rod of fire beyond understanding...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Having thee alone as helper, [p253LA] O all-pure one, we thy sinful servants flee unto thee, and unceasingly cry out, asking remission of countless sins!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Leave me not to perish in sins, darkened by the multitude of mine evils; but as thou art compassionate, save me, and grant me tears of repentance!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* Born of the Virgin, He Who delivered us from corruption hath taken upon Himself our life; therefore we cry unto Thee: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* The judgment is at the doors, the tribunal is ready; make ready, my wretched soul, and cry out: When Thou comest to judge, O Word, condemn me not, for the sake of her that gave Thee birth!

*Katavasia (Ode 7):* The rod of fire beyond understanding prefigured thy childbearing; for the dry rod budded, and in the furnace a cool dew went forth unto those who faithfully sang: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Ode 8, Tone 3. Heirmos:* Uniting in the irresistible fire, the godly youths overcame the flame unharmed, singing the divine hymn: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord![^291]

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* A disobedient and untoward people refused to submit, and judged Thee to be crucified and put to death; but Thou, having died of Thine own will, didst bring to life the dead who sing unto Thee, O Christ, throughout all ages!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* Thou didst stretch forth Thy palms upon the Cross, healing the first-formed man who stretched forth his hands unrestrained unto the shaded tree, O Master; beholding this in fear, the sun hid its rays, and all creation was shaken!

*Theotokion (Cross):* Higher than the heavens hast thou been shown, an abode...

[p254LA] *Another Canon (Theotokos). Ode 8. Heirmos: To serve the living God...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Subject to passions and darkness of sin, O all-pure one, having boldness, pray for us wretched ones, that we may be delivered from eternal fire!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Earnestly we fall down before thee, O pure one, now thy sinful servants, knowing thine ineffable mercy, that thou mayest deliver us, O Sovereign Lady, from all tribulations, who cry: Blessed is the God of our fathers!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* As of old Thou didst deliver the Prophet from the belly of the sea-monster, O divine and all-glorious Word, deliver my soul that hath fallen into the pit of destruction, through the constant supplications of her that knew not wedlock and gave birth unto Thee!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Together with the Prophets and Apostles, O pure one, and with the Martyrs and all the Saints, pray earnestly for us sinners, that He may have mercy upon our souls, who cry: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Katavasia (Ode 8):* To serve the living God, the children of old in Babylon endured, taking no heed of the tormenting instruments; standing in the midst of the flame, they sang: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord!

*Ode 9, Tone 3. Heirmos:* In the law, in the shadow and the scriptures, we the faithful behold an image: every male that opens the womb is holy unto God; therefore the firstborn Word of the unoriginate Father, the firstborn Son of a Mother who knew not man, we magnify![^292]

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Thou wast nailed upon the Tree, O Christ, Who didst establish the earth upon nothing; I have sunk into the mire of sin of mine own will, but as Thou art good and compassionate, raise me up, O greatly Merciful One!

[p255LA] *Troparion 2 (Cross):* The army of the Saints, the chosen flock, the early-blooming flowers blossoming from the Tree of Life of Christ, the honorable assembly of passion-bearers: ye are manifested as glorious of Christ God!

*Theotokion (Cross):* The Word of the Father, beyond word becoming incarnate, thou didst bear, O Mother of God; we hymn and bless thy majesty, O Mother of God!

*Another Canon (Theotokos). Ode 9. Heirmos: Poured out...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* With the drops of compassion wash me, O all-pure one, pouring upon my wounds the oil of mercy and the wine of repentance; heal the wounds of all mine evils, as earnestly in hymns I magnify thee, O pure one!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* With the Angels and Archangels, O pure one, and the whole choir of Prophets, Apostles, and Martyrs with the Venerable, pray, O Sovereign Lady, to cast down our enemies, to forgive our sins, and to establish peace, O pure one!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* May I be delivered today by thy prayers, O blameless Virgin, from all wrath and deadly passions, and from the cruel generation of fire, from unrighteous men and from cruel enemies; I flee beneath thy protection, calling upon thee for help!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Unto thy protection, O pure one, we thy sinful servants now fall down, asking forgiveness, for we have no other helper in tribulation save thee, O all-pure one!

*Katavasia (Ode 9): Ineffably, O Virgin, a shadowed paradise... More honorable than the Cherubim...*

*Exaposteilarion of the Cross, Tone 3. Podoben: "An Invincible Weapon" (Оружіе непобѣдимое):* An invincible weapon and confirmation of the faith, the divine Cross we hymn, and we venerate it in faith: the great refuge of Christians and boast, the glory of the Orthodox, and the salvation of the faithful![^293]

*Staurotheotokion, Tone 3. Podoben: "Standing by the Cross" (Предстоящи у креста):* Standing by the Cross, O all-pure one, and seeing thy Son upon the Tree, [p256LA] she wailed, crying out: Woe is me, my sweet Sun! How hath Thy comeliness set, full of sweet fragrance, which adorned human nature?

*Aposticha Stichera of the Cross, Tone 3:*

*Sticheron 1 (Cross):* The tree of disobedience brought forth death for the whole world; but the tree of the Cross poured forth life and incorruption. Therefore we bow down before God Who was crucified, that the light of Thy countenance may be marked upon us, O Lord!

*Sticheron 2 (Cross):* Through the tree we came to know death, and through the Cross we found life again, by the prayer of Thy mercy; for having slain mine enemy, Thou didst not turn away from me, but dost save me and lead me into glory; O Redeemer, glory to Thee!

*Martyricon:* Prophets, Apostles, and Martyrs of Christ taught us to sing the consubstantial Trinity, and enlightened the deceived nations, and made the sons of men partakers with the Angels!

*Glory... Both now... Theotokion:* Without seed thou didst conceive of the Holy Spirit, and glorifying we sing unto thee: Rejoice, O all-pure Virgin!

*Wednesday Liturgy. The Beatitudes, Tone 3:*

*Troparion 1 (Beatitudes):* When our forefather Adam rejected Thy commandment, O Christ, Thou didst drive him out of Paradise; but the thief who of old confessed Thee upon the Cross, Thou didst establish therein, as he cried: Remember me in Thy Kingdom![^294]

*Troparion 2 (Beatitudes):* Thou wast crucified for my sake, and pierced in Thy side by a lance, pouring forth two streams for our salvation; saved thereby, we sing unto Thee, O Good One, and glorify Thy compassion: Remember me, O Savior, when Thou comest in Thy Kingdom!

*Troparion 3 (Beatitudes):* Numbered with the transgressors, Thou didst take upon Thyself the sins of all, O Jesus, [p257LA] and wast crowned with thorns as King of all, uprooting and cutting down the sins of the forefather, O Christ; therefore now in faith we glorify Thy sufferings!

*Martyricon (Beatitudes):* Having been likened unto the sufferings of Christ, O sufferers, all-glorious Martyrs, ye cast down the enemy's deceit by divine help and received heavenly glory; pray for us all, O glorious Martyrs!

*Glory... (Trinity):* The Father and the Son let us glorify, and the Holy Spirit, saying: O Holy Trinity, save our souls!

*Both now... (Theotokion):* Beholding upon the Cross Him Whom thou didst incarnate of thy blood, O pure Mother of God: What is this, O Child, that the deceitful assembly hath rendered unto Thee, to put Thee to death Who art the Life and Resurrection of all?

*Liturgy Prokeimenon. Song of the Theotokos, Tone 3:* My soul doth magnify the Lord, and my spirit hath rejoiced in God my Savior. *Verse:* For He hath regarded the lowliness of His handmaiden; for behold, from henceforth all generations shall call me blessed.

*The Reading from the Epistle of the Holy Apostle Paul to the Galatians (Gal. 4:22–31):*

Brethren, Abraham had two sons, the one by a bondmaid, the other by a freewoman. But he who was of the bondwoman was born after the flesh; but he of the freewoman was by promise. Which things are an allegory: for these are the two covenants; the one from Mount Sinai, which genders to bondage, which is Hagar. For this Hagar is Mount Sinai in Arabia, and answers to Jerusalem which now is, and is in bondage with her children. But Jerusalem which is above is free, which is the mother of us all. For it is written, Rejoice, thou barren that bearest not; break forth and cry, thou that travailest not: for the desolate hath many more children than she which hath an husband.[^295] Now we, brethren, as Isaac was, are the children of promise. But as then he that was born after the flesh persecuted him that was born after the Spirit, even so it is now. Nevertheless what says [p258LA] the scripture? Cast out the bondwoman and her son: for the son of the bondwoman shall not be heir with the son of the freewoman. So then, brethren, we are not children of the bondwoman, but of the free. Stand fast therefore in the liberty wherewith Christ hath made us free.

*Alleluia, Tone 4:* Give ear, O daughter, and see, and incline thine ear. *Verse:* The rich among the people shall entreat thy face.

*The Reading from the Holy Gospel according to Luke (Luke 10:38–42; 11:27–28):*

At that time, Jesus entered into a certain village: and a certain woman named Martha received Him into her house. And she had a sister called Mary, which also sat at Jesus' feet, and heard His word. But Martha was cumbered about much serving, and came to Him, and said, Lord, dost Thou not care that my sister hath left me to serve alone? bid her therefore that she help me. And Jesus answered and said unto her, Martha, Martha, thou art careful and troubled about many things: but one thing is needful: and Mary hath chosen that good part, which shall not be taken away from her. And it came to pass, as He spake these things, a certain woman of the company lifted up her voice, and said unto Him, Blessed is the womb that bare Thee, and the paps which Thou hast sucked. But He said, Yea rather, blessed are they that hear the word of God, and keep it.

## WEDNESDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR THURSDAY)

*On Wednesday Evening (Thursday Vespers), on "Lord, I have cried", set 6 stichera: for the Apostles and Saint Nicholas, on 6; we sing stichera in Tone 4.*

*Podoben: "Being called from on high" (Зва́нный свы́ше):*

*Sticheron 1 (Apostle Peter):* Named mighty for the Kingdom, the summit and beauty, O most blessed Apostle Peter, the immovable foundation of the faith of Christ, bearing upon thyself the spiritual Church, whereof He said: The gates of hades shall not prevail against it; for He hath established it upon the rock[^296] [p259LA] of thy confession, O Peter, and given thee authority in heaven and on earth to bind and to loose sins; therefore I pray thee: loose me also from all evils!

*Sticheron 2 (Apostle Peter):* The Master hath given thee as an eminent luminary unto the universe, O most blessed Peter, shining with divine brightness, and giving thee authority in heaven and on earth to bind and to loose sins; therefore look also upon me, the wretched one, who am grievously afflicted; remember not my countless evils, that I may hymn with rejoicing thy most holy dormition, so that through thee I may obtain from Christ eternal heavenly delights!

*Sticheron 3 (Apostle Peter):* Christ hath appointed thee as a fountain and a physician in all the universe, O blessed Peter, healing the sick with thy shadow and driving away demons and all manner of passions with words; therefore falling down before thee now in thy most radiant memory, we all entreat cleansing from all evils; remember not my countless sins, but being delivered by thy prayers, make us worthy of the Heavenly Kingdom!

*Other Stichera to the Saint (Nicholas), in the same Tone.*

*Podoben: "Being called from on high" (Зва́нный свы́ше):*

*Sticheron 1 (Saint Nicholas):* O Father Nicholas, the shrine of thy relics pours forth myrrh, enriching the people of Myra; in which, appearing in a dream unto the emperor by thine apparition, thou didst deliver those bound and unjustly condemned from death and from the executioner;[^297] and now, even as then, ever manifest thyself by thine apparition, praying for our souls!

*Sticheron 2 (Saint Nicholas):* Gazing steadfastly upon the height of understanding, thou didst behold the unsearchable depth of wisdom; [p260LA] with thy teachings thou hast enriched the whole world, O Nicholas; ever interceding on our behalf, pray that our souls may be saved!

*Sticheron 3 (Saint Nicholas):* O man of God, faithful servant and steward of His mysteries, and man of spiritual desires, an image of the Spirit: as a divine treasure the Church of Myra found and acquired thee, and an intercessor for our souls!

*Theotokion:* Deliver us from our tribulations, O Mother of Christ God, who didst bear the Creator of all, that we all may cry unto thee: Rejoice, thou only salvation of our souls!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 4:* Save me, O God, by Thy name, and judge me by Thy strength. *Verse:* Hear my prayer, O God; give ear unto the words of my mouth.

*On the Aposticha, Stichera of the Apostles, Tone 4:*

*Sticheron 1 (Apostles):* The choir of the Apostles Thou didst enlighten by the Holy Spirit, and washed away the bodily defilement of our sins; O God, have mercy on us!

*Sticheron 2 (Apostles):* Unlettered disciples the Spirit of Thy truth manifested, O Christ God, and the deceit of the eloquent nations Thou didst abolish; O Almighty Lord, glory to Thee!

*Sticheron 3 (Apostles):* Glorified in the memory of Thy Saints, O Christ God, therefore we pray Thee: grant also unto us great mercy!

*Theotokion:* Having thee, O Mother of God, as helper of our hope, we fear not the snares of the enemy, for thou dost shelter our souls!

*At "God is the Lord", Dismissal Troparion of the Apostles, Tone 3:* Holy Apostles, intercede with the merciful God, that He may grant unto our souls remission of transgressions!

*Dismissal Troparion of Saint Nicholas, Tone 4:* A rule of faith and a model of meekness, a teacher of temperance, hath the truth of things revealed thee unto thy flock; therefore through humility hast thou acquired high things,[^298] [p261LA] and through poverty riches, O Father our Nicholas; intercede with Christ God that He may save our souls!

*Theotokion:* The Word of God our Father, Who was incarnate of thee, we know, O Virgin Mother of God, only pure and only blessed one; therefore unceasingly singing of thee we magnify thee.

*After the First Stichology, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*

*Sessional Hymn (Apostles):* As first-enthroned of the Apostles and teachers of the universe, entreat the Master of all to grant peace unto the universe, and unto our souls great mercy!

*Another Sessional Hymn (Apostles):* Even as Moses guided Israel[^299] from bondage and through the Red Sea by Thy right hand, O Most High, and drowned Pharaoh: so by miracles Thine elect disciples cleaved the sea of evil unbelief, and guided the people unto Thee, O Word without beginning, only Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion:* Thee we magnify, O Virgin, crying: Thou art the mountain from which the stone was cut without hands, and shattered the gates of hades!

*After the Second Stichology, Sessional Hymn, Tone 4:*

*Sessional Hymn 1 (Apostles):* Preachers of the faith, Thy disciples, O Christ, Thou hast revealed unto the whole world, guiding the nations unto Thy divine knowledge, enlightened by rays of theology; and having driven away the darkness of ignorance by faith, through their prayers save our souls!

*Sessional Hymn 2 (Apostles):* Luminaries unto the ends of the earth Thou hast revealed Thy disciples, O Christ, shining forth preaching, enlightening our souls, and having destroyed the deceit of idols, enlightening the whole world with pious teaching; by their prayers save our souls!

*Martyricon:* Thy Martyrs, O Lord, in their suffering received crowns [p262LA] of incorruption from Thee, our God. For having Thy strength, they cast down the torturers, and shattered the feeble might of idols; by their prayers save our souls!

*Theotokion:* Nurtured in the temple in the Holy of Holies, clothed with faith and wisdom, and having preserved undefiled virginity, the Archistrategos Gabriel brought greeting from heaven: Rejoice, thou full of grace; rejoice, O most glorious one, the Lord is with thee!

*On Thursday, Canon to the Apostles on 4, and to Saint Nicholas on 4. Canon of the Apostles, Tone 4. Ode 1. Heirmos:*

The choirs of Israel with unwet feet, traversing the deep of the Red Sea, and beholding the three-tiered captains, their enemies, drowned, sang with gladness: Let us sing unto our God, for He hath been glorified!

*Troparion 1 (Apostles):* By communion of the light of the immaterial Mind, O all-glorious fathers, spiritually enlightened, bright disciples, ye also enlighten the whole universe without deceit, destroying the deceptive darkness by the radiance of your preaching, O glorious Apostles!

*Troparion 2 (Apostles):* Having chosen for yourselves the fairest of all, the obedience of Christ, ye follow after Him in greatness of soul, pouring forth rays of light from Him most radiantly; by goodness have ye enlightened the world, O holy Apostles!

*Theotokion:* He that is uncircumscribed in the bosom of the Father is bodily nourished in the bosom of a Mother; and after childbirth having preserved the pure Virgin beyond understanding, unto Him let us sing in harmony, unto our God, for He hath been glorified!

*Another Canon to Saint Nicholas. Ode 1. Heirmos:* The mighty captains[^300] [p263LA] *Troparion 1 (Saint Nicholas):* Having inherited the sorrowless life, O blessed one, and ever enjoying spiritual joy, drive away all sorrow, I pray thee, from my soul, that with rejoicing I may glorify thee, O holy Father Nicholas!

*Troparion 2 (Saint Nicholas):* When the deep of corruptible life and every tribulation overflows, O father, those who ever run unto thee find a pilot; unto calm dost thou turn them, I see, O blessed one, by thy prayers!

*Troparion 3 (Saint Nicholas):* With halting tongue and lips, I have come to offer small praise and supplication unto thy godlike greatness, O Nicholas; now, as a godlike giver, grant that the Savior and God be merciful unto me!

*Theotokion:* Possessing unsleeping prayer, O Pure One, lull to sleep our soulish passions by thy holy intercessions, and grant divine vigilance for the crushing of the devil!

*Katavasia:* O Thou Who wast born of the Virgin, drown the three parts of my soul, I pray Thee, in the depth of dispassion, as of old the mighty captains, that in the mortification of the body, as upon a timbrel, I may sing unto Thee a triumphal song!

*Ode 3. Canon of the Apostles. Heirmos:*

For the barren Church of the Gentiles hath borne many children, and the assembly of seven is grown feeble; let us sing unto our marvelous God: Holy art Thou, O Lord!

*Troparion 1 (Apostles):* Come, let us honor in songs and bless as disciples of Christ the stewards of understanding, who established the Church upon the faithful Rock!

*Troparion 2 (Apostles):* Appearing as lightning unto the universe, O most glorious disciples, ye made it radiant, and drove away the deceitful darkness[^301] [p264LA] by the divine command!

*Theotokion:* Human minds cannot declare thy childbirth that is beyond understanding, O Virgin; for ineffably in thy womb hast thou conceived the Word of the primal Mind, O pure Mother of God!

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Ode 3. Heirmos:* Not in wisdom...

*Troparion 1 (Saint Nicholas):* Manifest a weapon cutting down adversary foes, and preserve us unharmed from their temptations, doing the will of God!

*Troparion 2 (Saint Nicholas):* Heal the contriteness of my soul, O Hierarch Nicholas, and deliver me from the enemy's temptations and assaults, as the protector and defender of those who faithfully glorify thee!

*Troparion 3 (Saint Nicholas):* Appearing visibly in dream, thou dost most gloriously deliver the youths from death, crying unto the emperor: Do not wrong the three men falsely accused through bitter envy!

*Theotokion:* Thee as protectress, O all-pure Virgin, have I acquired; deliver us from tribulation that brings forth death!

*Katavasia:* Not in wisdom, nor in power, nor in riches do we boast, but in Thine hypostatic Wisdom, O Father, for there is none holy beyond Thee, O Lover of mankind!

*Ode 4. Canon of the Apostles. Heirmos:*

For the sake of the love of Thine image, O Merciful One, Thou didst stand upon Thy Cross, and the nations melted away; for Thou art our God, our strength and our praise!

*Troparion 1 (Apostles):* Let us bless the all-wise Apostles, who instructed earthly men unto divine understanding, O divinely wise teachers!

*Troparion 2 (Apostles):* Lawgivers of piety, ye instruct men with understanding through faith; innocent disciples, ye were shown forth as all-praised; therefore we bless you!

*Theotokion:* O Undefiled Virgin[^302]

[p265LA] glory of Apostles, adornment of passion-bearers, with them pray that He may save us who faithfully praise thee!

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Ode 4. Heirmos:* He that sits in glory...

*Troparion 1 (Saint Nicholas):* Thy glorious name everywhere we glorify through glorious divine miracles, O adornment and praise of hierarchs, who honor thee with joyful songs!

*Troparion 2 (Saint Nicholas):* O thou who dwellest in the heavens, rejoice, Nicholas! Invisibly in the midst of all that call upon thee, come and lighten the afflictions of our souls, and grant us calm!

*Troparion 3 (Saint Nicholas):* Everywhere thou gushest forth healings unto those running with faith to thee, and deliverest from plagues; therefore turn our sorrow into joy for thy servants, by thine acceptable prayers, O most glorious Nicholas!

*Theotokion:* The angelic hosts marvel, O Pure One, at thy divine childbirth, and hymn and magnify it; with them, O Virgin, save those who magnify thee in faith!

*Katavasia:* He that sits in glory...

*Ode 5. Canon of the Apostles. Heirmos:*

Send down enlightenment upon us, O Lord, and loose the vapour of sinful gloom, O Good One, and grant us Thy peace, O Lover of mankind!

*Troparion 1 (Apostles):* Branches of the living vine, mystically offering unto the faithful the clusters of divine sweetness: let us praise the Apostles, truly of God!

*Troparion 2 (Apostles):* Ever contending against the night of ignorance that envelopes all creation, O wise ones, bearing the radiant word, ye enlighten it and drive away the darkness!

*Theotokion:* O unwedded Virgin, joy of angels, gladness of martyrs and apostles, unceasingly pray for us all, to save those who sing unto Christ[^303] [p266LA] our God!

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Ode 5. Heirmos:* The ungodly shall not see Thy glory...

*Troparion 1 (Saint Nicholas):* Having set like the sun, thou didst shine forth with the radiant rays of thy miracles, enlightening the whole world under the sun with wonders![^304]

*Troparion 2 (Saint Nicholas):* O Hierarch Nicholas, come unto us in the day of the onset of trials and sorrow, and comfort all our sufferings by the grace of the Spirit that dwells in thee!

*Troparion 3 (Saint Nicholas):* Nourisher of widows, and father of orphans, most excellent helper in afflictions, comfort of them that weep, shepherd and guide unto all that wander: O Nicholas, deliver us from tribulations by thy prayers!

*Theotokion:* With noetic eyes the choir of the prophets, seeing the spiritual vision, cried unto us: Behold, Jesus God is about to be born of the Virgin Bride of God, for the renewal of men!

*Katavasia:* The ungodly shall not see Thy glory, O Christ; but we, rising early at night, sing of Thee, the Only-Begotten, the Father's radiance of God's glory, O Lover of mankind!

*Ode 6. Canon of the Apostles. Heirmos:*

Prefiguring the three-day burial, the Prophet Jonah prayed in the whale: Deliver me from corruption, O Jesus, King of powers!

*Troparion 1 (Apostles):* Having received in your hands the commandments of Christ, O all-wise ones, ye uprooted the thorns of unbelief and planted steadfast faith in the souls of the faithful!

*Troparion 2 (Apostles):* The Lord sends you forth in the midst of wolves as lambs, and thereby subjecting blasphemous deceit unto sheep-like meekness, O glorious Apostles

[p267LA] *Theotokion:* O Most Holy Mother of God, praise of Apostles and Martyrs, ineffable adornment of Prophets, with them pray, to deliver us from all affliction!

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Ode 6. Heirmos:* I came into the depths...

*Troparion 1 (Saint Nicholas):* Strengthen us by thy power, O divinely wise one, that we may be able to destroy the visible enemy, from whose grievous harm deliver us by thy prayers, O Nicholas!

*Troparion 2 (Saint Nicholas):* From all oppression and violence and harm of evil men deliver us, by thy holy prayers, O most glorious Nicholas, deliver us!

*Troparion 3 (Saint Nicholas):* With noetic eye foreseeing things to come, thou fillest all ends of the earth with teachings; thou didst announce unto us the Son consubstantial with the Father, and didst abolish the fury of Arius, establishing thy venerable achievements as a pillar of the Orthodox faith!

*Theotokion:* Thy people and city entreat thee, O Mother of God: deliver us from every temptation, O common Mother, all-pure Lady, and from eternal condemnation!

*Katavasia:* I came into the depths of the sea, and a storm of many transgressions hath overwhelmed me; but descending as God, Thou hast brought up my soul, O Most Merciful One!

*Kontakion of the Apostles, Tone 2:* The steadfast and divinely-speaking preachers, the summit of Thy disciples, O Lord, Thou hast received into the enjoyment of Thy good things and repose; for Thou didst accept their labors and death above every whole burnt-offering, O Thou Who alone knowest the secrets of the heart!

*Ode 7. Canon of the Apostles. Heirmos:*

The children of Abraham of old in Babylon, trampling upon the flame of the furnace, rejoiced, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers![^305]

[p268LA] *Troparion 1 (Apostles):* Having humbled the pride of the haughty enemy, ye cast it down, O Apostles of the Lord, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion 2 (Apostles):* Instructed by Christ through the words of dispensation unto the holy mystery, ye taught the nations of grace, singing, O Apostles: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:* Thou art the Orient of the noetic Sun, having appeared as Mother, and thou enlightenest all, O all-glorious Virgin Mother of God!

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Ode 7. Heirmos:* Unto the youths...

*Troparion 1 (Saint Nicholas):* Entreat the Creator of all, Who alone rests in the Saints, to cleanse us and to send forth His rich mercies!

*Troparion 2 (Saint Nicholas):* Righteous and meek, gentle and humble wast thou, O Father; and unto height wast thou most gloriously exalted, O Hierarch, working healings and miracles!

*Troparion 3 (Saint Nicholas):* Having delivered of old the military commanders from death, thou didst raise up their groaning to sing and glorify Christ the Savior with fervent faith, who cried aloud: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Theotokion:* Having calmed the turmoil and passions of my soul by thy vigilant prayer, O Virgin, grant me peace, that I may glorify thee, O Lady!

*Katavasia:* The three youths in Babylon, turning the tyrant's command into foolishness, standing in the midst of the flames, sang: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Ode 8. Canon of the Apostles. Heirmos:*

Bless the Lord, all ye works of God and all creation; ye holy and humble of heart, sing and highly exalt Him!

*Troparion 1 (Apostles):* Appointed by God as teachers throughout the whole universe, O holy Apostles, ye utterly destroyed the prince of darkness and guided the people unto knowledge of God!

*Troparion 2 (Apostles):* Mystically sounding as pipes the songs of the Lord, O wise ones, ye were revealed, sweetening the hearts of the faithful[^306] [p269LA] by the Spirit, highly exalting Christ unto ages of ages!

*Theotokion:* O urn that contained the divine manna, O pure one, in songs we faithful cry: Rejoice, thou full of grace, Mother, praise of Apostles and Martyrs!

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Ode 8. Heirmos:* Unto the Redeemer...

*Troparion 1 (Saint Nicholas):* Eloquently proclaiming divine things, O Nicholas, thou didst gloriously stop the mouths of the lawless, and deliver from the grievous malice of Arius, singing gloriously: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, and sing!

*Troparion 2 (Saint Nicholas):* The order of Patriarchs and Apostles, the assembly of Hieromartyrs, and the choir of Prophets, the whole Christian multitude, bless thy divine life; therefore we also cry aloud with thee, praising: Bless the Lord!

*Troparion (Holy Trinity):* In Orthodox manner let us worship the undivided Trinity, crying unto the Father, and to the Son, and to the Holy Spirit: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and highly exalt Him!

*Theotokion:* Make me worthy of thy mercy, O Mother that didst bear God, blessed and pure one, in the day of judgment; protect and deliver me from condemnation, O most holy protectress, that we may praise and bless thee!

*Katavasia:* O almighty Redeemer of all, having descended into the midst of the flame, Thou didst refresh them that believed, and taught them to sing: Bless the Lord, all ye works of the Lord, sing and highly exalt Him!

*Ode 9. Canon of the Apostles. Heirmos:*

Thy childbirth was revealed as incorrupt: God came forth from thy womb, appearing in the flesh, and dwelt on earth with men; therefore, O Mother of God, we all magnify thee!

*Troparion 1 (Apostles):* In the midst of the judgment-seat ye boldly proclaimed God, O wise Apostles, trampling down godless deception; and as gracious victors, ye were revealed glorious upon earth[^307] [p270LA] *Troparion 2 (Apostles):* Truly beautiful were the feet of the holy Apostles, scribes of peace unto all the world, richly proclaiming good tidings, whom unceasingly we magnify!

*Theotokion:* Having come forth from the Father's bosom, the Word was received in thy womb without seed, O Virgin; Him the assembly of Apostles and Martyrs sing, and in the Spirit unceasingly we magnify thee!

*Canon to Saint Nicholas. Ode 9. Heirmos:* Eve indeed by the disease of disobedience...

*Troparion 1 (Saint Nicholas):* Ever doth the shrine of thy grace, O venerable one, pour forth healings unto the faithful who come with love; O Nicholas, thou deliverest from every disease and temptation; therefore we all bless thee!

*Troparion 2 (Saint Nicholas):* Meditating upon the dread day and hour, O my soul, when thou must come unto the judgment to be judged according to secret deeds, cry unto the Savior: Save me by the prayers of Nicholas, Thy favored servant!

*Troparion 3 (Saint Nicholas):* Let all the ends of the earth unceasingly sing in hymns, adorning the head of Nicholas with crowns of praise, as the favored servant of Christ our God, by whose prayers we are delivered from afflictions!

*Theotokion:* Bringing unto thee the divine voice, Gabriel joyfully cries aloud with it unto thee: Rejoice, thou that hast in the midst the paradise Tree of Life! Rejoice, noetic palace! Rejoice, O undefiled Virgin!

*Katavasia:* It is truly meet... More honorable than the Cherubim...

*Exaposteilarion, Tone 2, Podoben: "Women, give ear" (Жены услышите):*

Peter and Paul, Andrew and James, Bartholomew and Matthew, James and Philip, and Thomas, and Simon, Luke, John, and Mark, all the Apostles of the Lord, let us praise in faith!

*Theotokion:* O Unwedded Virgin, unceasingly entreat Christ with them[^308] [p271LA] and the wise Apostles, together with the choir of the disciples, and with wise Nicholas, to deliver from all tribulations those who have placed their hope in thee, O Maiden!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Apostles, Tone 4:*

*Tone 4. Aposticha Sticheron 1 (Apostles):* The apostolic choir Thou hast enlightened by the Holy Spirit; and having [cleansed] the sinful defilement... grant [merciful healing], O Good One!

*Sticheron 2 (Apostles):* The apostolic choir, Thy disciples, O Christ, like radiant clouds and unbeguiled stars, enlightened the universe with teaching and proclaimed the Trinity, teaching the faith of Paul: having lived soberly and godly of old, let us cast away all deceit of idols and ungodliness, as it is written!

*Martyricon:* Precious is the death of Thy saints, O Lord; for by sword and fire, and with contrite soul, they poured forth their blood, having hope in Thee to receive the reward of their labors; they endured and received from Thee, O Savior, great mercy!

*Theotokion:* O rekindling of the extinguished, O righteous throne, O all-pure Sovereign Lady, merciful salvation of our souls!

*On Thursday at the Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 4:*

*Troparion 1 (Beatitudes):* Because of the tree Adam was banished from Paradise, but through the tree of the Cross the thief took up his dwelling in Paradise. For the one, having tasted, [transgressed] the commandment of Him that made him; while the other, crucified together with Thee, confessed Thee, the hidden God: Remember me, O Savior![^309]

*Troparion 2 (Apostles):* Being spiritual sheep of the Shepherd and the Lamb, sent forth in the midst of wolves, ye were wise messengers by divine preaching, turning their fierceness into meekness [p272LA] calling out with unswerving mind: Remember me, O Savior, in Thy kingdom!

*Troparion 3 (Apostles):* Having traversed the ends of the earth, O disciples of the Savior, like radiant stars ye dispersed the gloom of deceit, and enlightened with the light of the universe those that were deceived; therefore we call you blessed, praying, O preachers of Christ: Entreat the Lord in our behalf!

*Martyricon:* Crushed by torments and scorched by material fire, ye burned up bitter, skin-flaying deceit, O blessed ones; and now ye pour forth streams of healings unto those who approach you with faith, warmly crying and shouting unto Christ: Remember us!

*Trinity:* The Father and the Son and the Holy Spirit in one accord let us glorify, worthily praying unto the Unity of the Godhead in three Persons, abiding unconfused, simple, undivided, and unapproachable, through Whom we have been delivered from torment!

*Theotokion:* Joy of the Angels and unfading crown of the passion-bearers, in truth thou art the Mother of God, only undefiled one; on these days ask for us deliverance from sins, who in faith sing to thee and cry aloud: Rejoice, O Mother of our Life!

*Prokeimenon: Their proclamation hath gone out into all the earth (LXX Ps. 18:5). Verse: The heavens declare the glory of God (LXX Ps. 18:2).*

*Epistle to the Corinthians:*

Brethren: God hath displayed us, the apostles, last, as it were appointed unto death; for we have been made a spectacle unto the world, both to angels and to men. We are fools for Christ’s sake, but ye are wise in Christ; we are weak,[^310] [p273LA] but ye are strong; ye are honorable, but we are despised. Even unto this present hour we both hunger, and thirst, and are naked, and are buffeted, and have no certain dwelling-place; and labor, working with our own hands: being reviled, we bless; being persecuted, we endure it; being defamed, we entreat: we are made as the filth of the world, and are the offscouring of all things unto this day. I write not these things to shame you, but as my beloved children I warn you. For though ye have ten thousand instructors in Christ, yet have ye not many fathers: for in Christ Jesus I have begotten you through the Gospel. Therefore I beseech you, be ye followers of me.

*Alleluia: The heavens declare the glory of God (LXX Ps. 18:2). Verse: Into all the earth (LXX Ps. 18:5).*

*Gospel according to Matthew:*

At that time: Jesus called unto Him His disciples, and gave them power against unclean spirits, to cast them out, and to heal all manner of sickness and all manner of disease. These Jesus sent forth, and commanded them, saying: Go not into the way of the Gentiles, and into any city of the Samaritans enter ye not: but go rather to the lost sheep of the house of Israel. And as ye go, preach, saying: The kingdom of heaven is at hand. Heal the sick, raise the dead, cast out devils: freely ye have received, freely give.

*Kinonikon: Their proclamation hath gone out into all the earth (LXX Ps. 18:5).*

## THURSDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR FRIDAY)

*On Thursday evening, suppliant Stichera: 3 to the Cross, and 3 to the Theotokos, Tone 6.*

*Podoben: "Having laid aside all earthly hope" (Все оупованіе·):*

*Tone 6.*

*Podoben: "Having laid aside all earthly hope" (Все оупованіе·):* [^311]

[p274LA] ...whereby corruption was abolished and brought to naught, and the power of death was swallowed up, and we were raised from earth unto heaven; invincible weapon, adversary of demons, glory of Martyrs, truly fair confirmation of the Monastics, haven of salvation, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Sticheron 2 (Cross):* Rejoice, O Cross of the Lord, whereby the curse of mankind was loosed, sign of joy unto them that exist, driving away foes by thine elevation, O all-honored helper unto us, might of kings, helper of the righteous, beauty of priests, delivering from severe misfortunes; rod wherewith we are shepherded, weapon of defense in fear, about which the Angels stand: divine glory of the Cross, granting great mercy unto the world!

*Sticheron 3 (Cross):* Rejoice, O Cross of Christ, guide of the blind, and physician of the infirm, resurrection of all the dead, who hast raised us up who fell into corruption; O precious Cross, whereby the curse was destroyed and incorruption blossomed, whereby we mortals were deified and the devil was utterly cast down! Today beholding thee lifted up on high by the hands of the hierarch, we exalt Him that was lifted up upon thee, and Him we worship, drawing forth rich and great mercy!

*Other Stichera to the Theotokos.*

*Podoben: "The same Tone and Podoben" (Гла́съ и҆ подо́бенъ то́йже):*

*Sticheron 1 (Theotokos):* Burdened with transgressions and tempest-tossed by thoughts, we fall down before thy love for mankind, O Sovereign Lady,[^312] [p275LA] receive the genuflection, the uplifting of hands, the cries and sobbing of the heart, and tears from our eyes; have mercy, as Mother of the Merciful One, and grant help, O Mother of God, all-undefiled Virgin!

*Sticheron 2 (Theotokos):* Turn not away from my mouth, cast me not away from thy face, who hope in thee, O all-pure Mother of God! Whither shall I go except unto thee to find salvation? Or who will deliver me from eternal fire except thee, O Sovereign Lady? Pray therefore unto thy Son, O all-undefiled helper!

*Sticheron 3 (Theotokos):* The deep of thy love for mankind, the abyss of goodness of thy compassions, and thine innumerable bounties, show forth, O All-pure One, upon me, the wretched; quell the sinful passions, grant me chastity, and keep me unharmed in soul and body, O all-undefiled Sovereign Lady!

*Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Thee hanged upon the Tree, O Christ, She that gave Thee birth cried aloud: What strange mystery is this which I behold, O Son? How dost Thou die upon the Tree, hanged in the flesh, O Life of all?

*At the Stichologia, Prokeimenon: My help comes from the Lord, Who made heaven and earth (LXX Ps. 120:2). Verse: I lifted up mine eyes unto the hills (LXX Ps. 120:1).*

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*

*Sticheron 1 (Cross):* Thy Cross, O Lord, hath become life and protection unto Thy people; trusting in it, we sing unto Thee, our crucified God: Have mercy on us!

*Sticheron 2 (Cross):* Sheltered by the Cross, we set ourselves against the enemy, not fearing his wiles and assaults; for his pride was abolished, and he was trampled down by the power of Christ crucified upon the Tree!

*Martyricon:* Thy Martyrs, O Lord, denied Thee not, nor departed from[^313] [p276LA] Thy commandments; by their prayers have mercy on us!

*Theotokion:* And no one that runs unto thee goes away from thee confounded, O most holy Mother of God; but whoever asks for grace receives gifts profitable unto his petition!

## FRIDAY MATINS: THE PRECIOUS CROSS AND THE THEOTOKOS

*On Friday at Matins, at "God is the Lord", Troparion, Tone 4:*

*Troparion (Cross):* Save, O Lord, Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance, granting victories unto our princes over the barbarians, and by Thy Cross preserving Thy people!

*Glory, both now, Stavrotheotokion:* Having acquired thy protection, O All-pure One, and being delivered from tribulations by thy prayers, preserved everywhere by the Cross of thy Son, as is due we all piously magnify thee!

*After the First Stichology, Sessional Hymns of the Cross, 3, Tone 6:*

*Sessional Hymn 1 (Cross):* Today the prophetic word is fulfilled; for behold, we worship in the place where Thy feet stood, O Lord; and having received the Tree of salvation, we obtained freedom from sinful death, by the prayers of the Mother of God, O only Lover of mankind!

*Sessional Hymn 2 (Cross):* Thy Cross, O Lord, was made radiant; for by it healings are bestowed upon them that revere Thee; therefore we fall down before Thee: Have mercy on us!

*Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Thee lifted up upon the Cross, O Christ, the all-pure Maiden who bore Thee was pierced through the soul with the sword of sorrow, and wept, sobbing maternally; by her prayers have mercy on us, O Lord!

*After the Second Stichology, Sessional Hymns, 3, Tone 6:*

*Sessional Hymn 1 (Cross):* Barely was the wood of Thy Cross set up, O Christ, and the foundation of death was shaken, O Lord; for whom hades had swallowed with longing, it released trembling, and revealed unto us His salvation, O Holy One; we glorify Thee,[^314] [p277LA] O Son of God, have mercy on us!

*Sessional Hymn 2 (Cross):* The Jews condemned Thee unto death, O Lord, the Life of all; they that crossed the Red Sea with the rod nailed Thee upon the Cross, and they that sucked honey from the rock brought Thee gall; but of Thine own will Thou didst endure, that Thou mightest free us from the slavery of the enemy: O Christ God, glory to Thee!

*Martyricon:* In the memory of Thy saints, O Lord, all creation keeps festival; the heavens with the Angels rejoice, and the earth makes glad with men; by their prayers have mercy on us!

*Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Thee hanged, O Christ, She that bore Thee cried aloud: What is this strange mystery which in mind I behold, O Son? How dost Thou die in the flesh upon the Tree, hanged, O Life of all?

*On Friday, Canon to the Cross and to the Mother of God, Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos:*

*Heirmos:* He Who of old covered the pursuing tyrant with the surge of the sea, the children of the saved buried beneath the earth; but let us, as the handmaidens, sing unto the Lord, for gloriously hath He been glorified!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* By Thy Passion Thou hast honored dishonored human nature; therefore with fear we honor Thee, and we magnify Thy dominion, for gloriously hath He been glorified!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* By Thy blood, O Christ, Thou hast stayed deceitful and unjust blood, and hast cleansed human nature from all malice; therefore, O Savior, we glorify Thy Sufferings, for gloriously hath He been glorified!

*Martyricon:* The blood-drinking beasts ye truly conquered, O all-praised Martyrs, and the threatening of the fire ye quenched by the dew of God's grace![^315]

[p278LA] *Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos, Tone 6. Ode 1. Heirmos: A helper and protector...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* The overshadowed mountain, the Prophet Habakkuk foresaw in the Spirit; praying, O pure one, visit me shaken by passions, and from the net of death and troubling evils deliver me!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* By the sprinkling of the divine blood poured forth from Thy Son, wash away my heart's wounds, that I may magnify thee, and as is due glorify thee, the ever-blessed and undefiled one!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* Equal in action to Him that begot Him, thou didst bear the Word Who deified human essence; pray unto Him for me who fall through lack of understanding and grow faint through the enemy's wiles, granting me divine comfort!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* He that of old fashioned our foremother from the rib, clothed Himself in flesh from thine all-pure womb, whereby He destroyed the might of death!

*Katavasia:* A helper and protector was He unto me for salvation: this is my God, and I will glorify Him; the God of my father, and I will exalt Him, for gloriously hath He been glorified!

*Ode 3. Heirmos:*

*Heirmos:* Thee, Who didst hang the whole earth unheld upon the waters, the creation beholding suspended upon Calvary was seized with terror, crying: There is none holy save Thee, O Lord!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Thee, Who didst hang the whole earth unheld upon the waters, raised upon the Tree and having Thy side pierced, the sun beholding was darkened, knowing Thee as enlightenment unto all the ends of the earth, O Lord!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* Thou wast pierced in the side, O Lord, and by nails[^316] [p279LA] transfixed, O Long-suffering One; and Adam, wounded of old, was healed by Thy being wounded; therefore we faithful found healing for our wounds!

*Martyricon:* When the angelic hosts beheld the Martyrs slain for the Lamb and being cut limb from limb, they marveled how, being in the body, they conquered the incorporeal foes; and the passion-bearers received the crowns of victory!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 3. Heirmos: By Thee, O Christ, the heavens are established...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Him Who is infinite in mercy and rich in bounties, God Who is merciful, O All-pure One, beseech to have mercy upon us afflicted!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Being the house of the Creator of the ages, make glad to pray in intercession unto the Comforter for him that was in the cave of soul-destroying passions, O pure Virgin!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* Bearing in thine arms, O Mother of God, Him that fed the people of God with manna, by His nod deliver me, who have nodded towards evil passions!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Beholding God incarnate of thee, O Mother of God, the angelic ranks marveled with fear; as Mother of God unceasingly we glorify thee!

*Katavasia:* By Thee, O Christ, all the heavens are established, O Word of God and Power: they declare Thine ineffable glory, the work of Thine all-creating hand; for there is none holy save Thee, O Lord!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:*

*Heirmos:* Foreseeing Thy divine dispensation upon the Cross, Habakkuk cried out in terror: Thou, O Good One, hast cut down the might of the strong, promising to hades as Almighty!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Having stretched out Thy hands, O Lord, the net of the enemy did not hold[^317] [p280LA] unto the tree of food; Thou didst stretch out Thy hands upon the Tree, and from Thy side Thou didst gush forth forgiveness unto all!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* Thou didst endure suffering, O Blessed One, and hast freed us from shameful passions; Thou didst taste gall, O sweetness of the Church, and hast given life unto the dead!

*Martyricon:* Having received the beloved divine fire in your rational soul, O wise ones, ye quenched the fire of godlessness; shining forth as Christ's archers, ye were shown forth radiant!

*Another Canon to the Mother of God. Ode 4. Heirmos: The Prophet having heard...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Beyond word thou didst bear the Word of the Father, Who looses the irrationality of men; entreat Him diligently, O only Virgin, to set free him that is enslaved to irrational passions!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Thou gushest forth healings unto us from thy palms, sanctifying the temples unto all men, shining and filling them with sweet-smelling myrrh, O all-pure Bride of God!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* We sing of thee, O all-pure and undefiled Virgin, for through thee we found salvation; singing, we cry aloud: Blessed art thou, who didst bear God in the flesh for us!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Thou didst bear for us the unwaning Light, O Virgin, shining in the flesh upon them that are in darkness, O pure one; singing to thee, [we cry]: Rejoice, O ever-virgin!

*Katavasia:* The Prophet, having heard of Thy coming, O Lord, was afraid that Thou shouldst be born of a Virgin and appear unto men, and he said: I have heard Thy report, and was afraid; glory to Thy power, O Lord!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:*

*Heirmos:* Thy manifestation, O Christ, which mercifully came to pass toward us, Isaiah beholding as the unwaning Light, rising early from the night [^318] [p281LA] [cried aloud]: The dead shall rise, and they that are in the tombs shall stand up, and all they that are upon the earth shall rejoice!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Dead didst Thou appear on Calvary, free among the dead wast Thou seen; and through Thy bodily Passion, Thou hadst neither form nor beauty seen upon the Tree, O Christ, desiring to adorn mankind, O Thou Who art abundantly merciful!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* To deliver first-fashioned Adam from condemnation, O Lord, Thou wast nailed to the Cross with nails, and pierced in the side with a spear: Thou didst forbid the flaming weapon to bar the gates of Eden!

*Martyricon:* Following the Passion of the Master, the choirs of martyrs, being nailed upon the tree, lift up their hands together; and their feet are stretched out for torments, guided along divine paths!

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Ode 5. Heirmos: Rising early from the night...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Shining brightly in purity, thou becamest the receptacle of God, O all-hymned one; for thou alone wast shown forth unto the Merciful One, bearing Him in thine arms as an Infant!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Thou bearest noetic beauty, all-comely in soul; thou becamest the Bride of God, sealed with pure virginity, and enlightened with divine light!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* Transform my bodily sicknesses and the diseases of my soul, and drive away the clouds of sloth, O Virgin, robe of Light, granting health and healing unto me who sing, who faithfully glorify thee!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Enlighten my darkened mind and soul, O Virgin, and make me a partaker of divine understanding; for behold, I am filled with evils, and have become enslaved unto passions of every kind[^319] [p282LA] and bring unto thee an undefiled bride!

*Katavasia:* Rising early from the night, enlighten us, we pray, and guide us in Thy commandments, and teach us, O Savior, to do Thy will!

*Ode 6. Heirmos:*

*Heirmos:* Jonah was seized, but not held fast in the belly of the whale; bearing the image of Thee Who didst suffer and wast given over to burial, he came forth from the monster as from a bridal chamber, and cried out unto the watch: Ye that observe vanity and lies have forsaken your own mercy!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Stretching out Thy hands of old in the image of the Cross, as on a pillar the Most High appeared unto us, delivering mankind from the ancient curse; Thou didst pour forth life and incorruption: Thee, O Savior, the only Creator, we glorify!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* Great Moses prefigured Thy Passion, lifting up the brazen serpent of old; and they that looked upon the sign of the venomous foe [bitten] by wounding gnawings, because the Master was crucified voluntarily, Thou by Thy goodness hast delivered us from the wounded harm!

*Martyricon:* Sheltered by the Cross, O archers, ye pulled down the deceitful walls, and removed unto the city on high, adorned with a victorious crown; therefore we call you blessed!

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Ode 6. Heirmos: The lowest abyss...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Great Moses among the Prophets prefigured thee: ark and table, lampstand and vessel of manna, symbolically revealing the incarnation of the Most High Who is of thee, O Virgin Mother!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* The law grew weak and the shadow passed away[^320] [p283LA] beyond mind and thought; the revealed grace prefigured thy childbearing, O Virgin, of God the Savior, O all-hymned Bride!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* I surpassed the proud, exalted mind of the Pharisee, ever rushing into the valleys of countless falls; do thou alone, O pure one, have compassion on me!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* O marvelous conception and maidenly birth-giving, show forth thy mercy unto me in a strange land, conceived in iniquities and born into slavery to passions!

*Katavasia:* The lowest abyss of sins hath surrounded me, and unable to endure its billows, like Jonah I cry aloud unto the Master: Lead me up from corruption!

*Kontakion of the Cross, Tone 4.*

*Podoben: "Thou Who wast lifted up upon the Cross" (Вознесы́йся на Крⷭ҇тъ во́лею):*

*Ode 7. Heirmos:*

*Heirmos:* An ineffable wonder: He that delivered the holy children from the flame in the furnace, is laid breathless in a new tomb, for the salvation of us who sing: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* When the Hebrew council condemned Thee upon the Cross, the earth, beholding Thee, was terrified; the sun hid its light, and the saints in darkness, seeing it, sang: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* Enduring reviling, O Savior, beaten with a reed and crowned with thorns like a king of Thine own will, O Christ God[^321] [p284LA] for the salvation of us who sing to Thee: Blessed art Thou, O our Deliverer!

*Martyricon:* Binding all day long the malice [of the tyrants], O all-lauded ones, ye endured rivers of wounds and unjust deaths, desiring the life on high, singing: Blessed art Thou, O God our Deliverer!

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Ode 7. Heirmos: Destructive darkness...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* Thou wast shown forth as the resting place of the divine nature, thou alone blessed one; for God Who sits upon the shoulders of the Cherubim, thou didst bear in thine arms, crying: Blessed is the God of our fathers!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* The judgment is at the doors, ready for the judge; be ready, O my wretched soul! Cry aloud when He judges: Condemn me not, for the sake of her that bare Thee!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* Encompassed by the fruits of sin, I have been deadened; bringing a barren soul, I cry unto thee: Show me fruitful, who have destroyed the fruit of my prayer!

*Katavasia:* Having heard the song of the holy children in the fiery furnace, Thou didst cool the flame, O Savior: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:*

*Heirmos:* Be astonished and fear, O heaven, and let the foundations of the earth be shaken! For behold, He Who dwells in the highest is numbered among the dead, and takes up His abode in a small tomb: ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; and supremely exalt Him throughout all ages!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Thou drinkest the cup, O Christ, which in gall [Thou wast offered] in the Passion on the Cross; Thou pourest forth forgiveness from Thy life-bearing side unto those deadened through the rib [of Adam], crying and singing: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; and supremely exalt Him throughout all ages!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* When the lawless people condemned Thee unto crucifixion in the midst of the tribunal, O Jesus, then the earth was shaken, and all creation cried aloud with fear: Ye children, bless; ye priests, sing; and supremely exalt Him[^322] [p285LA] throughout all ages!

*Martyricon:* Precious before the Lord was your death shown forth, O martyrs of Christ; partakers of many sufferings were ye, and partakers of the passionless counsel were ye shown to be: pray for us who faithfully sing of you!

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Ode 8. Heirmos: Him Whom the heavenly hosts glorify...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* By the union of the Word with the clay of man, a divine resting place wast thou shown forth, O all-pure one, making radiant the brightness of the Godhead unto me, Christ Whom we supremely exalt unto the ages!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* The golden-beaming lampstand prefigured thee; having received the ineffable, unapproachable Light, with light thou dost illuminate all things, Christ Whom we supremely exalt unto the ages!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* Being full of every good, and near unto the King, O Mother of God, fill me with good deeds, who have lived with malice and corrupted my whole life with sloth, that I may glorify thee unto the ages!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* Thou wast found clothed in a comely robe through bearing God, O Mother of God; but I, working malice, am stripped of it; but do thou thyself enlighten me with divine vestments through repentance, by thine intercessions, O Virgin!
