*Katavasia:* We praise, we bless, and we worship the Lord. Him Whom the heavenly hosts glorify, and before Whom the Cherubim and Seraphim tremble, let every breath and all creation sing, bless, and supremely exalt Him throughout all ages!

*Ode 9. Heirmos: Weep not for Me, O Mother, beholding in the tomb...*[^323] [p286LA] the Son Whom thou didst conceive in the flesh without seed; for I shall arise and be glorified, and as God I shall unceasingly exalt in glory them that magnify thee with faith and love!

*Troparion 1 (Cross):* Through corruptible food and ancient pleasure, O only Savior, as Good One and Deliverer Thou didst heal us: Thou wast crucified voluntarily and didst deign to be dead; unceasingly we sing, O Christ, of Thy great compassion!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* How didst Thou endure pains upon the Cross when Thou wast crucified, and hast delivered us from grievous pains, O Christ! How wast Thou crowned with thorns, rooting out all the thorns of passions! Having tasted vinegar and gall, Thou hast filled for us the cup of sweetness!

*Martyricon:* Being bound, O holy ones, and cast into prison, and cruelly dismembered limb by limb, bound unto wheels, and given as food unto wild beasts, ye did not deny Christ, the Master of all!

*Another Canon to the Most Holy Theotokos. Ode 9. Heirmos: He that [bore] joy...*

*Troparion 1 (Theotokos):* The Star shining with rays of the Godhead out of Jacob shone forth upon them that were held in darkness, through thee now, O all-pure one: Christ God, the incarnate Word, by Whom being enlightened, with songs we unceasingly magnify thee!

*Troparion 2 (Theotokos):* Strengthened by thy power and goodness, unto thee we send up song without ceasing; do thou accept this, O pure one, repaying with thy radiant grace from incorruptible treasures, O full of the grace of God!

*Troparion 3 (Theotokos):* Corrupted by fornication, and corrupted in soul, a bride defiled by malice, and stripped of all good things, shown forth, O incorrupt Virgin: O undefiled one, despise me not, but adorn me[^324] [p287LA] with good deeds!

*Troparion 4 (Theotokos):* May I be delivered by thine intercessions, O undefiled Virgin, from every wrath, and death-bearing passions, and the cruel race of fire, and from unrighteous men, and from cruel enemies: fleeing under thy shelter, I call upon thee for help!

*Katavasia: That which was from Jacob... More honorable than the Cherubim.*

*Exapostilarion of the Cross.*

*Podoben: "Give ear, ye women" (Же́ны оу҆слы́шите):*

*Stavrotheotokion:* Beholding Thee nailed upon the Cross, the All-pure One, weeping and lamenting, cried aloud: How hast Thou set, O Sun of glory, O primordial Light? How art Thou nailed upon the Cross, and pierced in the side with a spear, O my Savior and Son, for the sake of the salvation of mankind!

*At the Aposticha, Stichera of the Cross, Tone 6:*

*Tone 6. Aposticha Sticheron 1 (Cross):* Having hope in the Cross, O Christ, and boasting therein, I cry aloud unto Thee, O Lord Lover of mankind: Cast down the pride of the adversaries, who confess Thee not as God and Man!

*Sticheron 2 (Cross):* Raising up Adam by the Cross, O Christ, Thou hast delivered us from corruption; and crushing the power of the enemy, Thou didst put to death the dominion of death, and hast exalted the Christian faith, as Thou art merciful!

*Martyricon:* Having suffered for Thy sake, O Christ, and endured many torments, by their prayers, O Lord, and by their supplications, preserve us all!

*Theotokion:* O joyfully favored Virgin Mother of Christ God, Who was born of thee for our sake, unceasingly beseech Him[^325] [p288LA] as our God to send down His bounties upon the world!

*On the Hours, the Beatitudes, Tone 6:*

*Tone 6. Beatitudes, Troparion 1:* Remember me, O God my Savior, when Thou comest in Thy kingdom, and save me, O only Lover of mankind!

*Troparion 2 (Cross):* Stretching out Thy hands upon the Cross, Thou didst call the nations that sing of Thee, O compassionate Christ!

*Troparion 3 (Cross):* Thou wast nailed to the Cross of Thine own will, and by Thy power Thou didst utterly destroy the sting of the tormentor, O Christ!

*Martyricon:* Nourished by torments, O passion-bearers, ye were accounted worthy of heavenly food: pray unceasingly for the world!

*Trinity:* Glorifying the Father and the Son, and proclaiming the Holy Spirit, [we cry]: O Holy Trinity, save our souls!

*Theotokion:* Beholding Thee crucified upon the Cross, the Virgin sighed and wept; by her intercessions, save us, O Lord!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 6: Save, O Lord, Thy people, and bless Thine inheritance (LXX Ps. 27:9). Verse: Unto Thee, O Lord, will I cry, O my God, be not silent unto me (LXX Ps. 27:1).*

*Epistle to the Corinthians (1 Cor. 1:18–24):*

Brethren: The preaching of the Cross is to them that perish foolishness; but unto us which are saved it is the power of God. For it is written: I will destroy the wisdom of the wise, and will bring to nothing the understanding of the prudent. Where is the wise? Where is the scribe? Where is the disputer of this world? Hath not God made foolish the wisdom of this world? For after that in the wisdom of God the world by wisdom knew not God, it pleased God by the foolishness of preaching to save them that believe. For the Jews require a sign, and the Greeks seek after wisdom: but we preach Christ crucified, unto the Jews a stumblingblock, and unto the Greeks foolishness; but unto them which are called, both Jews and Greeks, Christ the power of God, and the wisdom of God.[^326]

[p289LA] *Alleluia: Remember Thy congregation, which Thou hast purchased from the beginning (LXX Ps. 73:2). Verse: Unto Thee, O Lord, have I lifted up my soul (LXX Ps. 24:1).*

*Gospel according to John (John 3:13–17):*

The Lord said: No man hath ascended up to heaven, but He that came down from heaven, even the Son of Man which is in heaven. And as Moses lifted up the serpent in the wilderness, even so must the Son of Man be lifted up: that whosoever believes in Him should not perish, but have eternal life. For God so loved the world, that He gave His only-begotten Son, that whosoever believes in Him should not perish, but have everlasting life. For God sent not His Son into the world to condemn the world; but that the world through Him might be saved.

*Kinonikon: The light of Thy countenance hath been signed upon us, O Lord (LXX Ps. 4:7).*

## FRIDAY EVENING VESPERS (FOR SATURDAY)

*On Friday evening, at "Lord, I have cried", Stichera of the Prophets and Martyrs, Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѡ пресла́внагѡ чꙋдесѐ):*

*Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "O most marvelous wonder" (Ѡ пресла́внагѡ чꙋдесѐ):*

*Sticheron 2:* Despise me not, wretched one, lying in passions and perishing in temptations; but do ye all arise: O Prophets, pray for us; entreat Him, O Hierarchs and Monastics; and ye Martyrs and Passion-bearers of Christ, together with the Queen and the Forerunner, diligently entreat Christ to deliver us from condemnation on the day of judgment!

*Sticheron 3, Podoben: "With what crowns of praise" (Кыими похвальными вѣнцы):* With what crowns of praise[^327] [p290LA] can we weave garlands for you, O all-lauded Prophets, with the honorable Hierarchs and Venerable Fathers, whom the glory of the Comforter from on high hath ineffably crowned! Therefore pour forth your all-pure prayers, asking remission of countless evils!

*Other Stichera to the Monastics and Hierarchs, of the same Tone:*

*Tone 8. Sticheron 4 (Prophets and Apostles):* The Prophets obedient, and partakers of grace together with the twelve Apostles, who pour forth water from Eden like life-bearing rivers, watering the face of the earth with life-flowing waters, through divine streams of great elements, who in faith established creation: let them worthily be honored!

*Sticheron 5 (Monastics and Teachers):* Having ascended unto the height of Christ's love, ye beheld the ineffable mysteries of His divine glory, which ye revealed and explained unto men as radiant preachers of grace; therefore pray that they may be delivered from corruption and misfortunes, who in faith honor your teachings, O venerable ones!

*Sticheron 6 (Fathers of the Councils):* Ye loosed the bonds, O venerable ones, and cut down the heretical snares by the wise command of your words, and gathered the Orthodox faith into one mind; who glorify Christ in the knowledge of God, O venerable fathers, entreat Him to deliver from corruption and misfortunes those who with faith accept your divinely inspired traditions!

*Glory, and now, of the oncoming Tone, whichever it be on that day, and that is the greater Doxasticon:*

*Tone 8.*

*Podoben: "Martyrs of the Lord" (Мч҃ницы Гдⷭ҇ни):* [^328]

[p291LA] ...and every infirmity ye heal; and now pray that our souls may be delivered from the snare of the enemy, by your prayers!

*Sticheron:* Martyrs of the Lord, pray unto our God, and ask for our souls a multitude of compassions and cleansing from our many stumbling-blocks, we pray!

*Sticheron:* Thy Martyrs, O Lord, having forgotten the things of this life and disregarded torments for the sake of the life to come, were shown to be inheritors thereof, and rejoice with the angels: by their prayers grant unto Thy people great mercy!

*Tone 8 (Doxasticon of the Departed by St. John of Damascus):* I weep and I wail when I contemplate death, and behold our beauty, fashioned according to the image of God, lying in the tombs, disfigured, inglorious, having neither form nor comeliness. O wonder! What is this mystery concerning us? How have we been surrendered unto corruption? How have we been yoked unto death? Truly by the command of God, as it is written, Who grants rest unto the departed.[^329]

*Glory, and now. Theotokion:* Thy protection, O Theotokos, is a spiritual infirmary, wherein all we who flee [unto it] are delivered from spiritual infirmities.

*At "God is the Lord", Troparion to the Prophets and Apostles, Tone 2:* Apostles, Martyrs, Prophets, Hierarchs, Venerable and Righteous Ones, who have fought the good fight and kept the faith, having boldness before the Savior: entreat Him on our behalf, as He is good, that our souls may be saved, we pray!

*Another Troparion, of the same Tone:* Remember, O Lord, as Thou art good, Thy servants, and forgive whatsoe'er they have sinned in life: for none is without [p292LA] sin, save Thee alone, Who art able to grant rest unto the departed.

*Theotokion:* Holy Mother of the Ineffable Light, honoring thee with angelic hymns, we magnify thee!

*At the 1st Stichologia, of the same Tone 8. Sessional Hymn (Martyrs):* Spiritual luminaries ye were shown to be, holy martyrs; the deceitful darkness ye abolished by faith, having illumined your spiritual lamps by faith; with the Bridegroom in glory ye entered into the heavenly bridal chamber: and now pray, we beseech you, that our souls may be saved!

*Sessional Hymn (Martyrs):* Unto the narrow and sorrowful path, unswerving travelers ye were shown to be, holy martyrs; afflictions and sicknesses ye endured, the life of the bodiless ones ye received, stones of endurance and godly foundations of the faith ye were shown to be: entreat Christ God, that our souls may be saved!

*Theotokion:* The heavenly hosts praise thee, O full of grace, unwedded Mother, and we glorify thine ineffable childbearing: O Theotokos, pray for the salvation of our souls!

*At the 2nd Stichologia, of the same Tone. Sessional Hymn (Martyrs):* What shall we call you, O saints? Cherubim, for upon you Christ rested? Seraphim, for truly ye were glorified? Angels, for ye turned away from the body? Powers, for ye work miracles? Many are your names, yet greater is your grace: pray for our souls!

*Sessional Hymn (Martyrs):* Greatly did ye contend, O saints, and wounds from the lawless ye manfully endured; with boldness ye confessed Christ before the tormentors; therefore having departed from this life, ye work wonders and miracles throughout all the world, healing the infirm: by your[^330] [p293LA] prayers, O holy ones, pray for the salvation of our souls!

*Kontakion of the Martyrs, Tone 8:* As the first-fruits of nature unto the Planter of creation, the universe offers unto Thee, O Lord, the invincible martyrs: by their prayers preserve Thy Church in peace, and Thine inheritance, for the sake of the Theotokos, O Many-merciful One!

*Tone 8, Podoben: "Having secretly received the command" (Повеленное тайно):* Having received the secret command into his mind, in the chamber of Joseph the Bodiless one swiftly came, saying unto her that knew not wedlock: He that bowed the heavens by His descent is held entirely and without change within thee! Beholding Him in thy womb having received the form of a servant, I cry unto thee in awe: Rejoice, unwedded Bride!

*On Saturday, Canon to the Prophets, Apostles, Martyrs, and Venerable Ones, Tone 8. Ode 1. Heirmos:* Having passed through the water as upon dry land, and having escaped from the Egyptian malice, the Israelite cried: Let us sing unto our Redeemer and God!

*Troparion (Prophets):* The Prophets foretold the fearful dispensation of the Word of God unto men; and by their intercession illumine us, [O Lord], with the light of Thy love for mankind!

*Troparion (Martyrs):* Plunged in cruel passions, darkened in the abyss of deceit, O crown-bearing Martyrs of the Lord: guide my soul unto salvation!

*Theotokion:* In thee all flesh trusts, O joy of the sorrowing: therefore now we ever pray unto thee, as our haven and our wall: may those who glorify thee be filled with thy gifts!

*Another Canon for the departed, of the same Tone, the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion (Martyrs):* Having died in Christ, and through sufferings having conquered passions, all ye venerable martyrs received divine and blessed[^331] [p294LA] life.

*Troparion (Departed):* Overlooking the transgressions of youth and lawless deeds, O Christ the Savior, do Thou sustain Thy servants, and count them among Thine elect!

*Troparion (Departed):* They who have attained glory and joy, possessing a blessed life: grant [unto them], O bountiful One, those things which they have received, O Many-merciful One!

*Theotokion:* Thou hast conceived the Word of God in the flesh, united hypostatically from thee, O immaculate one, Who by His divine power hath abolished hades.

*Ode 3. Heirmos:* O Lord, Fashioner of the vault of heaven, and Builder of the Church: do Thou establish me in Thy love, O summit of desires, confirmation of the faithful, only Lover of mankind!

*Troparion (Prophets and Martyrs):* The Prophets proclaimed, the Martyrs confessed: by their prayers deliver me from cruel evils!

*Troparion:* Having fallen into the sinful depth, by the prayers of all the saints, O Christ, save me!

*Theotokion:* Might of the faithful and unfailing hope, O all-pure one: grant good season unto those who truly honor thee!

*Another Canon for the departed, the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion (Martyrs):* Having cleansed the ancient forefatherly fall by baptism and renewal, and having sprinkled yourselves with streams of blood, ye reign with Christ, O blessed ones!

*Troparion (Departed):* He Who as a corpse was willingly laid in the tomb, and Who calls back those dwelling in hades: O Savior, deign that those whom Thou hast received from us may dwell in the mansions of Thy holy hosts!

*Troparion (Departed):* By Thy divine compassions, O Master, and naturally understanding Thy goodness, be Thou entreated, granting remission of sins[^332] [p295LA] unto Thy servants, and give them rest with Thyself.

*Theotokion:* Having become man in the flesh without alteration, the Son, the only Lover of mankind, God, saves man from the gates of death, O all-pure Mother of God, only immaculate one!

*Ode 4. Heirmos:* Thou art my Lord, O Christ, Thou art my power, Thou art my God, Thou art my rejoicing; leaving not the bosom of the Father, and visiting the poverty of our wretchedness: therefore with the Prophet Habakkuk I cry: Glory to Thy power!

*Troparion (Prophets):* Fulfil, O Lord, the things of God proclaimed by those who prophesied; and by their prayers fill my heart with divine understanding!

*Troparion (Prophets and Martyrs):* O Prophets and Martyrs, upon whom the Lord rests: by their prayers, as Thou art merciful, illumine my wretched soul, O Lord!

*Theotokion:* By thy power, O all-pure one, may we be delivered from every tumult, wrath, and misfortune; and may we who honor thee with faith ever find delight in thy good things!

*Another Canon for the departed, the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion (Martyrs):* That they may behold Thy glory, O Master, and Thy light-giving radiance, and brilliantly receive it in heaven, the divine Martyrs endured every kind of torment and agony, singing unto Thee, O Christ: Glory to Thy power, O Lover of mankind!

*Troparion (Departed):* Many are the mansions with Thee, O Savior, distributed unto all according to their recompense, and according to the measure of virtue: therefore, O compassionate One, fill them with power, and account worthy thereof those who have reposed in faith, who piously sing unto Thee: Glory to Thy power!

*Troparion (Departed):* Having appeared equal to us men, O mortal One, having endured an equal[^333] [p296LA] death for all, and having shown the way unto life: account worthy thereof those who have reposed, as Thou art merciful, and as Master grant them remission and communion of light!

*Theotokion:* Thou art the praise of the faithful, O unwedded Mother; thou art the protection and refuge of Christians, a wall and a haven: for unto thy Son thou bearest prayer, O immaculate one, and from perils thou deliverest those who with faith and love acknowledge thee as the pure Mother of God!

*Ode 5. Heirmos:* Why hast Thou cast me off from Thy face, O Light unwaning, and alien darkness hath covered me, wretched one? But turn me back, and unto the light of Thy commandments direct my paths, I pray Thee!

*Troparion (Prophets and Martyrs):* The night of sin hath covered me; but illumine me with light, O Lover of mankind, by the prayers of Thy Prophets and Martyrs!

*Troparion (Prophets and Martyrs):* Together with the Martyrs and Prophets, pray ye with divinely inspired cries: deliver us from temptations and eternal torment!

*Theotokion:* We ever sing of thee as our haven and our wall, O Sovereign Lady who knewest not wedlock: ask at all times that our hearts may be gladdened!

*Another Canon for the departed, the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion (Departed):* Unto Thine ineffable glory, and beyond words of Thy blessedness, under the tabernacle of the righteous, where the voice of those who feast is joyful: having shown compassion, account those added unto passionless life worthy thereof, O only Lover of mankind!

*Troparion (Departed):* Where the angelic ranks are, where the choirs of the righteous rejoice: in the bosom of Abraham settle Thy servants, O merciful One, with boldness before the most fearful[^334] [p297LA] throne of God to stand, do Thou deign, O merciful One!

*Troparion (Departed):* Cleansing appeared unto us, and righteousness and redemption: by Thy wound, O Christ, Thou hast healed our sicknesses; therefore, O compassionate One, as Thou art good, account the departed worthy of delight in Paradise!

*Theotokion:* Mercifully, O compassionate One, Thou didst assume the human measure, while majestically adorning the divine glory in the heights; from the virginal womb Thou didst receive animate and rational flesh, through which death was overthrown.

*Ode 6. Heirmos:* Cleanse me, O Savior, for many are mine iniquities; and lead me up from the depth of evils, I pray; for unto Thee have I cried, and hear me, O God of my salvation!

*Troparion (Prophets and Martyrs):* O Prophets with the holy Martyrs, having slain the deceitful serpent by death, ye inherited eternal life: by their prayers, as Thou art good, quicken my deadened soul!

*Troparion:* Turn back, O my soul! How long abidest thou in evil habits? Accept good repentance, falling down and crying with tears: O holy Prophets with the Martyrs, by your prayers raise me up unto the path of repentance!

*Theotokion:* Higher than the Seraphim art thou, O all-pure one, as Queen and Lady: cease not to pray unto thy Savior on behalf of thy people and city!

*Another Canon for the departed, the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion (Martyrs):* Thy Martyrs, O Savior, endured many torments, having their souls pierced with Thy love, O holy ones, desiring Thine ever-abiding glory and communion with Thy sweetness.

*Troparion (Departed):* The belly of the enemy, by Thy death[^335] [p298LA] O Savior, Thou hast torn asunder, and all the prisoners therein Thou hast raised up, granting life: account those who have reposed worthy thereof, O Benefactor!

*Glory. Troparion (Departed):* From tears and sighing which are in hades free Thy servants, O Savior: for as the only Compassionate One, Thou hast wiped away every tear from every face of those who faithfully glorify Thee!

*Both now. Theotokion:* He Who by nature is the Creator is formed in thy womb, and He Who is complete as God empties Himself, O all-immaculate one: the only Immortal One, the Covenant of salvation, dies by death.

*Kontakion of the Departed, Tone 8:* With the saints give rest, O Christ, unto the soul of Thy servant, where there is neither sickness, nor sorrow, nor sighing, but life everlasting!

*Ode 7. Heirmos:* The Hebrew children in the furnace, having trampled upon the flame, and having turned the fire into dew, cried out: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Troparion (Apostles, Prophets, and Martyrs):* Glorious Apostles, Prophets with the Martyrs, having become all-honored spiritual vessels: deliver us from the snare of the evil one, ever making fervent prayers on our behalf unto Christ our God!

*Troparion (Martyrs):* With the streaming of your blood ye extinguished the deceitful flame, O Martyrs: by their prayers, O Christ, quench the flame of my soul and the unceasing burning fire!

*Theotokion:* O all-immaculate Sovereign Lady, pray with His saints unto Him Who made all things by His nod, singing unto the Savior: Blessed art Thou, O God of our fathers!

*Another Canon for the departed, the same Heirmos:*[^336] [p299LA] *Troparion (Martyrs):* All the love of the Martyrs was extended unto the one Master, joining themselves unto Him in love, singing: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Troparion (Departed):* Thou givest unto those who have reposed in faith the brightness of the divine Kingdom, granting the garment of incorruption unto those who cry: Blessed art Thou, O Lord God of our fathers!

*Troparion (Departed):* Fill with joy and gladness those whom Thou hast translated, Thy servants, O compassionate One, and account them worthy to cry and sing: Blessed is God!

*Theotokion:* Loosing the curse of Eve, He dwelt in the undefiled Maiden, pouring forth a fountain of sanctification unto those who sing: Blessed is the God of our fathers!

*Ode 8. Heirmos:* The King of heaven, Whom the hosts of angels praise: praise and supreme exalt unto all ages!

*Troparion (Apostles, Prophets, and Martyrs):* Guardians of the faith and protectors, ministers of the Word, Apostles, Prophets with the Martyrs: save and preserve, praying unto Christ, all Orthodox who harmoniously sing: Praise, sing, and supreme exalt Him!

*Troparion:* Why came I forth from the womb of my mother, and was born into passion? How shall I not perish, being about to suffer bitterly in eternal fire? Woe is me! Now there is none to weep for me, and none to bear me up! O holy Prophets with the Apostles and Martyrs, praise Christ and supreme exalt Him forever!

*Theotokion:* Most holy Virgin, praise of the Apostles and glory of the Martyrs: make me a partaker of eternal glory, as I sing and supreme exalt!

*Another Canon [of the departed], the same Heirmos:*

*Troparion (Martyrs):* Having passed through earthly passions, the true martyrs received heavenly crowns, crying unto Thee unceasingly: Bless [the Lord], ye children!

*Troparion (Departed):* Having descended into the nethermost pit[^337] [p300LA] and by Thy life-originating right hand having raised up those dwelling in the tombs, do Thou grant rest unto Thy servants who have reposed in faith, O Many-merciful One!

*Troparion (Departed):* As Thou art the fountain of everlasting life, and a torrent of delight: account worthy, O Savior, Thy servants who have piously passed over unto Thee, to sing: Bless [the Lord], ye children!

*Theotokion:* O Virgin Mary, Mother of God, who in the flesh barest God and the Savior of men: save those who faithfully sing of thy childbearing!

*Katavasia (Ode 8):* When the musical instruments sounded together, and countless peoples worshipped the image on the plain of Dura, the three children regarded not the godless decree, but cast into the midst of the flame, they sang: Bless [the Lord], ye children; praise Him, ye priests!

*Ode 9. Heirmos:* Every hearing was struck with awe at the ineffable condescension of God: that the Most High willingly came down even unto the flesh, having become Man from the virginal womb. Therefore, O all-pure Theotokos, we believers magnify thee!

*Troparion (Prophets and Martyrs):* Trumpets of the divine Spirit ye were shown to be, O divinely speaking Prophets, sounding forth! Wounded by the abomination of a darkened heart, by carnal passions and lusts, O passion-bearing Martyrs: heal my soul by your prayers, that I may magnify [the Lord]!

*Troparion:* Sigh and weep, O my soul, before the end overtaks thee! Prepare repentance unto the Master, crying and saying: By Thy compassions, O Lord, grant me quietude, as Thou art good and the Lover of mankind!

*Theotokion:* Look down into the abyss, and see my soul surrendered to deceit[^338] [p301LA] to be tormented; but bowing thy knees, shed tears for her, that He Who gave His Blood as a ransom may be inclined by thy prayers, and lead me forth from thence!

*Another Heirmos, the same:*

*Troparion (Martyrs):* Having unconquerable strength and invincible might, O Martyrs of Christ, ye reproached the godless decree of the tormentors, and were shown heirs of the heavenly Kingdom, being illuminated by the rays of the Trinity, O right-marvelous ones!

*Troparion (Departed):* O Thou Who hast destroyed bitter death, when hades was overthrown, O Lover of mankind, and Who hast raised up those who have reposed from the ages: account those who have passed over unto Thee, O Merciful One, worthy of Thine unwaning light!

*Troparion (Departed):* Thou art all sweetness, Thou art all joy and insatiable gladness, Thou art ineffable beauty: deign that those translated unto Thee may delight in Thy beauty, and account them worthy of divine goodness!

*Theotokion:* O Tabernacle of the mercy of God, who didst bear Christ my Savior, both God and Man, in two natures and not in two persons, the Only-begotten of the Father; and we magnify thee, who art more radiant than all creation, who in virginity barest Him!

*Then: It is truly meet, and More honorable. Sessional Hymn (Exapostilarion).*

*Podoben: "Give ear, ye women" (Же́ны оу҆слы́шите):*

*Troparion (All Saints):* Glorious Prophets, sovereign Apostles, righteous Patriarchs, solitary company of Hierarchs, venerable ones, and choir of righteous women, holy Martyrs: with them all, pray unto the Lord with the Mother of God, to have mercy upon Thy servants!

*Troparion (Departed):* O Thou Who rulest over the dead and the living as God, give rest unto Thy servants in the habitations of Thine elect...[^339]

[p302LA] *Theotokion:* The heavenly hosts sing of thee, O joyful Maiden and Unwedded Mother, and we glorify thine unsearchable childbearing: O Theotokos, pray for the salvation of our souls!

*On Saturday at the Liturgy, the Beatitudes, Tone 8:*

*Refrain:* Remember us, O Christ, Savior of the world, as Thou rememberedst the thief upon the tree, and account us worthy of Thy heavenly Kingdom!

*Troparion (Martyrs):* Firmly touching upon every pain, by your wounds, O glorious passion-bearers, ye drove away the entire multitude of demons by your divine grace!

*Troparion (Prophets):* Having clearly cleansed yourselves beforehand spiritually, with your eyes, O Prophets, ye foresaw the divine descent upon earth: pray unto Him, as He is good, on behalf of the afflicted soul!

*Troparion (Departed):* All the faithful who have passed over from life, do Thou establish, O Blessed One, in the land of the righteous, and show them to be partakers of Paradise, divinely glorifying Thee!

*Trinity (Troparion):* O unoriginate Trinity, indivisible in essence, unconfused in unity, undivided in glory, primordial nature and kingdom: save us who believe in Thee!

*Theotokion:* Thee truly, O Virgin Theotokos, all we the faithful harmoniously hymn as our firm defender, mediatress, and salvation of our souls!

*Prokeimenon, Tone 8:* Be glad in the Lord, and rejoice, ye righteous! [LXX Ps. 31:11] *Verse:* Blessed are they whose transgressions are forgiven. [LXX Ps. 31:1]

*Epistle of the Holy Apostle Paul to the Thessalonians (Departed) [1 Thess. 4:13–17]:* Brethren, I would not have you to be ignorant concerning those who are fallen asleep, that ye sorrow not, even as others who have no hope; for if[^340] [p303LA] we believe that Jesus died and rose again, even so those also who sleep in Jesus will God bring with Him. For this we say unto you by the word of the Lord, that we who are alive and remain unto the coming of the Lord shall not prevent those who are asleep. For the Lord Himself shall descend from heaven with a shout, with the voice of the archangel, and with the trumpet of God; and the dead in Christ shall rise first. Then we who are alive and remain shall be caught up together with them in the clouds to meet the Lord in the air; and so shall we ever be with the Lord.

*Alleluia, Tone 8:* Blessed is he whom Thou hast chosen and taken unto Thyself, O Lord! [LXX Ps. 64:5] *Verse:* His soul shall dwell among good things. [LXX Ps. 24:13]

*Gospel according to Matthew (Departed) [John 5:24–30]:* The Lord said unto the Jews who came unto Him: Truly, truly, I say unto you: He that hears My word, and believes on Him that sent Me, has everlasting life, and shall not come into judgment, but is passed from death unto life. Truly, truly, I say unto you: The hour comes, and now is, when the dead shall hear the voice of the Son of God; and they that hear shall live. For as the Father has life in Himself, so has He given to the Son to have life in Himself; and has given Him authority to execute judgment also, because He is the Son of Man. Marvel not at this; for the hour comes, in which all that are in the graves shall hear the voice of the Son of God, and shall come forth: they that have done good, unto the resurrection of life[^341] [p304LA] and they that have done evil, unto the resurrection of judgment. I can of Mine own self do nothing: as I hear, I judge; and My judgment is just, because I seek not Mine own will, but the will of the Father Who sent Me.

*Kinonikon:* Rejoice in the Lord, O ye righteous! [LXX Ps. 32:1]
